考研英语精读 · No.01

The Robber Who Thought Lemon Juice Made Him Invisible

以为柠檬汁能隐身的劫匪
On 6 January 1995,1995年1月6日，McArthur Wheeler麦克阿瑟·惠勒and和an一名accomplice同伙
raided two Pittsburgh-area banks at gunpoint持枪抢劫了匹兹堡地区的两家银行，
in broad daylight,光天化日之下，without不加disguise.乔装。
Their他们only唯一的camouflage伪装was是lemon juice,柠檬汁，
rubbed on涂抹在身上，in the怀着earnest真心实意的conviction信念，
（另译：对此深信不疑）
that认为it它would能render使them他们invisible to cameras.在镜头前隐形。
（render：使……处于某种状态）
The这套rationale逻辑had倒也有a kernel of truth.几分真实的内核。
Lemon juice柠檬汁is a是一种time-honoured由来已久的invisible隐形ink,墨水，
（用柠檬汁在纸上写字，干后看不见，加热才显出字迹）
and Wheeler惠勒则made an做了一次inventive富有创意的leap跳跃，from paper从纸面to skin.跳到了皮肤上。
His他的Polaroid宝丽来self-portrait自拍照came out洗出来后，devoid of完全没有its照片的subject,拍摄对象（他本人），
a result这个结果，he他took as当成了conclusive.确凿的证据。
（他用这个相机拍了一张自拍，实际上没拍到，但他以为是他自己涂了柠檬水，隐身了。）
The security cameras监控摄像头begged to differ.可不敢苟同。
On 19 April4月19日，the eleven o'clock news11点新闻
showed viewers向观众播出了the那些damning足以定罪的images,画面，
and Wheeler was arrested shortly after midnight.午夜刚过不久，惠勒就被捕了。
Confronted with面对the tapes,这些录像，
he他protested坚决表示in sheer完全disbelief不相信，that说he had worn自己明明抹了the juice.柠檬汁。
Reading about读到the case,这个案子时，
the Cornell psychologist康奈尔大学心理学家David Dunning戴维·邓宁pondered仔细思考了a一个disquieting令人不安的proposition.观点。
Perhaps也许，incompetence无能（这种东西）can会cloak掩藏住itself,它自己，
robbing people of剥夺人们的the discernment识别能力，to notice去注意到their deficiencies.他们自身的不足。
For a 1999 paper,为了1999年的一篇论文，Dunning邓宁and和his student他的学生Justin Kruger贾斯汀·克鲁格
quizzed测试了Cornell康奈尔的undergraduates本科生，on考察了（关于）humour,幽默、grammar语法and和logical reasoning.逻辑推理的能力。
The bottom垫底的quarter of四分之一scorers答题者，averaged平均只处在the 12th percentile（倒数）第12百分位，
but却generously大方地（高估自己）placed themselves把自己估在near the 62nd,第62百分位附近，
whereas而high achievers成绩优异者showed却表现出undue过分的modesty.谦虚。
The unskilled,能力不足的人，the authors argued,两位作者认为，bear背负着a dual burden,双重负担，
since因为appraisal评估（自我评估）calls for需要the same skills相同的技能，as execution.与执行（所需）的一样。
Detractors批评者traced查明part of部分the Dunning-Kruger disparity邓宁-克鲁格差异，
to源于regression to the mean,均值回归，
（trace A to B：追溯 A 的来源到 B、查明 A 源于 B）
a一种statistical统计学的mirage假象，the paper该论文（即前文1999年邓宁-克鲁格论文）partially也曾部分acknowledged.承认过。
Studies designed to avoid it专为避开这一问题而设计的研究
still find仍发现了a smaller一个较小but tangible但切实存在的effect.效应。
In 20232023年，the pair两人garnered赢得了the Grawemeyer格文美尔psychology心理学award.奖。
In fairness,平心而论，Wheeler惠勒had tested his method empirically确实用实证的方法检验过他的这套办法，
before在staking his liberty on it.他把自己的自由赌在抢劫上之前。
（stake A on B：把A赌在B上）
The camera that missed him那台没拍到他的相机，
was正是the one he他turned转动（将镜头）on对准himself.自己的那一台。
（那相机只是没有拍到他，他却以为自己隐形了，误以为是柠檬水的作用）

考研英语精读 · No.02

The Truth About One Marshmallow

一颗棉花糖的真相
Picture想象一下a nursery school一所幼儿园，
on Stanford's campus位于斯坦福大学校园里，
in the late 1960s and early 1970s.时间是20世纪60年代末至70年代初。
A一个child of about four or five大约四五岁的孩子
sits in solitude独自坐在before one treat,一份零食前，
facing面对a一桩blunt直截了当的bargain:交易：
eat it now,现在就吃，
or或者resist抵挡temptation诱惑for about 15 minutes约15分钟，
and就能earn挣得two.两份。
Impulsive冲动的children孩子often往往surrender instantly;立刻缴械投降；
resolute意志坚定的ones孩子hold out.则坚持到底。
Follow-ups后续研究made让the test这项测验legendary.变得家喻户晓。
A 1990 study in Developmental Psychology1990年《发展心理学》上的一项研究
found that,发现，as adolescents,到了青少年时期，
longer waiters等得更久的孩子tended to往往attain higher scores on the SAT,在SAT中取得更高分数，
a US college-entrance exam.即美国的大学入学考试。
A一份trivial微不足道的snack零食
became成了an emblem of virtue,美德的象征，
and而restraint at four四岁时的克制
was glorified as被美化为a predictor of destiny.命运的预测指标。
Yet然而，the foundation其根基was precarious.岌岌可危。
Only只有a few dozen children几十个孩子
entered进入了the SAT analysis,SAT分析，
predominantly主要是the sons and daughters of Stanford faculty and postgraduate students.斯坦福教师和研究生的子女。
Such a如此homogeneous,同质的、privileged优越的group群体
could scarcely很难represent代表ordinary families,普通的家庭，
and而且prosperous homes富裕的家庭may可能foster patience and academic success alike.同时培养出耐心与学业成就。
A study published in Psychological Science in May 20182018年5月发表于《心理科学》的一项研究
ran做了a一次conceptual概念性的replication:重复验证：
the same idea,同样的思路，a new sample,新的样本，modified调整过的methods.方法。
More than 900900多名youngsters from diverse backgrounds来自多样背景的少年儿童
were assessed at four and a half在四岁半时接受评估，and again at 15.15岁时再测一次。
The raw link未经校正的相关was roughly half as strong as the original finding.强度大约只有原研究结果的一半。
Adjusting for在校正family background家庭背景
and和early早期cognitive ability认知能力之后，
shrank it这一相关缩小了by about two thirds,约三分之二，
leaving使得most estimates大多数估计值statistically在统计上insignificant.不显著。
The test这项测验is not并非worthless;毫无价值；
it它may still或许仍能reflect反映something substantive.某些实质性的东西。
Research published in Cognition in January 20132013年1月发表于《认知》的研究
adds补充了context:背景：
children whose trust an adult had just betrayed刚被成年人辜负信任的孩子，
waited等待的时间markedly less than明显短于
peers dealing with a reliable adult.与守信的成年人相处的同龄人。
How long a child perseveres孩子能坚持多久，
depends partly on部分取决于
how trustworthy the adults around them seem.身边的大人看起来有多可信。

速查表 · 54 词

词释义段
nursery school本文：幼儿园；n. 幼儿园，托儿所（nursery school；a day nursery）；育婴室1
campus本文：校园；n. 校园（on campus；a university campus）1
in solitude本文：独自；n. 独处，孤独（in solitude；peace and solitude）1
blunt本文：直截了当的；adj. 直截了当的，生硬的（a blunt question/refusal）；钝的1
bargain本文：交易；n. 交易，协议（strike/make a bargain）；便宜货；v. 讨价还价1
resist本文：抵挡；v. 抵制，抵挡（resist temptation/pressure；can't resist doing）1
temptation本文：诱惑；n. 诱惑（resist/give in to temptation）1
earn本文：挣得；v. 挣得，赢得（earn money/respect/a reward）1
Impulsive本文：冲动的；adj. 冲动的（impulsive behaviour/buying）1
surrender本文：缴械投降；v. 投降，屈服（surrender to temptation/the enemy）；n. 投降1
instantly本文：立刻；adv. 立即，马上（be instantly recognisable）1
resolute本文：意志坚定的；adj. 坚决的，意志坚定的（a resolute refusal；resolute in doing）1
Follow-ups本文：后续研究；n. (follow-up) 后续研究，追踪（a follow-up study/visit）2

考研英语精读 · No.03

The Doctor Who Drank Bacteria to Win an Argument

喝下细菌的医生

In 1984 Barry Marshall, a 32-year-old doctor at Fremantle Hospital, drank a brew of beef broth into which the microbial growth of two Petri dishes had been scraped.

1984年，弗里曼特尔医院32岁的医生巴里·马歇尔喝下了一杯汤剂。那是一份牛肉清汤，两个培养皿里长出的微生物菌落已被刮进其中。

The prevalent doctrine blamed ulcers on stress, spicy food and excess acid secretion. No bacterium, the profession firmly held, could withstand so caustic and hostile a habitat.

当时流行的学说把溃疡归咎于压力、辛辣食物以及胃酸过量分泌。医学界一直坚定地认为，没有哪种细菌能经受得住如此具有腐蚀性、如此充满敌意的栖息环境。

Yet Robin Warren, a pathologist at Royal Perth Hospital, had seen curved, coiled bacteria in gastric biopsies from patients with gastritis. Marshall began to collaborate with him. Attempts to culture the germ were invariably unsuccessful until Easter 1982, when plates sat undisturbed in the incubator for about five days. They were ordinarily binned after two. Colonies blossomed. The organism was later named Helicobacter pylori.

然而，皇家珀斯医院的病理学家罗宾·沃伦，已经在胃炎病人胃部的活检样本中，看到了弯曲盘绕的细菌。马歇尔开始与他合作。培养这种病菌的尝试一律以失败告终，直到1982年复活节，培养皿原封不动地在培养箱里放了约五天。这些培养皿平常两天后就会被扔掉。菌落蓬勃生长起来。这种微生物后来被命名为幽门螺杆菌。

In 1983 unimpressed assessors at the Gastroenterological Society of Australia relegated the pair's paper to the bottom tenth of that year's submissions. Efforts to infect swine were likewise in vain. Marshall made himself the guinea pig.

1983年，澳大利亚胃肠病学会那些不以为然的评审，把两人的论文归入当年投稿中最差的10%。让猪只感染的尝试也同样是徒劳。马歇尔便把自己当成了小白鼠。

Within days Marshall was vomiting, his breath had a disagreeable odour and weariness set in. About ten days later an endoscopy revealed gastritis, an inflammation of the stomach's mucous membrane and a precursor to ulcers, albeit not an ulcer itself. The germ was cultured directly from his stomach.

几天之内，马歇尔就开始呕吐，呼吸带着难闻的气味，疲倦也随之袭来。约十天后，胃镜检查发现了胃炎，也就是胃黏膜发生炎症，这是溃疡的前兆，尽管本身还不是溃疡。从他的胃里直接培养出了这种细菌。

Spurred on by his insistent spouse, Marshall took antibiotics, which cleared the infection. Ulcers came to be treated with such medication.

在态度坚决的妻子一再催促下，马歇尔服用了抗生素，清除了感染。后来，溃疡开始用这类药物治疗。

In 2005 Marshall and Warren shared the Nobel Prize in Physiology or Medicine for discovering Helicobacter pylori and its role in gastritis and peptic ulcer disease. Gastritis, the illness Marshall had willingly given himself, is named in the prize citation.

2005年，马歇尔与沃伦因发现幽门螺杆菌及其在胃炎和消化性溃疡中的作用，共同获得诺贝尔生理学或医学奖。马歇尔当年心甘情愿让自己染上的胃炎，就写在获奖评语里。

考研英语精读 · No.04

The Brain That Learned London

被街道重塑的大脑

Name two addresses and a London cabbie will rattle off the quickest route. That prowess is hard-won. To earn a licence, aspiring drivers must conquer "the Knowledge", an arduous examination on about 25,000 streets within six miles of Charing Cross. Hopefuls typically spend three to four years committing this labyrinth to memory, and only about half qualify.

随便说出两个地址，伦敦的出租车司机就能脱口说出最快的路线。这份本领来之不易。要拿到执照，立志入行的司机必须攻克「知识大考」（The Knowledge，伦敦出租车执照考试的俗称）——一场艰巨的考试，考查查令十字周边6英里内约25,000条街道。入行考生通常要花三到四年才能把这座迷宫记在脑子里，而最终合格的只有大约一半。

A study published in PNAS in April 2000 scanned 16 male cabbies and 50 other men. The hippocampus is a seahorse-shaped structure vital to memory and navigation. In the cabbies, its rear, or posterior, portion was larger than in the other men, and its front, or anterior, portion smaller. Longer service went with a larger rear portion.

2000年4月，《美国国家科学院院刊》（PNAS）发表的一项研究扫描了16名男性出租车司机和50名其他男性的大脑。海马体是一个形似海马的结构，对记忆和导航至关重要。司机们海马体靠后的部分，即后部，比其他男性的大；而靠前的部分，即前部，则较小。从业时间越长，后部也越大。

Yet the link left the cause uncertain. Perhaps an innate flair for spatial tasks lured people to the occupation, or perhaps stressful driving was the culprit. A December 2006 study dispelled the second notion: cabbies had more rear grey matter, the tissue dense with nerve cells, than bus drivers, confined to predetermined routes, of equivalent experience and stress.

然而，这种关联让原因仍不确定。也许是天生的空间天赋把人们吸引到这个职业中来，也许紧张的驾驶才是罪魁祸首。2006年12月的一项研究打消了第二种想法：出租车司机后部的灰质——即密布神经细胞的组织——多于那些局限于预先确定的线路、经验和压力都相当的公交司机。

A December 2011 longitudinal study tackled the first, scanning 79 trainees and 31 non-trainees at enrolment and three to four years later. Their brains began alike. Only the 39 who qualified exhibited an expansion of rear grey matter, so the sequence suggests learning preceded the enlargement.

2011年12月的一项纵向研究着手解决第一种疑问：它在入学时以及三到四年后，两次扫描了79名学员和31名非学员。所有人的大脑起点相同。只有最终合格的39人显示出后部灰质的增长，因此这一先后顺序提示，学习先于增大发生。

The gain appeared to entail a trade-off: qualified drivers trailed the non-trainees on one test of recalling an intricate figure. The cohorts were small and exclusively male, yet the studies imply the healthy adult brain can remodel itself through intensive learning. Every street learned, it seems, occupied room once claimed by something else.

这种收获似乎必然带来一种此消彼长的取舍：在一项回忆复杂图形的测试中，合格司机落后于非学员。这些研究的样本群体规模小，而且清一色是男性，但它们意味着，健康的成人大脑能够通过高强度学习重塑自身。看来，每记住一条街道，都占据了一块原本被其他东西占用的空间。

考研英语精读 · No.05

The Fingerprint in the Blood

血里的指纹

In November 1983 a 15-year-old girl was murdered in the Leicestershire village of Narborough, and the homicide went unsolved. In the summer of 1986 a second 15-year-old was slain near neighbouring Enderby. A 17-year-old local youth confessed to the second killing, and to detectives the perplexing investigation was seemingly resolved.

1983年11月，莱斯特郡纳伯勒村一名15岁女孩遇害，这起凶杀案一直未能侦破。1986年夏，又一名15岁少女在邻近的恩德比附近被杀。当地一名17岁青年供认第二起命案是他所为；在警探看来，这场扑朔迷离的调查似乎已经了结。

The revelation had come two years previously. On 10 September 1984 Alec Jeffreys, a geneticist at the University of Leicester, discerned on an X-ray film molecular markers whose layout varied considerably from person to person. He called them DNA fingerprints, since, like fingerprints, they differentiated individuals. The journal Nature promptly carried his paper in March 1985.

关键的发现其实早在两年前就已出现。1984年9月10日，莱斯特大学遗传学家亚历克·杰弗里斯在一张X光片上看出，一些分子标记的排布因人各异，差别相当大。他把这些标记称为DNA指纹，因为它们像指纹一样，能把不同的个人区分开来。1985年3月，《自然》期刊很快刊登了他的论文。

Police enlisted Jeffreys, and his genetic tests overturned the erroneous confession. The youth was not the perpetrator, and the same attacker had killed both girls. The youth became the first person known to be cleared by DNA evidence. The forensic innovation had freed an innocent before it implicated anyone.

警方请来杰弗里斯，他的基因检测推翻了那份错误的供词。那名青年并不是行凶者，杀害两名女孩的是同一个袭击者。这名青年成为已知第一个因 DNA 证据洗清嫌疑的人。这项法医创新先让一名无辜者重获自由，之后才牵连到别人。

In January 1987 police commenced the world's first large-scale DNA screening, asking more than 5,000 men in three villages for blood or saliva. None matched. The elusive killer, a local baker, had induced a co-worker to give a sample in his name, evading suspicion. In August 1987 someone overheard the co-worker discussing the substitution and notified police.

1987年1月，警方开始了世界上首次大规模DNA筛查，请三个村庄的5,000多名男子提供血液或唾液。结果没有一份吻合。这个难以捉摸的凶手是当地一名面包师，他诱使一名同事冒他的名字提供样本，躲过了怀疑。1987年8月，有人无意中听到这名同事谈起冒名顶替的事，并告知了警方。

Colin Pitchfork was detained on 19 September 1987, and his DNA profile matched. In January 1988 he pleaded guilty and was jailed for life, the first person convicted of murder on the basis of DNA evidence. A tip-off, not the screening, had unmasked him. The test's first beneficiary had been a blameless juvenile who had falsely admitted the offence.

1987年9月19日，科林·皮奇福克被拘留，他的DNA图谱相符。1988年1月，他认罪，被判处终身监禁，成为第一个依据 DNA 证据被判谋杀罪的人。让他现出原形的是一条举报，而不是那次筛查。这项检测的第一个受益者，是一名清白的少年，他曾违心认下这项罪行。

考研英语精读 · No.06

The Horse That Could Count

会算术的马

Wilhelm von Osten, a mathematics teacher and trainer in Berlin, owned a stallion, Hans. Asked to calculate a sum, attempt subtraction or name a date, Hans tapped out the answer with a hoof. Such aptitude suggested brilliance, and demonstrations in his owner's courtyard made him seem intelligent. The New York Times reported the stunning act in 1904.

冯·奥斯滕是柏林的一名数学教师和驯马师，他有一匹名叫汉斯的公马。让汉斯计算一道算式、试做减法或说出一个日期，它就会用蹄子敲出答案。这样的天资显示出一种聪慧，在主人庭院里的表演也让它看上去很聪明。1904年，《纽约时报》报道了这一令人惊叹的表演。

A commission of thirteen was formed to investigate. It included a veterinarian, a circus manager, a cavalry officer, schoolteachers and the director of the Berlin zoological garden. Its members looked for trickery and collusion. In September 1904 they found no deception, in effect certifying the act authentic.

一个13人委员会成立，负责调查。成员包括一名兽医、一名马戏团经理、一名骑兵军官、几名中小学教师，以及柏林动物园园长。委员们查找诡计和串通。1904年9月，他们没有发现任何骗局，实际上等于认证了这场表演货真价实。

In 1907 the psychologist Oskar Pfungst tested the act systematically, altering one variable after another. He kept onlookers away, rotated questioners, fitted blinders and sometimes used people ignorant of the answer. When Hans could see the questioner, he answered correctly 50 times in 56. With the questioner invisible, his accuracy fell to two in 35.

1907年，心理学家奥斯卡·普丰斯特对这一表演进行了系统测试，逐一改变各个变量。他让围观者远离，轮换提问者，给马戴上眼罩，有时还找对答案一无所知的人来提问。汉斯能看见提问者时，56次中答对了50次；提问者不在视线内时，它的准确率降到35次中仅对2次。

The cues came from the questioners. Each would lean forward as tapping began, and tension rose as the taps neared the answer. At the right tap it eased, and the questioner would straighten or jerk the head upward. Every gesture and motion was subconscious, made innocently by people with no wish to deceive.

提示来自提问者。敲击开始时，每个提问者都会前倾身体；敲击越接近答案，紧张就越强。敲到正确的那一下时，紧张随即缓解，提问者会挺直身子，或把头向上猛地一抬。每个手势和动作都出于下意识，都是无心做出的，做出这些动作的人并不想欺骗。

Double-blind designs in psychology and animal cognition guard against the Clever Hans effect, a phenomenon of bias in which expectancy shapes behavioural results. In the laboratory, Pfungst took the animal's place and proved sensitive to the cues of attentive volunteers. Hans went, on his owner's death in June 1909, to a jeweller still convinced that such animals reasoned.

心理学和动物认知研究中的双盲设计，正是为了防范"聪明汉斯效应"。这是一种偏差现象，指预期会影响行为结果。在实验室里，普丰斯特亲自扮演马的角色，事实证明，他对专注的志愿者发出的提示也很敏感。汉斯在主人于1909年6月去世后，转到一位珠宝商手中，此人仍坚信马具有推理能力。

考研英语精读 · No.07

The Octopus That Escaped

越狱的章鱼

One night in early 2016, after keepers at New Zealand's National Aquarium in Napier had left, an octopus named Inky crept through an aperture in the mesh atop its tank. The fugitive presumably dropped to the floor, crossed the room and compressed its elastic body into a drain, whose pipe stretched some 50 metres to the Pacific.

2016年初的一个夜晚，新西兰内皮尔国家水族馆的饲养员下班离开后，一只名叫 Inky 的章鱼从水箱顶部网罩上的一道开口悄悄爬出。这名逃犯想必随后落到地面，爬过房间，把富有弹性的身体压扁，挤进一个排水口；排水口下面那条管道延伸约50米，直通太平洋。

Staff reconstructed that route from the slime residue it deposited. Inky had resided in captivity since 2014, when a fisherman retrieved it from a crayfish pot off Napier. The breakout went unreported for about three months. Made public in April 2016, the story travelled the globe.

工作人员根据它留下的黏液残迹，还原了这条路线。Inky 自2014年起就生活在圈养环境中；那一年，一名渔民在内皮尔附近海域的捕龙虾笼子里捞到了它。这次出逃约三个月都无人报道。2016年4月消息公开后，这件事传遍了全球。

Such agility stems from an octopus's pliable body, which has no skeleton or shell. Its only hard part is its beak, so any crevice that accommodates the beak will do. Roughly two-thirds of its 500 million neurons lie in the limbs, which handle sensory input and react with some autonomy.

如此灵巧的身手源于章鱼柔韧的身体，它没有骨骼，也没有外壳。它唯一坚硬的部位是喙部，所以任何容得下喙部的裂缝都行。章鱼的5亿个神经元中，约三分之二分布在腕足里；腕足能处理感官信息，并在一定程度上自主作出反应。

Its wits are as versatile as its anatomy. A giant Pacific octopus in Seattle learned to open screw-top jars, needing 15 minutes at the outset, later about two. In a 2010 study, octopuses came to discriminate between two strangers, a feeder and one who touched them with a bristly stick, adopting a different hue and posture for each.

章鱼的才智和它的身体构造一样灵活多变。西雅图的一只巨型太平洋章鱼学会了拧开螺旋盖罐子，起初要花15分钟，后来约两分钟。在2010年的一项研究中，章鱼逐渐学会区分两个陌生人，一个是喂食者，另一个用带刺毛的棍子触碰它们；章鱼面对两人时呈现出不同的体色和姿态。

In July 2012 the Cambridge Declaration on Consciousness listed octopuses among animals possessing the neurological substrates for conscious awareness. A November 2021 UK government review recommended treating them as sentient, and the Animal Welfare (Sentience) Act encompassed them in April 2022. None of this proves an inner life. Inky, somewhere beyond the pipe, remained wholly oblivious to the debate.

2012年7月，《剑桥意识宣言》把章鱼列入具备有意识觉知所需神经基础的动物之列。2021年11月，一份为英国政府所做的评估报告建议把章鱼视为有感知力的动物；2022年4月，英国《动物福利（感知）法》将章鱼纳入其中。这些都不能证明章鱼有内心世界。Inky 身在管道另一头的某个地方，对这场争论全然不知。

考研英语精读 · No.08

The Doctor Who Solved the Cholera Mystery

破解霍乱之谜的医生

In the first ten days of September 1854, cholera ravaged an overcrowded corner of London's Soho; upwards of 500 people perished. Most doctors attributed epidemics to miasma, foul air arising from filth. John Snow, a physician who had been convinced since 1849 that cholera's poison was swallowed, not inhaled, suspected the Broad Street pump.

1854年9月上旬，霍乱在伦敦苏豪区一个拥挤不堪的角落肆虐，逾五百人丧命。多数医生把流行病归因于瘴气，即从污秽中产生的污浊空气。内科医生约翰·斯诺自1849年起就确信霍乱的毒素是吞进去而非吸进去的，他怀疑问题出在宽街那口水泵上。

Finding little visible impurity, he hesitated, then consulted the death register and questioned bereaved families. Most victims lived near the pump and drank from it. At his urging, parish officials removed the handle, but daily fatal attacks had already plunged from a peak of 143 to 12. He later mapped the deaths.

他在泵水里没发现多少肉眼可见的杂质，一时犹豫，随后查阅死亡登记册，又去询问痛失亲人的家庭。死者大多住在这口泵附近，也喝过它的水。在他的敦促下，教区官员卸下了泵柄，可这时每天新发的致死病例早已从143例的峰值骤降到12例。他后来把死亡病例标在了地图上。

In 1855 Edmund Parkes, a professor of clinical medicine, interpreted the cluster on that map as a poison drifting through the air. Yet a map could chart where people died, not what they drank.

1855年，临床医学教授埃德蒙·帕克斯看到那张地图上死者扎堆，便把这种聚集解读为空气中飘散的毒素所致。可地图只能标出人死在哪里，标不出他们喝了什么。

The crucial evidence, gathered in 1854, lay in exceptions. Amid the deaths in the surrounding streets, a workhouse with its own well lost a mere five of 535 inmates. More than 70 nearby brewery workers, with a beer allowance and their own well, escaped severe cholera.

关键证据早在1854年就已收集到，就藏在那些例外里。在周围街巷死者接连不断之际，一所自有水井的济贫院（收容贫民的机构）里，535名收容者只死了区区五人。附近一家啤酒厂的七十多名工人有啤酒配给，厂里也有自己的水井，结果都没有染上重症霍乱。

Conversely, a Hampstead widow who had not set foot in Soho for months had the pump's water carted to her daily. She died, as did a visiting niece who drank it. Hampstead then had no cholera.

反过来，汉普斯特德的一位寡妇已有几个月没踏进过苏豪，却每天让人把这口泵的水用车运来。她死了，来探望她并喝了这水的侄女也死了。当时汉普斯特德并没有霍乱。

In 1855 a government inquiry declined to condemn the pump, contending its water had at most absorbed airborne poison. Meanwhile, the sceptical curate Henry Whitehead set out to refute Snow but found that those who drank from the pump were far more likely to succumb to the disease than others, vindicating him.

1855年，政府的调查委员会不肯给这口泵定罪，坚称泵水至多是吸收了经空气传播的毒素。与此同时，心存怀疑的副牧师（即协助教区牧师的低级神职人员）亨利·怀特黑德一心想驳倒斯诺，却发现喝泵水的人死于这种病的可能性远高于旁人，反倒证明了斯诺是对的。

考研英语精读 · No.09

The Great Emu War

向鸸鹋宣战

Around Campion, Western Australia, most farmers were First World War veterans, settlers placed by a government scheme on marginal parcels of farmland. They were inexperienced growers, and a quarter had already quit. In 1932, after drought, roughly 20,000 emus swarmed in from arid country in flocks and trampled the wheat crops.

在西澳大利亚州坎皮恩一带，大多数农民是第一次世界大战的退伍军人。他们作为定居者，由政府计划安置在贫瘠的小块土地上，以这些农地为生。他们是缺乏经验的种植者，已有四分之一的人放弃了土地。1932年旱灾过后，约2万只鸸鹋结成鸟群，从干旱地区蜂拥而至，践踏了小麦庄稼。

The farmers' plight took a military turn. In desperation, a delegation of ex-soldiers aired their grievances and asked the Defence Minister for machine-guns. He agreed, sending not a battalion but a detachment: Major Meredith, an artillery officer, with two soldiers, two Lewis guns and 10,000 rounds of ammunition. The offensive began on 2 November 1932.

农民的困境由此走向了军事手段。绝望之中，一个由退伍士兵组成的代表团陈述了不满，请求国防部长提供机枪。部长同意了，但派出的不是整营兵力，而是一支小分队，由炮兵军官梅雷迪思少校带领两名士兵，携两挺刘易斯机枪和1万发弹药。1932年11月2日，攻势展开。

At a dam, about a thousand birds approached an ambush, but a gun jammed after a dozen fell. The flocks broke formation into small, agile groups, frustrating each tactical plan, and a truck-mounted gun proved futile. Six days of wasteful firing spent 2,500 rounds on perhaps 50 to 200 birds, a ludicrous return. After parliamentary questions and press ridicule, an embarrassing withdrawal came on 8 November, though shooting restarted within days.

在一处水坝，约一千只鸟走近伏击圈，但打倒十来只后，一挺机枪就卡壳了。鸟群打乱队形，分成灵活的小群，让每一个战术计划都落了空；装在卡车上的机枪也徒劳无功。六天的浪费射击打掉了2,500发，只打死大约50至200只鸟，这样的回报荒唐可笑。在议会质询和报界嘲讽之后，部队于11月8日撤出，颇为尴尬；不过没过几天，射击又重新开始。

Meredith was recalled on 10 December. His memo's inventory counted 986 kills from 9,860 rounds, a ratio of ten bullets to one bird. He likened the resilient birds' toughness to the armour of tanks. Five weeks of warfare had barely dented the hardy flocks' endurance.

12月10日，梅雷迪思被召回。他在备忘录里开列的清单显示，9,860发打死了986只，比例是十发子弹对一只鸟。他把这些耐打的鸟的坚韧比作坦克的装甲。五个星期的战事几乎没有削弱这些顽强鸟群的耐力。

Later pleas for reinforcements were refused, and intervention gave way to a bounty sanctioned by the government. This payout per bird gave farmers compensation and an incentive for extermination. Farmers claimed over 57,000 payments in six months of 1934, more than fifty-seven times Meredith's 986 kills.

后来请求增援的恳求都遭到拒绝，干预让位于政府批准的悬赏。这种按只计算的赏款给了农民补偿，也成为灭除鸸鹋的动力。1934年的六个月里，农民领取了5.7万多笔赏金，是梅雷迪思那986只战果的五十七倍多。

考研英语精读 · No.10

The Stolen Smile

被偷走的微笑

On Monday 21 August 1911, when the Louvre was customarily closed, the Mona Lisa was discreetly plucked from its wall. The disappearance went unnoticed until the next day, when a painter arriving to sketch found a conspicuous gap of bare plaster. Custodians shuttered the museum for a week.

1911年8月21日是星期一，卢浮宫照例闭馆，《蒙娜丽莎》就在这一天被人从墙上悄悄摘走。画作的失踪一直无人察觉，直到第二天，一位前来画素描的画家发现，灰泥墙面上露出一块显眼的空缺。管理人员把博物馆关闭了一周。

When the museum reopened, curious onlookers queued to stare at the vacant space where the masterpiece had hung. On 7 September 1911 the poet Guillaume Apollinaire was held in custody for about a week, and Pablo Picasso was interrogated. Both were cleared of involvement in the burglary.

博物馆重新开放后，好奇的围观者排队前来，凝视那块空荡荡的地方，那幅杰作原先就挂在那里。1911年9月7日，诗人纪尧姆·阿波利奈尔被拘押了约一周，巴勃罗·毕加索也受到审问。两人都洗清了牵涉这起盗窃案的嫌疑。

The intruder was Vincenzo Peruggia, an Italian who had temporarily worked at the Louvre as a glazier. Having lurked in a storeroom on Sunday, he strode out unchallenged in a workman's smock, the painting tucked beneath his clothes. For more than two years it lay stashed in a false-bottomed trunk in his Paris lodgings.

闯入者是文森佐·佩鲁贾，一个曾在卢浮宫短期做过玻璃工的意大利人。他星期天潜伏在一间储藏室里，之后穿着一件工作罩衫大步走了出去，无人盘问，画就塞在他的衣服底下。此后两年多，这幅画一直藏匿在他巴黎住处一只带夹层底的箱子里。

In December 1913 Peruggia contacted Alfredo Geri, an art dealer in Florence. The Uffizi's director authenticated the painting at Peruggia's hotel; Peruggia was apprehended on 11 December 1913. Citing patriotism, he said he wanted it repatriated, mistakenly believing Napoleon had looted it. In fact Leonardo da Vinci had accompanied it to France at the invitation of King Francis I.

1913年12月，佩鲁贾联系了佛罗伦萨的艺术品商人阿尔弗雷多·杰里。乌菲齐美术馆馆长在佩鲁贾下榻的旅馆鉴定了这幅画，1913年12月11日，佩鲁贾被逮捕。他以爱国为由，说想把画送回祖国；他误以为拿破仑掠走了这幅画。事实上，列奥纳多·达·芬奇应国王弗朗索瓦一世的邀请，随身带着这幅画去了法国。

After showings in Italy, the painting was reinstated in the Louvre on 4 January 1914. In June 1914 a tribunal imposed a penalty of a year and 15 days, but he was imprisoned for about seven months. Many historians argue the heist elevated one esteemed gem among many to the world's most renowned painting. If so, its fame began with a bare wall.

这幅画在意大利展出之后，于1914年1月4日重回卢浮宫。1914年6月，法庭判处佩鲁贾一年零十五天的刑罚，但他实际坐牢约七个月。许多史学家认为，这幅画原本是众多备受推崇的珍品之一，这起盗画案把它推上了世界最著名画作的位置。若真如此，它的名气始于一面光秃秃的墙。

考研英语精读 · No.11

Move 37

第37手

Go is said to allow more configurations than the universe has atoms, a daunting computational challenge. In October 2015 DeepMind's AlphaGo reached a milestone, becoming the first program to beat a professional on a full board without a handicap. In March 2016 its opponent in a televised Seoul showdown was Lee Sedol, a South Korean strategist with 18 international titles.

据说，围棋可能出现的局面比宇宙中的原子还多，这是一项艰巨的计算挑战。2015年10月，DeepMind 的 AlphaGo 成为首个在全尺寸棋盘上不让子击败职业棋手的程序，达成一个里程碑。2016年3月，在首尔一场电视转播的对决中，它的对手是拥有18个国际冠军头衔、善于谋略的韩国棋手李世石。

In game two, on 10 March, AlphaGo's 37th move was a shoulder hit, a tactic placing a stone adjacent to an opponent's on the diagonal. On the grid's fifth line, it was hardly orthodox. Commentators found the unexpected placement bizarre; one took it for a mistake. Lee left the room, his pause for deliberation lasting over ten minutes.

第二局于3月10日进行，AlphaGo 的第37手是一记肩冲。这种招法是把一枚棋子下在对方棋子的斜对角，并与之紧邻。这一手落在棋盘的第五线上，算不上正统下法。解说员觉得这步出人意料的落子十分怪异，其中一人还以为是失误。李世石离开了对局室，这次暂停思考持续了十多分钟。

DeepMind's diagnostic tools showed AlphaGo had put the odds of a human playing that stone at one in 10,000, a staggering rarity. AlphaGo had first learned to imitate strong amateurs online, then sharpened its play over tens of millions of simulated games against itself. Lee later acknowledged the stone's originality and beauty.

DeepMind 的诊断工具显示，AlphaGo 曾估算人类下出这一手的概率为万分之一，罕见得惊人。AlphaGo 起初在网上模仿强业余棋手的下法，随后通过数千万盘与自己的模拟对局磨炼棋艺。李世石后来承认了这一手的独创性和美感。

The fourth game, on 13 March, reversed the roles in neat symmetry. Lee's 78th move turned the tide, and many professionals heralded it as divine. DeepMind's team computed a professional's likelihood of finding it: about one in 10,000. Lee's only win was a consolation amid AlphaGo's overwhelming 4–1 victory.

第四局于3月13日进行，双方角色互换，形成工整的对称。李世石的第78手扭转了局势，许多职业棋手把它誉为神之一手。DeepMind 团队算出职业棋手找到这一手的可能性约为万分之一。李世石唯一的胜局，是 AlphaGo 4比1压倒性胜利中的一点安慰。

In November 2019 Lee withdrew from the professional circuit, a farewell he tied to AI's unrivalled dominance. Calling AI an invincible entity, he said the summit was beyond him. Yet his one win came from a stone whose odds, by DeepMind's figures, were as slim as those of AlphaGo's audacious shoulder hit.

2019年11月，李世石退出职业赛场，他把这次告别归因于人工智能无可匹敌的统治地位。他称人工智能是无法击败的存在，并说自己已无法登上顶峰。然而按 DeepMind 的数据，为他赢下唯一一局的那一子，概率和 AlphaGo 那记大胆的肩冲一样渺茫。

考研英语精读 · No.12

The 79 Days of New Coke

新可乐的 79 天

Pepsi had set up booths in shopping centres across the US, inviting shoppers to sip two unmarked beverages and state a preference. Figures varied by region, but tasters generally picked Pepsi more often. Each stall, letting consumers judge by palate alone, doubled as publicity.

百事可乐曾在全美各地的购物中心设置摊位，邀请购物者啜饮两种未标品牌的饮料，并说出自己的偏好。各地数字不一，但品尝者总体上更常选百事。每个摊点让消费者仅凭味觉作出判断，同时也成了一种宣传。

Coca-Cola's testing was thorough and extensive. Nearly 200,000 blind taste tests showed a sweeter formula beating the original and Pepsi. On 23 April 1985 the company announced it would replace its 99-year-old syrup, a year short of its centenary. The public called the soda New Coke. Its sweetness had given it a winning flavour, but launching the concoction was a wager.

可口可乐的测试既周密又广泛。近20万次盲测显示，一种更甜的配方胜过原版可乐和百事可乐。1985年4月23日，公司宣布将替换其已有99年历史的糖浆，此时距百年只差一年。公众把这种汽水称为新可乐。它的甜味让它拥有了能在盲测中胜出的口味，但推出这款调制饮品是一场赌注。

The backlash was a grassroots outcry. Consumer hotline calls rose from about 400 a day to about 1,500, almost all furious complaints. Protest groups were organised, and some faithful drinkers stocked up on the original. In June 1985 one protester, Gay Mullins, filed a lawsuit to keep New Coke out of packaging like the original's. Its dismissal came a week later.

这场反弹是一次草根的强烈抗议。消费者热线的电话从每天约400个增至约1,500个，几乎全是愤怒的投诉。示威团体组织起来，一些忠实的饮用者囤积了原版可乐。1985年6月，一位抗议者盖伊·马林斯提起诉讼，要求禁止新可乐使用与原版相似的包装。一周后，这起诉讼被驳回。

Pepsi's chief executive gave his employees a day off, treating the change as Coke's concession. After 79 days, on 11 July 1985, the original formula was relaunched as Coca-Cola Classic. The U-turn was a vindication for loyalists. ABC interrupted a soap opera to broadcast the comeback, which led two network news bulletins.

百事可乐首席执行官给员工放了一天假，把这次改变看作可口可乐的让步。79天后，1985年7月11日，原配方以"可口可乐经典"之名重新上市。这次大转弯是对拥趸的一次正名。ABC 电视台中断一部肥皂剧，播出可口可乐回归的消息，这条消息还成了两大电视网新闻简报的头条。

Coca-Cola had misjudged what the tests measured. They captured a mouthful, not the emotional attachment that nearly a century of familiarity had built. To many, the old taste carried nostalgia and heritage. The tests had hidden the brand from every taster, so they could not quantify what it meant to fiercely loyal drinkers.

可口可乐误判了这些测试衡量的是什么。测试捕捉的只是一小口的味道，而不是近一个世纪的熟悉所建立起来的情感依恋。对许多人来说，老味道承载着怀旧与传统。测试对每位品尝者都遮住了品牌，所以无法量化品牌对那些无比忠诚的饮用者意味着什么。

考研英语精读 · No.13

Eleven Days Without Sleep

十一天不睡觉的少年

On 28 December 1963 Randy Gardner, a 17-year-old science enthusiast at a San Diego high school, set out to forgo slumber for a regional science fair. Two companions diligently jolted him awake whenever his alertness waned and logged his mood. The project, a voluntary endeavour, went on to take first place.

1963年12月28日，圣迭戈一所高中的17岁科学爱好者兰迪·加德纳开始尝试放弃睡眠，以此参加一场地区性科学展。两名同伴尽心尽力，每当他的警觉减退，就让他猛然清醒过来，并记录他的情绪。这个项目是一次自愿的尝试，后来获得了第一名。

William Dement, a sleep researcher at Stanford, read press coverage of the attempt and was intrigued. He drove to San Diego and assisted John J. Ross, a Navy researcher, in monitoring the sleepless boy closely. The two investigators attentively noted the fluctuations in his state as the deprivation progressively deepened.

斯坦福大学的睡眠研究者威廉·德门特读到媒体对这次尝试的报道，大感好奇。他驾车前往圣迭戈，协助海军研究员约翰·J·罗斯密切监测这个不眠的少年。在睡眠剥夺逐步加深的过程中，两位研究人员细心地记下了他状态的种种波动。

The consequences soon mounted. Gardner grew moody, lost concentration and found his short-term memory impaired. He became suspicious, at times paranoid, and began to hallucinate. On the fourth day he was gripped by the delusion that he was a professional American football player.

后果很快接踵而至。加德纳变得喜怒无常，注意力难以集中，短时记忆也受损。他变得多疑，有时甚至偏执，还开始出现幻觉。第四天，他被一种妄想攫住，以为自己是一名美式橄榄球职业球员。

Gardner ended the wearying attempt on 8 January 1964, after 264.4 hours, about 11 days and 25 minutes. He then slept soundly for about 14 hours and woke apparently refreshed. Researchers probed the case in a 1965 scholarly paper, which helped highlight microsleeps, involuntary lapses into sleep lasting a few seconds. Gardner may have had such momentary bouts without noticing.

1964年1月8日，加德纳结束了这场令人疲惫的尝试，共历时264.4小时，约合11天零25分钟。随后他酣睡了约14小时，醒来时似乎神清气爽。研究者们在1965年的一篇学术论文中深入研究了这一案例，这篇论文帮助凸显了微睡眠，即持续几秒钟、不由自主地滑入睡眠的现象。加德纳可能经历过这种短暂的发作而未曾察觉。

In 1997 Guinness World Records discontinued monitoring such attempts because of the health perils, and others have since alleged longer stints awake. In a 2017 interview Gardner said severe insomnia had tormented him for about a decade, and he himself pinned the blame on the experiment. The boy who had seemed to bounce back from exhaustion with one long rest would, decades later, barely doze off.

1997年，吉尼斯世界纪录因健康风险停止监测此类尝试；此后，又有人宣称有过更长的清醒期。在2017年的一次采访中，加德纳说严重的失眠折磨了他约十年，他本人把它归咎于这次实验。那个睡了一个长觉、似乎就从疲惫中恢复过来的少年，几十年后几乎无法入睡。

考研英语精读 · No.14

The Fungus That Turns Ants into Zombies

让蚂蚁变僵尸的真菌

Deep in a Thai rainforest, a carpenter ant breaks from its colony's column in the lofty canopy. Swaying on unsteady legs, it wanders downward, as if intoxicated, into leafy undergrowth. Around midday it clenches its jaws on a major vein underneath a leaf, about 25 centimetres above the ground.

在泰国雨林深处，一只木匠蚁脱离了蚁群在高耸的树冠上行进的队列。它摇摇晃晃，腿脚不稳，像喝醉了一样一路向下慢慢游荡，钻进枝叶茂密的林下植被。正午前后，它把上颚死死咬紧在一片叶子下面的一条主叶脉上，离地面约25厘米。

By dusk the insect is lifeless, yet the bite holds rigidly. A fungus has destroyed its jaw muscles, so they cannot loosen. Two or three days later a fungal stalk pushes from its head and releases infectious spores on to the ground below, where foraging ants tread.

到黄昏时，这只昆虫已经断了气，那一口却仍牢牢咬着。一种真菌破坏了它上颚的肌肉，让它们无法松开。两三天后，一根真菌菌柄从它头部长出，把具有传染性的孢子撒到下方的地面上，那里正是觅食的蚂蚁踩踏经过的地方。

The invader, Ophiocordyceps unilateralis, is fussy about location. David Hughes and colleagues found the bites mostly on the north-west side of plants, where the air is humid and the temperature suits the fungus. When researchers moved infected ants higher, the fungus grew abnormally. Placed on the ground, the bodies disappeared.

这个入侵者，也就是偏侧蛇虫草菌，对位置十分挑剔。戴维·休斯和同事发现，这些咬合点大多在植物的西北侧，那里空气潮湿，温度也适合真菌。研究人员把受感染的蚂蚁移到更高处时，真菌长得很反常。放在地面上，尸体则不见了。

In 2017 a Penn State team examined infected ants with three-dimensional microscope imaging. Fungal cells had formed an intertwined lattice around muscle fibres in the legs and jaw, a picture that evokes the strings of a puppet. Yet the fungus had not infiltrated the brain.

2017年，宾夕法尼亚州立大学的一个团队用显微镜对受感染的蚂蚁做了三维成像。真菌细胞在腿部和上颚的肌肉纤维周围，形成了相互交织的网格，这幅图景让人想起操纵木偶的提线。不过，真菌并没有侵入大脑。

A Planet Earth episode, with David Attenborough's narration, showed one such ant. It helped inspire The Last of Us, a 2013 video game whose makers envisioned the parasite jumping to humans. The hypothetical scenario gave gamers a fictional nightmare.

由戴维·阿滕伯勒解说的一集《地球脉动》，展示过这样一只蚂蚁。它帮助启发了2013年的电子游戏《最后生还者》，游戏制作者们设想这种寄生菌跳到了人类身上。这个假想的情景给玩家带来了一场虚构的噩梦。

The real parasite's roots run far deeper, into the geological record. A 48-million-year-old fossil leaf from Messel, in Germany, bears the same characteristic bite scars.

现实中的寄生菌，其根源要深得多，一直延伸到地质记录之中。德国梅塞尔出土的一片4800万年前的叶子化石上，留着同样典型的咬合疤痕。

考研英语精读 · No.15

The Myth of the Unsinkable

永不沉没的神话

On 10 April 1912 the opulent liner Titanic left Southampton on her maiden voyage. A 1911 trade journal had dubbed her and her sister ship "practically unsinkable". At about 11.40pm on 14 April she collided with an iceberg; by 2.20am she had vanished beneath the waves, and the death toll would surpass 1,500.

1912年4月10日，豪华邮轮泰坦尼克号离开南安普敦，开始处女航。1911年，一家造船业刊物曾把她和她的姊妹船称作“几乎永不沉没”。4月14日晚约11时40分，她与一座冰山相撞；到次日凌晨2时20分，她已消失在海面之下，遇难人数最终突破1,500。

Folklore blames arrogance. Yet the Titanic was compliant with the law. Under 1894 rules, Britain's maritime regulator pegged lifeboat numbers to gross tonnage (volume, not weight) on a scale that topped out at 16 boats for 10,000 tons or more. At about 46,000 tons she carried 20, exceeding the requirement. Their capacity was 1,178, roughly half the 2,200 aboard: sufficient in law, inadequate at sea.

坊间流传的说法把灾难归咎于傲慢。然而泰坦尼克号合乎法规。按照1894年的规则，英国的海事监管机构把救生艇数量与总吨位（衡量船的体积而非重量）挂钩；按吨位分档的等级表，最高一档是“一万吨及以上”，只要求配16艘。泰坦尼克号约46,000吨，带了20艘，超出了要求。这些艇的容量为1,178人，大约只够船上2,200人的一半：按法律足够，到了海上却不足。

Within radio range, the steamer Californian kept an equally lawful routine. Her sole wireless operator, Cyril Evans, was on the payroll of the Marconi company; when he slept, so did the radio. Around 11.30pm he switched off his set. Hindsight may call that negligence, yet no rule obliged him to stay vigilant, so the Titanic's frantic distress calls went unheard there.

加州人号轮船就在无线电通讯范围内，船上的值班惯例同样合法。船上唯一的无线电报务员西里尔·埃文斯领的是马可尼公司的薪水；他一睡下，电台也就跟着睡了。晚上11时30分左右，他关掉了电台。以后见之明看，这或许算疏忽，但没有哪条规则责成他保持警觉，于是泰坦尼克号急切的求救呼叫在那艘船上无人听见。

Between April and July 1912, inquiries in Washington and London heard testimony from survivors and the Californian's crew. That July, American legislation made round-the-clock radio watch compulsory. In January 1914 delegates of 13 nations signed the first Safety of Life at Sea convention, stipulating lifeboat seats for all, continuous radio watch and an ice patrol. The First World War stalled the treaty; the patrol went ahead regardless.

1912年4月至7月，华盛顿和伦敦的调查听取了幸存者以及加州人号船员的证词。同年7月，美国一项立法把全天候电台值守定为强制措施。1914年1月，13国代表签署了第一部《海上人命安全公约》，规定救生艇座位要够所有人乘坐，电台要不间断值守，还要开展冰情巡逻。第一次世界大战让这项条约搁浅；冰情巡逻却照样开展起来。

The doomed liner sank within three hours. The outdated rules she sailed under had stayed afloat since 1894; it took her dead to discredit them.

这艘在劫难逃的邮轮不到三小时就沉没了。她所遵循的那套过时的规则，却自1894年起一直没有沉没；直到她的乘客葬身大海，它才信誉扫地。

考研英语精读 · No.16

The Bread Made from Air

从空气中造面包

In July 1909, BASF envoys gathered round a squat machine on a bench in Fritz Haber's Karlsruhe laboratory. Behind its gleaming, bolted valves, nitrogen and hydrogen met at crushing pressure and furnace heat, and transparent beads of ammonia began to drip slowly. Haber had pried nourishment from the air.

1909年7月，在弗里茨·哈伯位于卡尔斯鲁厄的实验室里，BASF的特使们围着工作台上一台矮墩墩的机器。在闪闪发亮、用螺栓紧固的阀门后面，氮和氢在巨大的压力和熔炉般的高温下相遇，合成氨凝成透明的液珠，开始缓缓地滴落。哈伯从空气中撬出了养分。

BASF's Carl Bosch scaled the method up, and from 1913 a gigantic plant at Oppau mass-produced ammonia. Hailed for making bread from air, Haber won the coveted 1918 Nobel Prize. Ploughed into stony soils as fertiliser, ammonia wards off famine as no manure heap could, bringing abundance. By 2008 it was feeding nearly half of mankind.

BASF的卡尔·博施把这一方法放大，从1913年起，奥保一座庞大的工厂开始大规模生产氨。哈伯因"从空气中造面包"而受到赞誉，获得了令人艳羡的1918年诺贝尔奖。氨作为化肥被翻耕进多石的土壤，抵御着饥荒，带来丰饶，这是任何粪肥垒成的肥堆都做不到的。到2008年，它养活了将近一半的人类。

Haber's ambition had a sinister side. Ammonia stocked Germany's arsenal with nitrates for munitions despite a British blockade, and Captain Haber marshalled chemical warfare. On 22 April 1915 at Ypres, troops vented about 168 tonnes of greenish, poisonous chlorine towards the Allied trenches, the first large-scale gas onslaught.

哈伯的雄心也有阴暗的一面。尽管英国实行封锁，合成氨仍为制造弹药所需的硝酸盐提供了来源，充实了德国的军火库；哈伯上尉则统筹指挥了化学战。1915年4月22日，在伊普尔，德军朝协约国的战壕方向释放了约168吨黄绿色、有毒的氯气，这是第一次大规模的毒气攻击。

Haber's wife, Clara Immerwahr, Germany's first woman with a doctoral degree in chemistry, voiced objections to his work. On 2 May 1915, after festivities in his honour, she shot herself in the garden with his service pistol. The next day the widower left for the Eastern Front.

哈伯的妻子克拉拉·伊梅瓦尔是德国第一位获得化学博士学位的女性，她对他的工作提出过反对。1915年5月2日，在为他举行的庆功活动之后，她在花园里用他的军用手枪自尽。第二天，这位丧妻之人动身前往东线。

Prestige offered no sanctuary. Of Jewish origin, Haber left Germany an outcast after Hitler's regime seized power, dying in Basel in 1934. His institute's pesticide research had produced Zyklon. A later variant, Zyklon B, was used in Nazi death camps, where relatives of his died.

声望没有给他带来庇护。哈伯是犹太裔，在希特勒政权随即夺取权力之后，他作为一个被放逐者离开德国，1934年死于巴塞尔。他研究所的杀虫剂研究曾催生齐克隆。后来的变体齐克隆B被用于纳粹死亡营，他的一些亲属就死在那里。

About half the nitrogen in our proteins and DNA has passed through a Haber-Bosch factory, so each of us carries a little of Haber's chemistry.

我们的蛋白质和DNA中约有一半的氮经过了哈伯-博施工厂，所以我们每个人身上都带着一点哈伯的化学。

考研英语精读 · No.17

The Curse of the Pharaoh

法老的诅咒

On 4 November 1922, in the Valley of the Kings, labourers engaged by the archaeologist Howard Carter uncovered a staircase carved into the rock. On 26 November, with his wealthy sponsor Lord Carnarvon beside him, Carter made a small hole in the sealed doorway and glimpsed the glittering gold of the boy king Tutankhamun. The spectacular find riveted the world.

1922年11月4日，在帝王谷，考古学家霍华德·卡特聘用的工人发现了一段凿入岩石的台阶。11月26日，卡特在他富有的赞助人卡纳冯勋爵的陪同下，在封住的门道上开了一个小孔，瞥见了少年国王图坦卡蒙闪闪发光的黄金。这一惊人的发现牢牢吸引了全世界。

Carnarvon sold exclusive rights to The Times, a monopoly that left competing reporters starved of news. Many resorted to speculation or sought outside commentators. In March 1923 the best-selling novelist Marie Corelli warned publicly that dire punishment awaited anyone who entered a sealed tomb. Within a fortnight, her words would seem uncanny.

卡纳冯把独家报道权卖给了《泰晤士报》，这种垄断让相互竞争的其他记者严重缺少新闻。许多人只好求助于猜测，或去找外界的评论家。1923年3月，畅销小说家玛丽·科雷利公开警告，任何进入封闭陵墓的人都将受到可怕的惩罚。不到两星期，她的话就显得出奇地应验。

That month a mosquito bit Carnarvon. He nicked the bite with a razor while shaving, it turned septic, and blood poisoning led to pneumonia. He died in Cairo on 5 April 1923. Newspapers reported that the city's lights failed at that instant and that his dog in England howled and died. Yet blackouts were almost a daily occurrence in Cairo.

当月，一只蚊子叮了卡纳冯。他刮胡子时用剃刀划破了叮咬处，伤口感染化脓，血液中毒又引发了肺炎。1923年4月5日，他在开罗去世。据报纸报道，就在那一刹那，开罗的灯全部熄灭，他在英国的狗也嚎叫着死去。然而，在当时的开罗，停电几乎是天天都有的事情。

No curse inscription was ever found in the tomb. In December 2002 a British Medical Journal study traced the fate of 44 Westerners in Egypt at the time. The 25 exposed to the tomb died at an average age of 70, versus 75 for the rest, a margin not statistically significant. The mortality figures lent the ominous rumour no credibility.

墓中从未发现任何诅咒铭文。2002年12月，《英国医学杂志》的一项研究追溯了当时身在埃及的44名西方人的命运。其中曾接触这座陵墓的25人，平均死亡年龄为70岁，相比之下其余的人为75岁；这一差距在统计上并不显著。这些死亡数据无法让这则不祥的传言显得可信。

Carnarvon's untimely demise had fuelled the tale, giving it impetus. Carter, the excavator who had unsealed the burial place, died in March 1939, aged 64, almost sixteen years after his patron. The legend had left him conspicuously unharmed.

卡纳冯的过早离世助长了这个故事，为它注入了动力。启封这处墓葬的发掘者卡特，于1939年3月去世，终年64岁，距他的资助人去世已近十六年。这个传说偏偏让他毫发无损，这一点格外醒目。

考研英语精读 · No.18

The Mould That Changed the World

改变人类命运的霉菌

In September 1928 Alexander Fleming, a bacteriologist at St Mary's Hospital, London, returned from holiday to find that a stray mould had contaminated a dish of staphylococcus bacteria. Nothing grew around it. Popular mythology blames an open window; the spores probably originated in a laboratory downstairs, where a colleague cultivated moulds.

1928年9月，伦敦圣玛丽医院的细菌学家亚历山大·弗莱明度假归来，发现一团不知从哪儿飘来的霉菌污染了一只培养葡萄球菌这种细菌的培养皿。霉菌周围什么也没长。坊间传说把它归咎于一扇敞开的窗户；其实孢子很可能来自楼下的实验室，一位同事在那里培育霉菌。

Fleming called the mould's juice penicillin and published in June 1929. Yet the crude juice was unstable and stubbornly hard to purify, and he and the chemists he enlisted lacked the expertise to refine it. He used it chiefly to isolate other microbes, and penicillin lay dormant, a curiosity rather than a cure.

弗莱明把霉菌分泌的这种液体称为青霉素，并于1929年6月发表了论文。然而，这种粗制的液体性质不稳定，又怎么也难以提纯；他和他请来的化学家都缺乏精炼它所需的专业知识。他主要用它来分离其他微生物，青霉素就此沉寂，只是件稀罕物，而非一种疗法。

A decade later, in Oxford, Howard Florey, an Australian pathologist, revived the work with Ernst Chain, a German-born chemist and Jewish refugee. Their colleague Norman Heatley improvised an ingenious apparatus to extract the compound. On 25 May 1940 eight mice received a lethal dose of bacteria; only the four also injected with penicillin lived. The Lancet, a prestigious journal, printed the outcome that August.

约十年后，在牛津，澳大利亚病理学家霍华德·弗洛里与德国出生的化学家、犹太难民恩斯特·钱恩一起，重启了这项研究。他们的同事诺曼·希特利用手边的材料临时拼凑出一套巧妙的装置，用来提取这种化合物。1940年5月25日，八只小鼠接种了致死剂量的细菌；只有另外注射了青霉素的那四只活了下来。同年8月，享有盛誉的期刊《柳叶刀》刊登了这一结果。

In February 1941 Albert Alexander, an Oxford constable gravely ill with infection, rallied on penicillin. When the scarce supply ran out, he died in March: the obstacle was quantity. From July 1941 a government laboratory in Peoria, Illinois, boosted yields about tenfold with corn-steep liquor, a starch by-product; later a mouldy market melon furnished the laboratory with a more prolific strain. By D-Day in June 1944 industrial fermentation met Allied needs.

1941年2月，牛津警员艾伯特·亚历山大因感染而病情危重，用上青霉素后有所好转。本就稀缺的青霉素用光后，他于3月不治身亡：障碍在于数量。从1941年7月起，伊利诺伊州皮奥里亚的一家政府实验室用玉米浆（淀粉生产的副产品）把产量提高了约十倍；后来，市场上一只发霉的甜瓜又为这家实验室提供了一株更高产的菌株。到1944年6月诺曼底登陆时，工业发酵已能满足盟军的需求。

In October 1945 Fleming, Florey and Chain shared the Nobel prize; Heatley was overlooked. Fortune put the mould in Fleming's dish; tenacious teams and years of toil transformed it into a remedy.

1945年10月，弗莱明、弗洛里和钱恩共同获得诺贝尔奖；希特利却遭到忽视。机缘把霉菌送进了弗莱明的培养皿；几支锲而不舍的团队，加上多年的辛劳，把它转化成了良药。

考研英语精读 · No.19

Tulip Mania: The Bubble and the Legend

郁金香狂热：泡沫与传说

In the winter of 1636–37, Dutch merchants and brokers met in taverns to trade contracts for tulip bulbs still in the ground, a speculative business they called windhandel, "trading in the wind". Prices of prized "broken" tulips, with streaked petals, soared.

1636至1637年的冬天，荷兰的商人和经纪人聚在小酒馆里，买卖尚埋在地下的郁金香球茎的合同。这是一门投机生意，他们称之为windhandel，即“风中交易”。名贵的“碎色”郁金香花瓣带有条纹，价格一路飙升。

In early February 1637, at a sale in Haarlem, the hub of the trade, no one would bid. Days later, at an auction in Alkmaar, one bulb still fetched 5,200 guilders, the Dutch currency of the day, more than a decade's wages for a skilled craftsman. But the frenzy was over. Prices collapsed, many buyers defaulted and unsettled contracts piled up. Why the market turned so swiftly is still contested.

1637年2月初，在郁金香交易的中心哈勒姆，一场交易会上无人出价。几天后，在阿尔克马尔的一场拍卖会上，一只球茎仍卖出了5,200荷兰盾（当时荷兰的货币），比一名熟练工匠十多年的工钱还多。但这股狂潮已是强弩之末。价格暴跌，许多买家违约，悬而未决的合同越积越多。市场为何转向得如此迅速，至今仍众说纷纭。

Two centuries later, Charles Mackay, a Scottish journalist, recounted the episode in a book on popular delusions, casting it as national hysteria. In his telling, rich and poor gambled, great merchants were reduced almost to begging and Dutch commerce reeled. His vivid account made tulip mania shorthand for bubbles fuelled by reckless buying.

两个世纪后，苏格兰记者查尔斯·麦凯在一本论述大众错觉的书中讲述了这段往事，把它写成一场举国上下的集体歇斯底里。照他的说法，富人穷人都在豪赌，大商人几乎沦落到乞讨的地步，荷兰的商业大受震荡。他的描述绘声绘色，从此郁金香狂热成了泡沫的代名词，专指由不计后果的抢购吹大的泡沫。

Yet the archives tell a smaller story. Mackay's story relied largely on satirical pamphlets of 1637 that preached against greed. Anne Goldgar, a historian at King's College London, scoured Dutch records and counted only a few hundred traders, mostly merchants and craftsmen. She found no bankruptcy caused by tulips and no measurable harm to the economy. Many buyers were later allowed to settle their contracts by paying a small fraction of the price, and few were ruined.

然而，档案讲述的故事要小得多。麦凯的故事所依据的，大多是1637年的讽刺小册子，这些小册子意在劝诫世人戒除贪婪。伦敦国王学院的历史学家安妮·戈德加遍查荷兰的文献记录，只数出几百名交易者，大多是商人和工匠。她没有找到一桩因郁金香而起的破产，也没有发现荷兰经济受到任何可以衡量的损害。许多买家后来获准只付价格的一小部分，就能了结合同，倾家荡产的人也寥寥无几。

The boom was real but fleeting. What spread, and still circulates nearly four centuries on, was the moral: a sermon drawn from those pamphlets and retold by Mackay as history.

这场繁荣确有其事，却转瞬即逝。真正传开、在近四个世纪后仍在流传的，是其中的寓意——一篇取材于那些小册子的训诫，被麦凯当作历史重新讲了一遍。

考研英语精读 · No.20

The 5,000-Year-Old Murder in the Ice

冰里的五千年凶案

On 19 September 1991 two German hikers on a trek in the Ötztal Alps found a corpse by a glacier. It lay on a ridge at about 3,210 metres, on the border between Austria and Italy. The assumption was that a modern-day climber had died on an alpine slope, since glaciers sometimes yield frozen remains.

1991年9月19日，两名德国徒步者在奥茨塔尔阿尔卑斯山远足时，在一条冰川旁发现了一具尸体。尸体躺在海拔约3,210米的一道山脊上，位于奥地利与意大利交界处。人们起初推测，这是一位现代登山者在高山山坡上遇难，因为冰川有时会让冰冻的遗体重见天日。

Taking it for a recent death, rescuers used a jackhammer that ripped flesh from his left hip. His possessions told another story: leather and grass clothing, a copper axe, a bow and arrows, all prehistoric artefacts. Known as Ötzi, the natural mummy was a man who lived about 5,300 years ago, between 3350 and 3105 BC.

救援人员把它当作新近死亡，动用了风钻，结果撕下了他左侧髋部的皮肉。他的随身物品却说明了另一回事。皮革和草编的衣物、一把铜制斧子、一张弓和几支箭，全是史前器物。这具天然木乃伊后来被称为奥茨，是一名男子，生活在约5,300年前，即公元前3350年至前3105年之间。

In 2001, X-rays revealed a flint arrowhead lodged in his left shoulder, unseen by examiners a decade earlier. A scanner showed that the puncture had ruptured an artery, a mortal injury causing heavy bleeding, and left a large clot. Researchers believe he died within minutes. He was about 46.

2001年，X 光片显示，他左肩里嵌着一枚燧石箭头，十年前的检查人员对此未曾察觉。扫描仪显示，这处穿刺伤撕裂了一条动脉，这是会引起大量出血的致命伤，伤处还留下一大块血块。研究者认为他在几分钟内就死了。他死时约46岁。

Another person, it is thought, killed him. From the wound's depth researchers inferred that an archer shot him from the rear, at about 30 metres. The shooter probably caught him unawares. A 2018 study found he had dined on dried ibex meat and fat, red deer and einkorn wheat, a hearty, nourishing meal reportedly consumed within two hours of his death.

据认为，是另一个人杀死了他。研究者根据伤口的深度推断，一名弓箭手从背后约30米处向他射箭。射手很可能趁他毫无防备时出手。2018年的一项研究发现，他最后一餐享用的是干野山羊肉和脂肪、马鹿肉以及单粒小麦，这是一顿丰盛而富有营养的饭；据报道，他在死前两小时内吃下了这顿饭。

Curators in Bolzano now keep Ötzi, whose 61 tattoos on joints and spine were perhaps an early form of acupuncture. The archer's identity is an enigma, and any motive is conjecture. Ötzi has a name, a last meal and a cause of death, while his anonymous killer has none.

博尔扎诺的博物馆管理员如今保管着奥茨，他身上的61处文身位于关节和脊柱，也许是一种早期的针灸。那名弓箭手的身份是个谜团，任何动机都只是猜测。奥茨有名字，有最后一餐，也有死因，而那个无名的凶手这些都没有。

考研英语精读 · No.21

The Man Who Sold the Eiffel Tower Twice

两次卖掉埃菲尔铁塔的骗子

In 1925 six scrap-metal traders met secretly at the Hôtel de Crillon in Paris. A deputy director-general of the Ministry of Posts and Telegraphs shared the momentous news, confidentially, that the Eiffel Tower was to be sold for scrap. He invited tenders and made each dealer solemnly vow secrecy, ostensibly to spare the public indignation.

1925年，六名经营废金属的商人在巴黎的克里雍酒店秘密会面。邮电部的一位副总长向他们私下透露了一个重大消息，埃菲尔铁塔将作为废铁出售。他请他们投标，并让每位商人郑重地发誓要保密，表面上是为了免得公众愤慨。

The pretext was not absurd. Built for the 1889 World's Fair, the monument had originally been destined to come down after twenty years. The high-ranking deputy was Victor Lustig, a crook whose ministerial stationery was a forgery. He had read a newspaper article in which the authorities lamented the hefty cost of the tower's upkeep.

这个借口并不荒唐。这座为1889年世博会而建的纪念建筑，原本就注定要在二十年后拆除。那位位高权重的副总长正是维克多·卢斯蒂格，一个骗子，他那些部门信笺都是伪造品。他读到过一篇报道，当局在文中抱怨铁塔高昂的维护费用。

André Poisson, a businessman impatient to gain a foothold in Paris, won. Lustig intimated that his modest civil-service remuneration needed supplementing, and Poisson paid an additional bribe. Lustig left for Vienna with the takings.

生意人安德烈·普瓦松迫不及待地想在巴黎站稳脚跟，他中了标。卢斯蒂格暗示，他那份公务员的微薄薪酬需要补贴，普瓦松便另外付了一笔贿赂。卢斯蒂格带着这笔进账去了维也纳。

Fleeced and too ashamed to go to the police, Poisson swallowed the loss quietly. Not a whisper of the deceit appeared in print.

被骗走钱财、又羞于报警的普瓦松，默默吞下了这笔损失。报纸上没有出现关于这场骗局的半点风声。

About a month later Lustig had the gall to reappear with an encore of his script for new dealers. This time the winner grew distrustful and went to the police. The affair made the papers, and Lustig made his getaway. The much-repeated second sale is a fairy tale.

大约一个月后，卢斯蒂格竟厚着脸皮又一次露面，对另一批商人重演他那套剧本。这一次，那位中标者起了疑心，报了警。这桩事件上了报纸，卢斯蒂格逃之夭夭。那个流传甚广的第二次出售，只是一个童话。

Later arrested in the United States for making fake money, Lustig broke out of detention, was recaptured 27 days later and served time on the penal island of Alcatraz. He died of pneumonia in 1947.

后来，卢斯蒂格在美国因制造假冒货币被捕，他从拘留所越狱，27天后又被抓回，在作为监狱岛的恶魔岛服刑。1947年，他死于肺炎。

Of Lustig's two marks, the one who paid kept mum and the one who kept his money went to the police.

卢斯蒂格的两个下手对象中，付了钱的那个闭口不言，没付钱的那个去报了警。

考研英语精读 · No.22

The Building That Melted a Car

会烤化汽车的大楼

On 2 September 2013 the news broke that a London skyscraper under construction had damaged a Jaguar. Martin Lindsay had left the saloon by the pavement on Eastcheap, opposite the tower's frontage. He returned to find its wing mirror warped, its panels deformed and its badge distorted. The glazed facade above was to blame.

2013年9月2日，一则新闻传开，说伦敦一座在建的摩天大楼损坏了一辆捷豹车。马丁·林赛把这辆轿车停在伊斯特奇普街的人行道边，正对着这座高楼的正面。回来时他发现，车的后视镜已经翘曲，车身板变形，车标也扭曲了。罪魁祸首是上方那面镶玻璃的外墙。

Rafael Viñoly curved the south face of 20 Fenchurch Street inward, enlarging the upper storeys, where tenants pay a premium. That geometry acted like a magnifying glass, focusing sunlight into a beam up to six times brighter than direct sun. It was measured at about 110 degrees Celsius, and a reporter fried an egg in a pan using the reflection.

拉斐尔·维诺利把芬丘奇街20号的南立面设计成向内弯曲，扩大了高处的楼层，那里的租户要付溢价。这种几何形状就像一面放大镜，把阳光汇聚成一道光束，亮度可达直射阳光的六倍。测得的温度约为110摄氏度，一名记者还在平底锅里利用反射光煎熟了一个鸡蛋。

The developers issued an apology and reimbursed almost 1,000 pounds for repairs. Parking bays were suspended and a provisional barrier was erected. The beam struck for about two hours a day, two to three weeks annually, because of the sun's angle of elevation. Newspapers dubbed the Walkie-Talkie the Walkie-Scorchie, a taunt.

开发商公开道歉，并赔付了近1,000英镑的修理费。路边停车位暂停使用，还架起了一道临时性的屏障。受这一时节太阳的角度与高度影响，这道光束每年有两到三周、每天约两个小时照到街上。报纸把这座"对讲机"大楼叫作"烤焦机"，这是一句嘲讽。

The architect was candid, admitting many mistakes. Viñoly had anticipated the focusing, he said, but not its intensity. Sun louvres in the original design were reportedly removed in cost-cutting. Nor was this his first architectural miscalculation. In 2010 the infamous glare from his curved, reflective Vdara hotel in Las Vegas burned a guest's scalp by the pool.

这位建筑师很坦率，承认犯了很多错误。他说，他预料到外墙会聚光，却没想到聚光的强度会这么大。据报道，原设计中的遮阳百叶在削减成本时被删掉了。这也不是他第一次出现建筑上的失算。2010年，他设计的拉斯维加斯维达拉酒店外墙弯曲、反光，那臭名昭著的强光在泳池边灼伤了一位客人的头皮。

On 12 February 2014 a permanent fixture was announced: horizontal aluminium fins to diffuse the brightness, reportedly costing several million pounds. In September 2015 Building Design's critics gave the notorious tower the Carbuncle Cup, a trophy for the UK's worst new building. The ballot was unanimous.

2014年2月12日，一项永久性的固定装置方案公布，那就是在外墙加装水平的铝制散光鳍片来散射光线、减弱亮度，据报道花费达数百万英镑。2015年9月，《建筑设计》杂志的评论家们把这座声名狼藉的大楼评为"烂疮杯"得主，这一奖杯颁给英国最糟糕的新建筑。这次投票是全体一致的结果。

考研英语精读 · No.23

The Man Who Gave Machines a Mind

为机器点燃思想的人

Born in June 1912, Alan Turing had a formidable intellect by his early twenties. In a paper read in November 1936, he outlined a "universal machine": a device able to execute any computation, given the right instructions. That abstract construct, rooted in mathematical logic, became the theoretical blueprint of the modern computer.

艾伦·图灵生于1912年6月。二十出头时，他已拥有超群的才智。在1936年11月宣读的一篇论文中，他勾勒了一种"通用机器"：只要给出恰当的指令，这种装置就能执行任何计算。这一抽象的构想植根于数理逻辑，后来成为现代计算机的理论蓝图。

On 4 September 1939 he joined the classified codebreaking effort at Bletchley Park, leading work on naval Enigma, Germany's encryption machine. Polish mathematicians had deciphered Enigma by late 1932 and shared their insights with Britain and France in July 1939. His team's machines exploited them and, operational from March 1940, automated the laborious search for each day's rotor settings.

1939年9月4日，他加入布莱切利园的机密破译工作，主持破解海军恩尼格玛密码机，即德国的加密机器。早在1932年底，波兰数学家就已破译了恩尼格玛，并于1939年7月将他们的破解思路告知英法两国。他的团队研制的机器利用了这些思路，从1940年3月起投入运行，把每天搜寻转子设置这项繁重的工作自动化了。

In October 1950, writing in the philosophy journal Mind, he posed a provocative question: "Can machines think?" He proposed a practical substitute, the imitation game, now widely called the Turing test. An interrogator exchanges typed messages with two concealed players, one human and one machine. If the interrogator cannot consistently distinguish them, the machine passes.

1950年10月，他在哲学期刊《心灵》（Mind）上撰文，提出了一个发人深省的问题——"机器能思考吗？"他建议用一种切实可行的替代办法取而代之，即"模仿游戏"，如今普遍称为"图灵测试"。一名提问者通过打字，与两个隐藏起来的参与者交流，其中一个是人，一个是机器。如果提问者无法始终如一地分辨二者，机器就算通过测试。

His exceptional wartime contribution did not protect him. On 31 March 1952 he was convicted of "gross indecency", the charge then used against gay men. He forfeited his security clearance and, to avert imprisonment, submitted to hormone injections. He died of cyanide poisoning on 7 June 1954. A coroner's inquest ruled it suicide, though some doubt it.

他在战时卓越的贡献并没有护住他。1952年3月31日，他被判定犯有"严重猥亵罪"，这是当时用来惩治男同性恋者的罪名。他失去了安全许可；为了避免入狱，他还接受了激素注射。1954年6月7日，他死于氰化物中毒。验尸官的死因调查裁定为自杀，不过有人对此存疑。

Recognition lagged. On 10 September 2009 Britain's prime minister publicly apologised, and on 24 December 2013 Turing was posthumously granted a royal pardon. On 23 June 2021, his birthday, a fifty-pound note bearing his portrait entered circulation. The country that prosecuted him put his face on its money.

认可来得迟缓。2009年9月10日，英国首相公开道歉；2013年12月24日，图灵在身后获得皇室赦免。2021年6月23日，也就是他的生日，印有他肖像的50英镑钞票开始流通。起诉他的国家，把他的头像印上了自己的钞票。

考研英语精读 · No.24

Survival in the Ice: The Legend of Endurance

冰海求生：坚忍号传奇

On 5 December 1914 Endurance departed South Georgia, a remote whaling outpost, for Antarctica's Weddell Sea. In January 1915 ice trapped the vessel. On 27 October, her hull buckling, the men abandoned ship. She foundered on 21 November; her captain, Frank Worsley, logged the spot.

1914年12月5日，坚忍号驶离南乔治亚岛——一处偏远的捕鲸前哨，开往南极洲的威德尔海。1915年1月，这艘船被冰困住。10月27日，船体开始扭曲变形，众人弃船。11月21日，船沉没；船长弗兰克·沃斯利把沉没地点记入了航海日志。

The stranded men endured over five gruelling months on the ice. Their floe split on 9 April 1916; on 15 April their lifeboats reached Elephant Island, a bleak, uninhabited rock far from shipping lanes.

进退无路的众人在冰上熬过了五个多月艰苦异常的日子。1916年4月9日，他们脚下的浮冰裂开；4月15日，他们的救生艇抵达象岛，那是一座荒凉、无人居住的岩岛，远离航线。

On 24 April Shackleton and five others embarked in the James Caird, a fragile lifeboat, on a perilous voyage of some 1,300 kilometres. As ferocious gales battered the boat, Worsley snatched only four sights of the sun, deducing the rest by dead reckoning from course, speed and time elapsed. On 10 May they landed on South Georgia's deserted south coast.

4月24日，沙克尔顿和另外五人登上单薄脆弱的救生艇詹姆斯·凯尔德号，踏上约1,300公里的凶险航程。狂暴的大风连番冲击着小艇，沃斯利只抓住间隙对太阳做了四次观测（即“测日”，航海上称 take a sight，据此定船位），其余都靠航位推算，根据航向、航速和经过的时间推断出来。5月10日，他们在南乔治亚岛空无一人的南岸登陆。

Shackleton, Worsley and Tom Crean traversed its mountainous interior in about 36 exhausting hours, reaching the Stromness whaling station on 20 May. Ice repelled three rescue attempts before the Yelcho, a Chilean tug, evacuated the 22 marooned men on 30 August. All 28 of Endurance's party survived their ordeal.

沙克尔顿、沃斯利和汤姆·克林用了约36个令人精疲力竭的小时，横越岛上山峦重叠的腹地，于5月20日抵达斯特罗姆内斯捕鲸站。浮冰三次逼退救援船，直到8月30日，智利的拖船耶乔号才把流落荒岛的22人撤出象岛。坚忍号一行28人全部挺过了这场磨难。

On 5 March 2022 searchers located the wreck 3,008 metres down, upright and intact. She lay about four miles south of the spot Worsley recorded in November 1915, months before the voyage: a slight discrepancy for a calculation on shifting ice.

2022年3月5日，搜寻队在3,008米深的海底找到了残骸，船身直立，完好无损。它位于沃斯利1915年11月所记位置以南约四英里处；这个位置是小艇出航前几个月测算的。冰面一直在漂移，在上面计算出的位置只有这么一点偏差，已属难得。

Legend rightly salutes Shackleton for his charisma. Yet both ends of the story hinge on Worsley's meticulous arithmetic. Leadership held 28 men together. Precision put six ashore within reach of help and, a century on, brought their ship back into view.

传说称颂沙克尔顿的超凡魅力，这理所当然。可这个故事的两头，都取决于沃斯利一丝不苟的算术。领导力让28人始终抱成一团；精确则把其中六人送上岸，送到了能求得救援的地方，一个世纪后，又让他们的船重回世人视野。

考研英语精读 · No.25

Apollo 13: The Successful Failure

阿波罗13号：成功的失败

On April 11th 1970 NASA launched Apollo 13, meant for the third crewed lunar landing. On April 13th, some 200,000 miles out, an oxygen tank exploded in the service module, home to most of the ship's oxygen and power. The blast crippled the spacecraft; the landing was aborted.

1970年4月11日，NASA发射了阿波罗13号，此行本应完成人类第三次载人登月。4月13日，在距地球约20万英里处，服务舱段里的一个氧气罐爆炸了，而飞船的氧气和电力大多储存在这个舱段里。这股爆炸气浪重创了飞船，登月只得中止。

The astronauts took refuge in the lunar module, Aquarius, built to support two men for about two days. Using its engine, the crew steered the craft onto a trajectory around the Moon, whose gravity swung it home. They rationed water, conserved power and shivered in a bitterly cold cabin.

宇航员们转到登月舱“水瓶座”号里避难，这艘登月舱原本只够两个人用两天左右。机组借助它的发动机，操纵航天器驶入一条绕月轨迹，再借月球引力把它甩回地球。他们限量用水，节约用电，在刺骨寒冷的舱室里瑟瑟发抖。

A quieter hazard emerged: with a third man aboard, carbon dioxide was accumulating. The command module, the crew's capsule, used square filter cartridges; Aquarius's sockets were round. From plastic bags, cardboard and tape on board, ground engineers devised an adapter, then talked the crew through assembling it. On April 17th the capsule splashed down in the Pacific.

一个不那么显眼的隐患随之浮现：舱里多了一个人，二氧化碳正不断积聚。机组乘坐的太空舱，也就是指令舱，配的是方形过滤筒，而“水瓶座”号的插口却是圆的。地面工程师用船上现有的塑料袋、硬纸板和胶带，设法拼出一个转接器，再一步步指导机组把它组装起来。4月17日，太空舱在太平洋溅落。

Yet the fault had been built in on Earth. A NASA review board traced the explosion to the tank's thermostat switches, designed for the ship's 28-volt supply. In 1965 the spacecraft's builder rewrote the specification to let the heaters run on 65-volt launch-site power; the switches were never upgraded, a mismatch neither that contractor nor NASA detected. Weeks before launch, to drain the stubborn tank, a heater ran about eight hours. The switches probably malfunctioned; unchecked heat scorched the insulation on the tank's wiring; in flight, that insulation ignited.

然而，祸根早在地面上就已埋下。NASA的一个调查委员会把这次爆炸追溯到罐里的恒温开关上，这些开关是按飞船的28伏电源设计的。早在1965年，飞船承建方就改写了规格，允许加热器使用发射场65伏的电源，开关却始终没有升级；这处不匹配，无论是那家承包商还是NASA都没有发现。发射前几周，那个罐里的氧气怎么也排不干净，为了把它排空，一台加热器连续工作了约八个小时。开关很可能因此失灵，失控的高温烧焦了罐内线路的绝缘层；飞行途中，这层绝缘材料起火了。

NASA called Apollo 13 a "successful failure". The paradox holds: a flight undone by a five-year-old engineering oversight was salvaged within four days by ingenuity on the ground.

NASA把阿波罗13号称作“成功的失败”。这个悖论名副其实：一次飞行，毁于五年前的一处工程疏忽，却在四天之内靠地面上的巧思得以挽救。

考研英语精读 · No.26

The Language the Children Invented

孩子们发明的语言

On a rattling bus in Managua, deaf pupils kept up a brisk, voiceless hubbub across the aisle, palms fluttering, wrists flicking. Their teachers could not follow a word.

马那瓜，一辆哐当作响的公共汽车上，聋人学生们隔着过道，轻快地进行着一场无声的热闹交谈，手掌上下翻飞，手腕飞快地舞动。他们的老师一个字也看不懂。

Before the late 1970s Nicaragua had no deaf community. Most deaf people led secluded lives, sharing a homemade repertoire of gestures with parents and siblings. In 1977 a special-education centre opened in Managua's San Judas district with 50 deaf pupils. By 1983 the centre and a vocational school for teenage pupils held more than 400.

20世纪70年代末以前，尼加拉瓜没有聋人群体。大多数聋人过着与世隔绝的生活，只和父母、兄弟姐妹共用一套自家创造的手势词汇。1977年，马那瓜圣胡达斯区开办了一所特殊教育中心，招收了50名聋童。到1983年，这所中心和一所面向青少年的职业学校，已共有400多名聋生。

The curriculum hinged on oral Spanish and lipreading, and teachers signed only the alphabet. Pupils studied lips in vain, and most failed to grasp what a Spanish word was. At recess, in the street and on the buses, they mingled gestures with home signs. A rough jumble ripened rapidly into a coherent language entirely their own.

课程全都围绕西班牙语口语和唇读展开，老师们打手语时只比划字母。学生们盯着嘴唇看，却是徒劳，多数人始终没弄明白西班牙语的"词"是什么。在课间、街头和公共汽车上，他们把各种手势与自家手势混合在一起。一堆粗糙的大杂烩，迅速地成熟为一门连贯的、完全属于他们自己的语言。

The teachers found it unintelligible, and in June 1986 the Ministry of Education brought in Judy Kegl, an American Sign Language linguist from MIT. A 2001 study found that pupils who arrived younger, and later intakes, used grammatical devices more often and more uniformly than their forerunners. Each fresh wave of young signers added complexity.

老师们觉得这种手语无法理解。1986年6月，教育部请来了麻省理工学院的美国手语语言学家朱迪·凯格尔。2001年的一项研究发现，入学年龄更小的学生，以及后来入学的几批新生，使用语法手段比他们的前辈更频繁，也更统一。每一批年幼的手语者加入，都让这门语言增添了复杂性。

In a 2004 study, gesturing Spanish speakers and the earliest signers described something rolling downhill by welding how it moved and where it went into one undivided movement. Later signers dismantled it into discrete segments in linear order, a framework found nowhere in the gestures around them.

在2004年的一项研究中，打手势的西班牙语使用者和最早一批手语者描述东西滚下坡时，都把它怎样运动、往哪里去熔接成一个不可分割的动作。后来的手语者则把它拆解成离散的片段，按线性顺序排列，这种结构在他们周围的手势中无处可见。

Linguists had a rare chance to watch a language grow from infancy to maturity. Its builders were not teachers or experts but deaf playmates in playgrounds, streets and buses.

语言学家难得有机会亲眼看着一门语言从婴儿期长到成熟期。它的建造者不是老师或专家，而是在操场、街头和公共汽车上一起玩耍的聋人玩伴。

考研英语精读 · No.27

Life in the Forbidden Zone

禁区里的生机

Chernobyl is synonymous with disaster, yet it holds a startling amount of wildlife. Early on 26 April 1986, one of its reactors exploded in Soviet Ukraine, dispersing radioactive fallout across Europe. On 27 April, nearby Pripyat was emptied of about 45,000 inhabitants within hours. A zone some 30 kilometres in radius became off-limits, and Belarus fenced off adjoining terrain.

切尔诺贝利是灾难的代名词，这里却生活着数量惊人的野生动物。1986年4月26日凌晨，位于苏联乌克兰的切尔诺贝利核电站有一座反应堆发生爆炸，放射性沉降物随之飘散到欧洲各地。4月27日，附近的普里皮亚季在数小时内撤空，约4.5万名居民全部离开。一片半径约30公里的区域成为禁区；白俄罗斯也把相邻的地带围了起来。

In October 2015, a study in Current Biology combined aerial surveys with footprint counts on the Belarusian side. Elk, roe deer, red deer and wild boar were comparable in density to those in four reserves free of contamination, and wolves were over seven times as numerous. Earlier helicopter counts showed elk, roe deer and boar surging in the post-disaster decade.

2015年10月，《当代生物学》刊发的一项研究在白俄罗斯一侧把空中调查与足迹计数结合起来。结果显示，驼鹿、狍、马鹿和野猪的密度与四个未受污染的保护区相当，而狼的数量是后者的七倍多。更早的直升机计数则显示，灾后十年间，驼鹿、狍和野猪的数量激增。

Later fieldwork bolstered these findings. In April 2016, camera traps snapped wolves, boar and raccoon dogs near the most hazardous spots, with no sign of suppressed numbers. The probable reason is mundane: the absence of agriculture, forestry and hunting.

后来的实地考察佐证了这些发现。2016年4月，相机陷阱在危险最大的地点附近拍到了狼、野猪和貉，没有迹象表明那里的数量被压低。可能的原因很平常：这里没有农耕、伐木和狩猎。

Smaller organisms fared worse in some studies. One, in March 2007, found fewer nesting birds, and fewer species, where radiation was more intense. Another, in March 2009, found pollinators and spiders less plentiful where exposure was greater. A third, in March 2014, found leaves decomposing about 40 per cent less at the most polluted sites, impeding the return of nutrients to the soil.

不过，一些研究发现，较小的生物境况更差。2007年3月的一项研究发现，辐射越强的地方，筑巢繁殖的鸟越少，种类也越少。2009年3月的另一项研究发现，辐射暴露越多的地方，传粉昆虫和蜘蛛越没那么丰富。2014年3月的第三项研究发现，在污染最重的地点，落叶的分解速度慢了约40%，阻碍了养分回归土壤。

Counting heads cannot guarantee the health of individual animals, and how much damage low but continual doses inflict remains contentious, as studies clash over methods. The burgeoning herds may trumpet the dividend of human departure, but the sluggish rot of fallen leaves keeps a quieter ledger of radiation's cost.

清点头数无法保证动物个体的健康；低剂量却持续不断的辐射究竟会造成多大损害，至今仍有争议，各项研究在方法上相互冲突。日益壮大的兽群或许在宣扬人类离去带来的红利，但落叶迟缓地腐烂，正悄悄替辐射记下一笔账。

考研英语精读 · No.28

A Handful of Wormwood That Saved Millions

青蒿一握，济世千万

Project 523, a covert military mission involving many institutes, was named after its launch date, 23 May 1967. Its urgent target was the chloroquine-resistant malaria weakening troops in the Vietnam War. In January 1969 Tu Youyou was entrusted with leading her institute's research group, despite having no doctorate or foreign training.

523项目是一项有多家机构参与的秘密军事任务，以启动日期1967年5月23日命名。它的紧迫目标，是对氯喹产生耐药性的疟疾，这种病削弱了越战部队的战斗力。1969年1月，屠呦呦受命领导所在研究所的研究组，尽管她既没有博士学位，也没有在国外受过训练。

In an exhaustive search, her team combed over 2,000 folk recipes, compiled 640 prescriptions and evaluated about 380 extracts from some 200 medicinal herbs. Qinghao, or sweet wormwood, showed flashes of promise, yet its results were erratic. Seeking a clue, Tu revisited the classical texts.

她的团队展开了彻底的搜寻，翻检了2,000多个民间药方，整理出640个方剂，并评估了约380种提取物，这些提取物来自200种左右的药用草药。青蒿，也就是黄花蒿，不时闪现希望，但效果时好时坏。为了寻找线索，屠呦呦重新翻阅了古典文献。

Around AD 340, the physician Ge Hong's handbook advised taking "a handful of qinghao immersed in two litres of water". This bypassed boiling. Tu suspected heat during extraction was degrading a delicate component, so she switched to ether, a volatile solvent boiling at about 35 degrees Celsius. On 4 October 1971 sample 191 completely inhibited the parasite in infected mice.

约公元340年，医学家葛洪在手册《肘后备急方》中建议：「青蒿一握，以水二升渍」。这个方法绕开了煮沸。屠呦呦怀疑，提取时的高温破坏了某种不耐热的成分，于是她改用乙醚。乙醚是一种易挥发的溶剂，沸点约为35摄氏度。1971年10月4日，第191号样品完全抑制了受感染小鼠体内的疟原虫。

Efficacy in mice indicated nothing about human safety. In July 1972, to assess its toxicity, Tu and two colleagues voluntarily took the extract. Thereafter, from August to October, it was administered to 21 malaria patients in Hainan, and on 8 November her team obtained pure crystals of the substance later named artemisinin.

在小鼠身上有药效，并不能说明对人是否安全。1972年7月，为了检验它的毒性，屠呦呦和两名同事自愿服下了这种提取物。此后，从8月到10月，这种提取物在海南用于治疗21名疟疾病人；11月8日，她的团队得到了这种物质的纯净晶体，这种物质后来被命名为青蒿素。

In September 2011 Tu was named a Lasker Award recipient; its jury credited the drug with saving millions. On 5 October 2015 she won half the Nobel Prize in Physiology or Medicine, the first Chinese woman Nobel laureate. Since 2006 WHO guidelines have endorsed artemisinin-based combination therapies against malaria's deadliest form. The essence of Ge Hong's line lay in the boiling it skipped.

2011年9月，屠呦呦获拉斯克奖，成为该奖得主；该奖的评审团称，这种药挽救了数百万人。2015年10月5日，她分享了诺贝尔生理学或医学奖，独得一半，成为第一位获得诺贝尔奖的中国女性。自2006年起，世界卫生组织的指南一直推荐以青蒿素为基础的联合疗法，用来对付疟疾中最致命的一种。葛洪那句话的精髓，正在于它略去的煮沸。

考研英语精读 · No.29

The Volcano That Gave Us Frankenstein

一座火山，造出了《弗兰肯斯坦》

In June 1816 a ceaseless downpour kept a party besieged at the Villa Diodati, a mansion the poet Lord Byron had leased on the outskirts of Geneva. After reading an anthology of German ghost stories in French translation, Byron proposed that each guest write a supernatural tale.

1816年6月，一场连绵不断的大雨，把一群人围困在迪奥达蒂别墅里。这座宅邸位于日内瓦郊外，是诗人拜伦勋爵租下的。读完一本德国鬼故事的选集的法文译本之后，拜伦提议每位客人各写一个超自然故事。

The melancholy weather was attributable to a distant blast. On 10 April 1815 Mount Tambora, on the Indonesian island of Sumbawa, blew itself apart in one of the most potent eruptions in recorded history. About 10,000 people died immediately, and tens of thousands more of starvation and disease in the aftermath.

这种阴郁的天气，源于远方的一次爆炸。1815年4月10日，印度尼西亚松巴哇岛上的坦博拉火山炸裂开来，那是有记录以来最猛烈的喷发之一。约1万人当场死亡，在随后的余波中，又有数万人死于饥饿和疾病。

The volcano pumped prodigious quantities of sulphur dioxide from its crater into the stratosphere, where it formed sulphate aerosols that blanketed the globe, reflecting sunshine and thereby lowering temperatures world-wide. Europe and North America came to know 1816 as the Year Without a Summer.

这座火山从火山口向平流层泵入了数量惊人的二氧化硫，它们在那里形成硫酸盐气溶胶，像毯子一样笼罩全球，反射阳光，从而使全世界的气温降低。欧洲和北美后来把1816年称作无夏之年。

Europe's summer was chilly and relentlessly wet. Crops failed or lay rotten in saturated fields, cereal and bread prices escalated, and scarcity, disease and unrest followed.

欧洲的夏天寒冷，雨也下个不停。庄稼要么歉收，要么腐烂在浸透了水的田地里，谷物和面包价格飞涨，接着出现了短缺、疾病和动荡。

The eighteen-year-old Mary Godwin, later Mary Shelley, had set out in May alongside Percy Bysshe Shelley and Claire Clairmont. In her 1831 preface she would recollect a damp, gloomy summer whose torrents often kept the group indoors for days.

十八岁的玛丽·戈德温，也就是后来的玛丽·雪莱，5月间与珀西·比希·雪莱和克莱尔·克莱蒙特结伴启程。在1831年的序言里，她回忆说，那是一个潮湿、阴沉的夏天，倾盆大雨常常把大家困在屋里好几天。

Byron's challenge proved fruitful. His physician John Polidori produced The Vampyre, whilst Byron's poetic output included Darkness. Mary began the ghastly yarn that became Frankenstein, which appeared anonymously in 1818 and was later reissued under her name. It is often called the founding work of the science-fiction genre.

拜伦的挑战成果丰硕。他的医生约翰·波利多里写出了《吸血鬼》，与此同时，拜伦诗歌方面的产出中有《黑暗》。玛丽开始写那个恐怖的故事，它后来成了《弗兰肯斯坦》，1818年匿名出版，后来又以她的名字再版。这部作品常被称为科幻体裁的开山之作。

A tropical volcano had supplied the rain, and a teenager the monster.

一座热带火山提供了雨，一个少女提供了怪物。

考研英语精读 · No.30

The Stink That Built London's Sewers

大恶臭逼出了下水道

In the fierce heat of summer 1858, the curtains of the new Houses of Parliament hung heavy with chloride of lime, a bleach to make the air tolerable. The Thames festered, and MPs contemplated moving out.

1858年夏天酷热难当，新落成的议会大厦里，窗帘上浸透了氯化石灰。这是一种漂白剂，用来让空气变得可以忍受。泰晤士河正在溃烂发臭，议员们考虑过搬离这里。

The river had become London's lavatory. Antiquated sewers and outlets discharged the untreated sewage of some three million city dwellers into its currents, where the excrement stewed and decayed. The vile fumes grew so overpowering that the episode earned a name, the Great Stink.

这条河已经成了伦敦的厕所。老旧的下水道和排污口向河水的水流中排放约三百万城市居民产生的未经处理的污水，粪便在河里闷沤、腐败。污浊的臭气变得如此令人难以招架，这段日子由此得名大恶臭。

Disgust found a voice on 15 July, when the Chancellor of the Exchequer, Benjamin Disraeli, denounced the river as a hellish pool of unbearable horrors. A bill funding new sewers was fast-tracked into law in about 18 days.

7月15日，这股厌恶有了代言人。财政大臣本杰明·迪斯雷利痛斥这条河是一潭地狱般的池水，满是令人无法忍受的恐怖。一项为新下水道拨款的法案被快速推进，约18天就成了法律。

The engineer Joseph Bazalgette masterminded the response. His 82 miles of stout intercepting sewers caught the flow short of the river and diverted it east, downstream of the city. With some 1,100 miles of street sewers as tributaries, the infrastructure took 318 million bricks. Having calculated an adequate calibre for each pipe, he doubled it. The backbone lies buried underneath the newly built Victoria, Albert and Chelsea Embankments. Substantially complete by 1875, much of the network is in use today.

工程师约瑟夫·巴泽尔杰特主持策划了这次应对。他修建的82英里坚固截流下水道，在污水入河之前把它截住，改道向东，送往城市下游。约1,100英里的街道下水道作为支流汇入其中，整套基础设施用了三亿一千八百万块砖块。他先算出每条管道足够的管径，再把它加倍。系统的主干就埋藏在新近修成的维多利亚、阿尔伯特和切尔西河堤之下。到1875年，这套管网已基本完工，其中大部分至今仍在使用。

Yet that sanitation rested on a fallacy. The predominant belief, miasma, blamed disease on foul air and vapours, so the sewers were intended principally to suppress the smell. They worked for an unintended reason, guarding the purity of the water Londoners drank.

然而，这套卫生设施建立在一个谬误之上。当时占主导地位的看法是瘴气说，认为疾病源于污浊的空气和秽气，所以修下水道主要是为了压住臭味。它们奏效，却是出于一个意料之外的原因，那就是守住了伦敦人饮用水的洁净。

Cholera resurfaced in 1866 and killed about 5,600 people in the East End, the one locality not yet connected.

1866年，霍乱卷土重来，在伦敦东区夺去约5,600人的生命，那是唯一尚未接入的地区。

A remedy aimed at the nose protected the gut.

一项冲着鼻子去的补救措施，护住的却是肠胃。

考研英语精读 · No.31

The Book Twelve Publishers Turned Down

被拒绝了十二次的哈利·波特

In 1990, on a delayed train from Manchester to London's King's Cross, Joanne Rowling, a would-be novelist, was unexpectedly struck by the inspiration for Harry Potter, an orphaned young wizard. Grief soon overshadowed that spark: her mother died on 30 December 1990, aged 45. Rowling then relocated to Porto to teach English.

1990年，在一列从曼彻斯特开往伦敦国王十字车站的晚点列车上，有志成为小说家的乔安妮·罗琳出乎意料地得到了哈利·波特的灵感。哈利是一个父母双亡的少年巫师。很快，悲痛盖过了那点火花。她的母亲于1990年12月30日去世，终年45岁。随后，罗琳移居波尔图，教英语。

Rowling married in Porto in October 1992, and a daughter's arrival in July 1993 ushered in motherhood. At the end of 1993 she resettled in Edinburgh, a lone mother reliant on state welfare. She habitually wrote in cafés, patiently polishing her unfinished novel.

1992年10月，罗琳在波尔图结婚；1993年7月，女儿的降生开启了她为人母的生活。1993年底，她在爱丁堡重新安家，成了一位独自带孩子、依靠国家福利生活的母亲。她习惯在咖啡馆里写作，耐心地打磨那部尚未完成的小说。

A literary agent, Christopher Little, took on the manuscript and pitched it repeatedly to prospective buyers. It was reportedly rejected by twelve publishers in succession, a relentless run of refusals that offered the newcomer little encouragement.

文学经纪人克里斯托弗·利特尔接下了这部书稿，并多次向潜在买家推荐。据报道，书稿接连遭到十二家出版社拒绝；这一连串毫不留情的回绝，没给这位新人多少鼓励。

At Bloomsbury, the chairman Nigel Newton gave the first chapter to his eight-year-old daughter, Alice, who relished it and eagerly asked for the next instalment. The firm paid a sum of 1,500 pounds as an advance. Fearful that boys might disregard a book by a woman, the publishers opted for initials to mask her gender. Having no middle name, Rowling took the K for her pseudonym from her grandmother Kathleen.

在布卢姆斯伯里出版社，董事长奈杰尔·牛顿把第一章交给八岁的女儿爱丽丝。爱丽丝读得津津有味，还急切地要看下一部分。出版社支付了1,500英镑的款项作为预付金。出版方担心男孩们可能会忽视女性写的书，选择用名字缩写隐去她的性别。罗琳没有中间名，便从祖母凯瑟琳的名字里取了 K 作为笔名的一部分。

Harry Potter and the Philosopher's Stone was published on 26 June 1997, in a cautious first print run of 500 hardbacks, 300 allocated to libraries. The American publisher Scholastic paid $105,000 for the US rights, and the renamed Sorcerer's Stone appeared there on 1 September 1998. The series and its spin-off volumes have collectively surpassed 500 million copies sold, in 80 languages. Alice's appetite was evidently shared by millions.

《哈利·波特与魔法石》于1997年6月26日出版，首印谨慎地定为500册精装本，其中300册分配给图书馆。美国的学乐出版社以10.5万美元买下美国版权，这本书改名为 Sorcerer's Stone，于1998年9月1日在美国出版。此后，这个系列及其衍生图书的销量合计已超过5亿册，涉及80种语言。显然，千百万读者都有着和爱丽丝一样的胃口。

考研英语精读 · No.32

The Planet That Was Demoted

被降级的冥王星

On 18 February 1930, comparing photographic plates at the Lowell Observatory, the astronomer Clyde Tombaugh perceived a dim dot that shifted between them. An eleven-year-old English girl put forward the name, after the Roman deity of the underworld. For 76 years Pluto held the ninth slot on the planetary roster.

1930年2月18日，天文学家克莱德·汤博在洛厄尔天文台比对照相底片时，察觉到一个在底片之间移动过的暗淡小点。一名11岁的英国女孩提议了它的名字，取自罗马神话中的冥界神祇。此后76年里，冥王星一直占据行星名单上的第九个位置。

In January 2005, astronomers found a far-off body, later named Eris, at first thought larger than Pluto. NASA briefly called it the tenth planet. The contender posed an awkward dilemma. The term "planet" had hitherto been used loosely, and with more icy worlds likely to be found, a precise definition became a necessity.

2005年1月，天文学家发现了一个遥远的天体，它后来被命名为厄里斯，起初看上去比冥王星还大。美国航天局一度称它为第十大行星。这个竞争者带来了一个棘手的难题。"行星"一词此前一直用得很宽泛，而且很可能还会发现更多冰冷的天体，一个精确的定义已势在必行。

On 24 August 2006 the International Astronomical Union, convened in Prague, voted that a planet must revolve around the Sun, be nearly spherical and have cleared the neighbourhood around its orbit. Pluto failed that last criterion and was reclassified as a dwarf planet, leaving eight. Pluto was physically unaltered; only the boundary had moved.

2006年8月24日，在布拉格召开大会的国际天文学联合会投票决定，行星必须环绕太阳运行、接近球形，并且已经清空其轨道附近的区域。冥王星不符合最后一条标准，被重新归类为矮行星，行星只剩下八颗。冥王星在物理上毫无改变，移动的只是界线。

The vote was valid, yet its legitimacy drew criticism: only 424 of some 10,000 members took part. Hundreds of dissenting astronomers signed a petition. The American Dialect Society made "plutoed", playful slang for "to devalue", its word of 2006, and New Mexico and Illinois lawmakers passed resolutions affirming that Pluto was a planet.

这次表决有效，但其正当性受到批评，因为约1万名会员中只有424人参加了投票。数百名持异议的天文学家签署了请愿书。美国方言学会把"plutoed"评为2006年度词汇，这是一个带有戏谑意味的俚语，意思是"贬低"。新墨西哥州和伊利诺伊州的立法者通过决议，确认冥王星是行星。

Amid the controversy, an irony was already in transit. New Horizons was launched on 19 January 2006, seven months before the vote, carrying a pinch of Tombaugh's ashes. On 14 July 2015 the unmanned craft flew past Pluto, transmitting images of an enormous heart-shaped plain, fittingly named Tombaugh Regio. The discoverer's ashes swept past the heart that bears his name.

就在一片争议声中，一件颇具讽刺意味的事早已在途中。新视野号于2006年1月19日发射，比那次表决早七个月，携带着一小撮汤博的骨灰。2015年7月14日，这艘无人飞船飞掠冥王星，传回了一片巨大的心形平原的图像，这片平原恰如其分地被命名为汤博区。这位发现者的骨灰，掠过了那颗以他命名的"心"。

考研英语精读 · No.33

Thirteen in the Cave

洞穴里的十三个人

On 23 June 2018, twelve boys aged 11 to 16 from a junior football squad went on an outing with their 25-year-old assistant coach. They entered Tham Luang, a sprawling maze of tunnels in northern Thailand, to penetrate its gloom. Monsoon rain then flooded the passages, and the rising water trapped all of them.

2018年6月23日，一支青少年足球队伍的12名11至16岁男孩，和25岁的助理教练一起出游。他们进入泰国北部的睡美人洞，那是一座由隧道构成、纵横延伸的迷宫，他们想要深入洞中的幽暗。随后，季风带来的雨水淹没了通道，上涨的水把他们全部困在里面。

For nine days their whereabouts were unknown. On 2 July two elite British divers, threading through submerged passages, reached an inundated chamber about 4 km from the entrance. All 13 were there, huddled on a muddy mound and perched just above the water, every one of them alive.

九天里，他们下落不明。7月2日，两名英国精英潜水员穿过水下通道，到达距洞口约4公里的一个被淹洞室。13人都在那里，蜷缩在一处泥泞的土堆上，栖身于水面之上，一个不少，全都活着。

The only exit ran through kilometres of constricted, treacherous passages, many of them navigable only with scuba gear. Rescuers began stockpiling oxygen cylinders along the route. On 6 July Saman Kunan, a former Thai Navy SEAL, fell unconscious while laying them and died.

唯一的出口要穿过数公里狭窄而凶险的通道，其中许多段只有借助水肺装备才能通行。救援人员开始沿途储备氧气钢瓶。7月6日，前泰国海军海豹突击队员萨曼·古南在布放钢瓶时失去知觉，随后死亡。

The evacuation began on 8 July, and the boys came out in batches. Each was given doses of sedative, wrapped motionless in a stretcher and passed from diver to diver, with medics stationed along the route. On 10 July the last four footballers and their coach were escorted out after their teammates.

7月8日，撤离开始，男孩们分批出洞。每人都用了数剂镇静药，一动不动地包裹在担架里，由潜水员一个接一个传递出去，沿途有医护人员驻守。7月10日，最后四名小球员和教练继队友之后被护送出洞。

The multinational mobilisation was a feat of logistics. Some 10,000 people converged on the site, including a contingent of more than 100 divers, plus 2,000 soldiers and 900 policemen, with teams deployed from abroad. The rescue had a sobering postscript: in December 2019 another Navy SEAL died of a blood infection caught during the operation. Every boy came home alive, and two rescuers were mourned.

这场多国动员堪称后勤壮举。约1万人汇集到现场，其中包括一支100多名潜水员组成的分队，另有2,000名士兵和900名警察，还有从国外调派来的救援小组。2019年12月，又一名海军海豹突击队员死于在救援中染上的血液感染，为这次救援留下一个令人沉重的尾声。每个男孩都活着回到了家，人们则为两名救援者哀悼。
