考研英语精读 · No.34

The Game That Escaped the Soviet Union

越过铁幕的俄罗斯方块

In June 1984 Alexey Pajitnov, a 29-year-old Moscow coder, grew engrossed in a diversion he had built. His primitive Elektronika 60 had a purely textual display, so every geometric piece of four tiles was assembled from characters. The result proved addictive. He called it Tetris, a fusion of the Greek tetra- and tennis, his favourite pastime.

1984年6月，29岁的莫斯科程序员阿列克谢·帕基特诺夫，沉迷于自己编写的一个消遣。他那台简陋的 Elektronika 60 只能显示文字，所以每个由四个小格组成的几何方块，全是用字符拼出来的。结果，这个游戏让人上瘾。他给它取名 Tetris，这是希腊语前缀 tetra- 和网球 tennis 的融合，网球是他最爱的消闲运动。

Under the communist system, the state, not its salaried designer, was the proprietor of this intellectual property, and Pajitnov held no entitlement to it. Copies mushroomed freely on floppy disks across the Eastern bloc.

在共产主义体制下，这份知识产权的所有者是国家，而不是领工资的设计者，帕基特诺夫对它没有任何权利。拷贝存在软盘上，在整个东欧集团内自由流传，遍地开花。

In 1986 Robert Stein, an enterprising British software vendor, stumbled upon Tetris in Hungary. In his ignorance of Elorg, the state bureau regulating Soviet software exports, he hastily hawked rights he did not yet legitimately hold. The rights were shuffled onward through a chaotic muddle of overlapping deals among Western firms, one owned by the magnate Robert Maxwell.

1986年，富有进取心的英国软件商人罗伯特·斯坦因在匈牙利偶然碰到了俄罗斯方块。他对 Elorg 这个管理苏联软件出口的国家机构毫不知情，就草率地兜售起他尚未合法拥有的版权。这些版权在西方公司之间被倒来倒去，一路传下去，陷入一团混乱的乱麻，交易彼此重叠，其中一家公司归大亨罗伯特·马克斯韦尔所有。

In February 1989 Henk Rogers, a resourceful Dutch-born publisher, reached Moscow on a tourist visa and found his way to Elorg. Stein and Kevin Maxwell, the magnate's son, were in Moscow that same hectic week of negotiations, each a bidder. Pajitnov felt a fondness for Rogers, and Nintendo scooped the handheld rights, then worldwide console rights.

1989年2月，足智多谋的荷兰裔发行商亨克·罗杰斯持旅游签证来到莫斯科，设法找到了 Elorg。斯坦因和那位大亨的儿子凯文·马克斯韦尔，在同一个忙乱的谈判周里也在莫斯科，各自都是竞标者。帕基特诺夫对罗杰斯颇有好感，任天堂拿下了掌机版权，后来又拿下了全球游戏主机版权。

Nintendo bundled Tetris with its pocket-sized Game Boy, and that version shipped over 35 million copies. Pajitnov reaped none of the spoils for years. His earnings began only after his arrangement's expiry around 1995. In 1996 he and Rogers founded The Tetris Company to manage the rights.

任天堂把俄罗斯方块和它那款口袋大小的 Game Boy 捆绑销售，这个版本出货超过3,500万份。多年来，帕基特诺夫没有分到任何好处。直到1995年前后他的协议到期之后，他才开始有收入。1996年，他和罗杰斯成立了俄罗斯方块公司，来管理这些版权。

The man whose puzzle once spread for nothing later helped decide who could sell it.

那个游戏曾免费四处流传的人，后来参与决定谁能卖它。

考研英语精读 · No.35

The Bird That Beat the Letter Home

比信先到家的鸟

On 3 June 1952 Rosario Mazzeo released a Manx shearwater in Boston. It came by rail and airliner from its burrow on Skokholm, an isle off Wales, and Mazzeo posted a letter at once. After a roughly 3,200-mile odyssey across the unbroken Atlantic, the feathered traveller was back on its doorstep twelve and a half days later. The letter arrived afterwards.

1952年6月3日，罗萨里奥·马泽奥在波士顿放飞了一只大西洋鹱。它来自威尔士外海斯科克霍姆这座小岛上的洞巢，先坐火车，再搭客机，一路被带到这里。马泽奥随即寄出一封信。这位披着羽毛的旅行者飞越约三千二百英里一望无际的大西洋，完成了一场远征，十二天半后回到自家门口。那封信是随后才到的。

The first hints of a compass were celestial. In the 1950s Kramer kept starlings cooped in cages and moved the apparent sun with mirrors. The duped birds swivelled to match, allowing for the sun's daily arc. In the 1960s Emlen curtained off stars in a planetarium's vault. His buntings steered by stars around the pole-star, the axis of the nightly whirl.

最初的罗盘线索来自天上。20世纪50年代，克雷默把椋鸟关进笼子里，再用镜子挪动太阳的视位置。受骗的鸟随之转身，而且会顾及太阳每天划过的弧线。20世纪60年代，埃姆伦在天文馆的穹顶上把星星一片片遮住。他的靛蓝彩鹀依靠北极星周围的星星辨向，北极星正是星空每夜都绕着旋转的轴心。

In 1972 the Wiltschkos showed that robins read Earth's magnetic field by inclination, not polarity, unlike a sailor's needle. In 1978 Schulten proposed a light-triggered reaction to decode the field. Proposed in 2000, cryptochrome, a protein in the retina, is now the foremost suspect. In 2021 robin cryptochrome proved more finely tuned in the laboratory than that of chickens or pigeons. Proof in a living bird is awaited.

1972年，维尔奇科夫妇证明，知更鸟读取地球磁场时，看的是倾角，而不是极性，这一点和水手的罗盘指针不同。1978年，舒尔滕提出，一种由光触发的化学反应可以解读磁场。2000年，视网膜中的一种蛋白质隐花色素被提了出来，如今它是头号嫌疑对象。2021年的实验室测试表明，知更鸟的隐花色素比鸡和鸽子的调校得更精细。在活鸟身上的证据，仍有待取得。

In 2014 came an eerie, man-made twist. At Oldenburg University, robins in campus huts lost that sense. In huts clad in grounded aluminium, they reclaimed their bearings. Removing the grounding, or piping radio noise in, bewildered them again in double-blind tests. The interference, a murmur from everyday appliances, sat far below WHO safety limits.

2014年，事情出现了一个诡异的、人为的转折。在奥尔登堡大学，校园小木屋里的知更鸟失去了这种感知。在包覆接地铝板的小屋里，它们找回了方向感。在双盲实验中，拆掉接地，或往屋里灌入无线电噪声，又让它们晕头转向。这种干扰只是日常电器发出的一阵电磁低语，强度远低于世卫组织的安全限值。

How a wanderer knows where it is remains unanswered. The compass code is partly cracked, but the atlas that steered the shearwater has yet to be read.

一位漫游者如何知道自己身在何处，至今仍没有答案。罗盘的密码已破解了一部分，但指引那只大西洋鹱的那张地图，还没有人读懂。

考研英语精读 · No.36

The Company That Invented Its Own Killer

发明了数码相机，却被它打败

In 1975 Kodak engineer Steven Sasson built the first digital camera. The toaster-sized gadget, a cumbersome heavyweight of about eight pounds, was hardly portable, let alone handheld. Its first rudimentary picture held 10,000 black-and-white pixels. Recording it on a cassette took 23 seconds, and a playback unit showed it on a television.

1975年，柯达工程师史蒂文·萨松造出了第一台数码相机。这台烤面包机大小的装置重约八磅，是个笨重的大块头，很难说是便携的，更谈不上手持。它拍下的第一张粗糙照片，只有1万个黑白像素。把照片录到盒式磁带上要花23秒，再由一台回放装置在电视上显示出来。

Kodak's revenues showed its dependence on film, the profitable pillar of its business. Technical staffers were appreciative of the camera, but it needed no film. By Sasson's recollection, his superiors at headquarters called it cute and urged him to keep it hushed. A patent followed in 1978. Production never did.

柯达的收入显示出公司对胶卷的依赖，胶卷是其业务中赚钱的支柱。技术员工对这台相机颇为赞赏，但它不需要胶卷。据萨松回忆，他在总部的上司说它挺可爱，又让他对此秘而不宣。1978年，这台相机获得专利，却始终没有投产。

The advent of digital photography proved disruptive. In June 2009 Kodak said it would stop making Kodachrome, its analogue film, citing lessened demand in the digital era. Kodak's manpower had reached an apex of about 145,000 in the 1980s. By 2012, layoffs since 2003 had slashed 47,000 jobs, alongside the shutdown of 13 factories and some 130 labs.

数码摄影的问世带来了颠覆性的冲击。2009年6月，柯达宣布停产 Kodachrome 这种模拟胶卷，理由是数码时代里需求减少。20世纪80年代，柯达的员工规模曾达到约14.5万人的顶峰。到2012年，自2003年以来的裁员已砍掉4.7万个岗位，同时13家工厂和约130个冲印室相继关闭。

On 19 January 2012 the 131-year-old firm sought Chapter 11 protection from its creditors, sapped by pension costs and years of slowness in going digital. Citigroup, a lender, provided $950 million for its restructuring. A portfolio of 1,100 digital imaging patents fetched about $525 million from a consortium of twelve buyers, including the giants Apple and Google.

2012年1月19日，这家已有131年历史的公司在养老金负担和多年来转向数码的迟缓的拖累下，申请《破产法》第11章保护，以免受债权人追讨。贷款方花旗集团为其重组提供了9.5亿美元。由1,100项数字成像专利构成的组合以约5.25亿美元卖给一个由十二家买家组成的财团，其中包括苹果和谷歌两大巨头。

After 19 months of reorganisation, the once-mighty Kodak left bankruptcy in September 2013, downsized, streamlined and refocused as a commercial printer for business clients. In November 2009 President Obama had decorated Sasson with national laurels, commending his digital camera. That medal's year also saw Kodak announce the end of Kodachrome.

经过19个月的改组，昔日强大的柯达于2013年9月走出破产，规模已缩小，业务经过精简，重新定位为面向企业客户的商业印刷商。2009年11月，奥巴马总统曾以国家级荣誉来嘉奖萨松，授予他国家技术与创新奖章，表彰他的数码相机。颁发这枚奖章的那一年，柯达也宣布了 Kodachrome 的停产。

考研英语精读 · No.37

Dynamite for the Heart

炸药大王的救心药

Around 1847 the Italian chemist Sobrero first made nitroglycerin, a colourless, oily liquid of acute instability. A mere droplet on the tongue, he reported, left his head throbbing violently for hours. On 3 September 1864 a nitroglycerin shed in Stockholm exploded. The tragic blast killed five, including Alfred Nobel's younger brother Emil.

1847年前后，意大利化学家索布雷罗首次制得硝酸甘油。这是一种无色、油状的液体，具有极度的不稳定性。他报告说，仅仅一小滴放在舌头上，就让他头部剧烈地抽痛了好几个小时。1864年9月3日，斯德哥尔摩的一间硝酸甘油棚屋发生爆炸。这场悲惨的爆炸造成五人死亡，其中包括阿尔弗雷德·诺贝尔的弟弟埃米尔。

Nobel tamed the liquid by blending it with kieselguhr, a porous earth, into a paste shaped into rods. Patented in 1867 as dynamite, it was harnessed for mining, quarrying and demolition, enriching him immensely. His 1895 testament made the fortune an endowment for prizes, first awarded in 1901.

诺贝尔把这种液体与硅藻土这种多孔的土混合，制成糊状物，再做成一根根棒条，从而驯服了它。1867年，它作为炸药获得专利，被用于采矿、采石和拆除工程，让他获利十分丰厚。他在1895年立下的遗嘱把这笔财产变成设立奖项的基金，奖项于1901年首次颁发。

Nitroglycerin had side-effects. Factory operatives suffered racking headaches, and their tolerance, built over the week, ebbed at weekends. Mondays brought a recurrence, with dizziness and palpitations, hence the name Monday disease. In 1879 William Murrell reported in The Lancet on its therapeutic use against angina pectoris, attacks of chest pain.

硝酸甘油有副作用。工厂工人饱受折磨人的头痛，他们的耐受性在一周中逐渐建立，到周末又减退。每逢星期一，头痛便复发，还伴有眩晕和心悸，因此得名"星期一病"。1879年，威廉·默雷尔在《柳叶刀》上报告了它对心绞痛的治疗用途，心绞痛即阵发性的胸痛。

On 25 October 1896 Nobel, who had angina, wrote to his aide that by an irony of fate doctors had prescribed him nitroglycerin to take internally. Its alias, Trinitrin, was chosen, he explained, to avoid frightening pharmacists or the public. He did not comply. Partly paralysed by a cerebral haemorrhage, he died at his San Remo villa on 10 December 1896.

1896年10月25日，患有心绞痛的诺贝尔写信给他的助手，说命运真是讽刺，医生竟给他开了硝酸甘油让他内服。他解释说，这种药的别名Trinitrin是特意选的，为的是避免吓到那些药剂师和公众。他没有遵从医嘱。他因脑部出血，身体部分出现瘫痪，于1896年12月10日在圣雷莫的别墅中去世。

In 1977 Murad ascertained that nitroglycerin releases nitric oxide, which widens blood vessels. Furchgott found an unidentified relaxing factor in vascular linings in 1980, and Ignarro recognised it as nitric oxide in 1986. The trio shared the 1998 Nobel Prize in Physiology or Medicine. Its presentation speech, on 10 December, the anniversary of Nobel's death, quoted his ironic letter.

1977年，穆拉德查明硝酸甘油会释放一氧化氮，使血管扩张。1980年，弗奇戈特在血管的内壁中发现了一种未经鉴定的舒张因子；1986年，伊格纳罗认定它就是一氧化氮。这个三人组分享了1998年诺贝尔生理学或医学奖。12月10日，即诺贝尔的忌日，颁奖致辞引述了他那封感叹命运讽刺的信。

考研英语精读 · No.38

The Web Beneath the Woods

林下之网

One of ecology's most romantic ideas grew out of plastic bags. In a Canadian forest, researchers enclosed paper birch and Douglas fir seedlings in them, then bathed each species in carbon dioxide laced with a different tracer. Carbon shuttled both ways, apparently via fungi linking the trees' roots, yet the firs ran a slight surplus, larger in deeper shade.

生态学中最浪漫的观念之一，萌生于塑料袋之中。研究人员在加拿大的一片森林里，把纸桦和花旗松的幼苗罩进袋里，再让两种幼苗分别沐浴在掺有不同示踪物的二氧化碳中。碳在两种树之间双向穿梭，似乎是经由连接两者根系的真菌；不过，花旗松最终出现了碳的小幅盈余，而且所受遮荫越重，盈余越大。

Most terrestrial plants harbour such fungi, whose filaments forage for water and nutrients including nitrogen, swapping them for plant sugars. One fungus can knit plants into a shared network. The journal Nature unveiled the study on 7 August 1997, its cover offering a catchy coinage: the "wood-wide web".

大多数陆生植物身上都栖居着这类真菌，它们的菌丝四处搜寻水分和氮等养分，再拿去换取植物的糖分。单个真菌就能把多株植物编织进同一张共享网络。1997年8月7日，《自然》杂志推出了这项研究，当期封面还送上一个响亮好记的新造词——“林木万维网”（wood-wide web）。

The metaphor proved irresistible. Between 2015 and 2021, a forester's bestseller, an acclaimed novel and an ecologist's memoir told readers that trees converse, warn one another and, as "mother trees", nurture their kin. Yet the 1997 trees were seedlings, not elderly mothers.

结果，这个比喻令人难以抗拒。2015年至2021年间，一位护林人的畅销书、一部广受赞誉的小说和一位生态学家的回忆录都向读者讲述，树木会交谈，会互相示警，还会作为“母树”照料自己的亲族。然而，1997年实验里的树都还是幼苗，并非年迈的母亲。

In February 2023, three researchers combed through the field evidence. They judged it too sparse, inconsistent or ambiguous to demonstrate that shared networks are widespread. In 26 field studies, networks helped and hindered seedlings about equally often, with neutral effects predominating. No peer-reviewed field study showed mature trees favouring their own offspring. Unsupported assertions in later papers had doubled over 25 years, with citations skewed towards positive results.

2023年2月，三位研究者梳理了野外证据。他们认为，这些证据或过于稀少，或互不一致，或含糊不清，不足以证明共享网络普遍存在。在26项野外研究中，网络对幼苗有所助益和有所妨碍的情形大致一样多，而以中性效应居多。没有任何一项经同行评审的野外研究表明，成年树木会偏爱自己的后代。在后来的论文中，缺乏依据的论断在25年间增加了一倍，引用也偏向正面结果。

None of this disproves the networks' existence. In January 2025, defenders faulted the critique's methods. The critics concede that carbon and nutrients can migrate between trees underground. It is evidence unearthed in real forests, not the allure of a family saga, that must settle how much the networks convey, and whether it matters.

这一切都不能否定这些网络的存在。2025年1月，共享网络说的拥护者指摘了这篇批评文章的方法。批评者也承认，碳和养分可以经由地下在树与树之间迁移。网络究竟输送了多少、这又是否要紧，必须由在真实森林中发掘出的证据来裁定，而不能凭一部家族传奇的魅力来决定。

考研英语精读 · No.39

Y2K: The Crisis That Success Erased

千年虫：被成功抹去的危机

The Y2K bug, or millennium bug, began as a shortcut. When memory and hardware were costly, programmers stored years as two digits, writing "99" for 1999. That compact code hid an ambiguity: a machine might read "00" as 1900, corrupting calculations of interest, ages or deadlines.

千年虫（Y2K 漏洞），又称"千年虫"，起初只是一条捷径。当年内存和硬件都很昂贵，程序员便只用两位数字记录年份，把1999年写成"99"。这种简洁紧凑的代码暗藏着一种歧义：机器可能把"00"读成1900年，从而搞错利息、年龄或截止日期的计算。

Governments and firms braced for chaos. In February 1998 President Clinton created a council to coordinate America's effort. Consultants audited legacy systems and patched faulty date logic. In November 1999 the Commerce Department estimated American governments and enterprises would spend about $100bn; projections of the global bill ran to hundreds of billions.

各国政府和企业都在严阵以待，准备迎接一场混乱。1998年2月，美国总统克林顿设立了一个委员会来协调全美的应对工作。顾问们审查了遗留系统，并给有缺陷的日期处理逻辑打上补丁。1999年11月，据美国商务部估算，美国各级政府和企业将为此花费约1,000亿美元；对全球总账单的各种预测则高达数千亿美元。

Then midnight struck on 31 December 1999, and little went wrong. Glitches that did crop up were sporadic and mostly minor. Radiation-monitoring equipment faltered at Japan's Shika nuclear plant without any threat to safety, and a Pentagon ground system that processes spy-satellite data failed. In February 2000 a Senate committee concluded that the expenditure had been justified.

随后，1999年12月31日的午夜钟声敲响，几乎没出什么问题。确实冒出来的故障也是零星的，而且大多轻微。日本志贺核电站的辐射监测设备出了故障，但并未威胁安全；美国国防部一套处理间谍卫星数据的地面系统也曾失灵。2000年2月，美国参议院的一个委员会得出结论，认定这笔开支是合理的。

Many saw it differently: the threat had been exaggerated, even a hoax. Yet calm prevailed largely because so much had been fixed. This is the preparedness paradox: when precautions work, the danger they ward off never appears, so it looks imaginary. Tedious repairs earned mockery rather than gratitude.

许多人却不这么看：在他们眼里，这一威胁被夸大了，甚至是一场骗局。然而，局面之所以能保持平静，很大程度上正是因为大量问题早已修好。这就是"防备悖论"：预防措施一旦奏效，它们所抵御的危险就永远不会出现，于是看起来像是虚构的。那些枯燥乏味的修复工作换来的是嘲笑，而不是感激。

Critics noted that Italy and South Korea spent far less yet fared no worse. The comparison misleads: those low figures often omitted what firms spent, and much of the remedial work replaced obsolete systems overdue for upgrading. Nobody can say which dollars were essential and which excessive. Prevention that works may leave nothing but an invoice, to be weighed against a catastrophe that might have been.

批评者指出，意大利和韩国花得少得多，结果也不比别国差。但这种比较具有误导性：那些低支出数字往往遗漏了企业自己的花费，而且很大一部分补救工作替换的是早该升级的过时系统。谁也说不清哪些钱是必不可少的，哪些是过度的。奏效的预防留下的可能只有一张账单，留待与一场本可能发生的灾难相权衡。

考研英语精读 · No.40

The Glue That Failed Its Way to Success

失败的胶水，成功的便利贴

In 1968 Spencer Silver, a scientist at 3M, set out to make a super-strong adhesive. The polymer he produced did something else. Its tiny spheres formed a layer with little tack, giving paper a temporary hold, so it would adhere, peel off and be reapplied. To an inventor chasing strength, the formulation was a setback.

1968年，3M公司的科学家斯潘塞·西尔弗着手研制一种超强黏合剂。然而他制成的聚合物却是另一回事。它的微小球体形成一层初黏力很小的涂层，让纸张获得暂时的黏着，因此纸张能粘附上去，揭下后还能重新贴上。对一个追求强度的发明者来说，这种配方是一次挫折。

For five years Silver acted as its promoter within 3M's corporate ranks, in informal chats and at seminars. In his own summary, it was a solution without a problem. His perseverance met indifference. The unconventional material found no use, and the pursuit proved fruitless.

五年间，西尔弗在3M的企业上下充当它的推广者，在非正式的闲谈中、也在研讨会上介绍它。按他自己的概括，这是一个找不到问题的解决方案。他的坚持不懈换来的是冷漠。这种非常规的材料找不到用途，这番追求终究一无所获。

In 1974 Art Fry, a colleague who had heard one of those talks, faced a small annoyance at church. The paper bookmarks wedged in his choir's hymn book kept getting dislodged and falling out. The frustration gave Fry an idea. The unwanted adhesive could make a marker that would cling to a page, stay put and leave it undamaged.

1974年，听过其中一次讲座的同事阿特·弗莱在教堂里遇到了一件小小的烦心事。他所在唱诗班的赞美诗集里夹着的纸书签总是移位，掉出来。这份懊恼让弗莱想到一个主意。这种没人要的黏合剂可以做成一种书签，附着在书页上不会移动，还能让书页完好无损。

In 1977 the notes were test-marketed as Press 'n Peel in four American cities, where purchasers showed little enthusiasm. In 1978 3M's marketers staged a blitz in Boise, Idaho, and dispensed complimentary packets of notes to offices. More than 90 per cent of respondents who tried them said they would buy. After the coolness came a turnaround.

1977年，这种便条以"Press 'n Peel"之名在美国四个城市试销，购买者的热情不高。1978年，3M的营销人员在爱达荷州博伊西发起一场闪电攻势，向各办公室分发一小包一小包的免费便条。试用过的受访者中，超过90%表示会购买。冷遇之后，迎来了转机。

On 6 April 1980 the pads, rebranded as Post-it Notes, went on sale nationally in the US, and Canada and Europe followed in 1981. The stationery staple owes its signature canary tone to an unplanned twist. The laboratory next door had only leftover yellow paper.

1980年4月6日，这种便笺本更名为"便利贴"，在美国全国上市；1981年，便利贴又进入加拿大和欧洲。这款文具必备品那标志性的金丝雀黄色调，源于一次计划外的转折。隔壁实验室手头只有剩下的黄纸。

考研英语精读 · No.41

The Toad That Invaded Australia

入侵澳洲的毒蟾

Greyback cane beetles, whose larvae devour roots, were plaguing lucrative sugar plantations in Queensland, north-eastern Australia. In June 1935 Queensland imported 102 cane toads, bulky amphibians native to the Americas, from Hawaii. That August about 2,400 of their young were released. Biological control, setting one species against a pest, had an ambitious new recruit.

灰背蔗甲虫的幼虫啃食根系，正困扰着澳大利亚东北部昆士兰州利润丰厚的甘蔗种植园。1935年6月，昆士兰州从夏威夷引进了102只甘蔗蟾蜍，这是一种原产美洲、体型粗壮的两栖动物。同年8月，它们的约2,400只后代被释放到野外。生物防治，即用一个物种去对付一种害虫，就此迎来了一名雄心勃勃的新兵。

The hasty plan met opposition. In November 1935 an insect expert, Walter Froggatt, persuaded officials to halt releases; after Queensland's lobbying, they resumed in September 1936. The scheme flopped: the toads barely dented beetle numbers, perhaps because the insects stayed high up on cane stalks.

这个草率的计划遭到了反对。1935年11月，昆虫专家沃尔特·弗罗格特说服官员叫停释放；经昆士兰州游说，释放于1936年9月恢复。这套方案以失败告终：蟾蜍几乎没能削减甲虫的数量，或许是因为这些昆虫一直待在甘蔗茎秆的高处。

The toads thrived instead. With few foes and fertile females spawning thousands of eggs, they multiplied unchecked. Their invasion frontier, creeping about ten kilometres a year at first, accelerated to about 50 a year in the tropical north. In February 2009 the first known toad breached Western Australia's border.

蟾蜍自己反倒兴旺起来。天敌寥寥，能生育的雌蟾产下数千枚卵，它们不受遏制地大量繁殖。它们入侵前沿起初每年约缓缓推进十公里，到了热带北部，加快到每年约50公里。2009年2月，已知的第一只蟾蜍突破了西澳大利亚州的边界。

Toxin glands in their skin make them a deadly meal. Numbers of goannas (large lizards) and northern quolls (small marsupial hunters) have slumped in places; some local quoll populations were wiped out. The toads have wreaked havoc, but the damage is uneven; some species have prospered.

它们皮肤里有分泌毒素的腺体，谁吃了它们，这一餐就是致命的。巨蜥和北袋鼬（一种小型有袋类猎手）的数量在一些地方锐减；部分地方的袋鼬种群被彻底消灭。蟾蜍造成了一场浩劫，但各地受害轻重不一；有些物种反而繁盛起来。

Biological control can triumph. From 1926 the cactus moth, Cactoblastis, cleared most prickly pear, an invasive cactus, from Queensland and New South Wales within about a decade. Tested first, its larvae starved rather than eat fifteen other plants; the toads faced no such test. Under an October 1984 law, recruits now face stringent scrutiny. The blunder was born of haste and sealed by lobbying that overrode a warning: an untested ally, once loose, may never be recalled.

生物防治也能大获成功。从1926年起，仙人掌螟这种蛾子在约十年内清除了昆士兰州和新南威尔士州的大部分刺梨，这是一种外来入侵的仙人掌。这种蛾子放出前经过检验，其幼虫宁可饿死也不吃另外十五种植物；蟾蜍却从未受过这样的检验。根据1984年10月通过的一部法律，如今新兵都要经过严格的审查。这个大错起于仓促，又因游说压过了警告而成定局：未经检验的盟友一旦放出，就可能再也召不回来。

考研英语精读 · No.42

The Telescope That Needed Glasses

需要戴眼镜的望远镜

On 24 April 1990 the space shuttle Discovery lofted the Hubble Space Telescope into orbit. The observatory cradled in the payload bay had cost about $1.5 billion, a lavish investment meant to fix its gaze on the cosmos and its galaxies. Money, it soon emerged, could not buy clarity.

1990年4月24日，"发现号"航天飞机把哈勃空间望远镜送上轨道。这座安放在有效载荷舱里的天文台耗资约15亿美元，是一笔巨额投资，旨在把目光投向宇宙及其中的星系。人们很快发现，钱买不来清晰。

Its first snapshots came back blurred and fuzzy. In June 1990 NASA announced a flaw in the main mirror: its outer rim had been flattened by 2.2 micrometres, roughly a fiftieth of the width of a hair. That deviation marred the optics, leaving light without a sharp focus.

它最初的快照传回来时模糊不清、朦胧一片。1990年6月，美国航天局宣布主镜有缺陷，主镜外侧边缘被磨平了2.2微米，大约相当于头发丝宽度的五十分之一。这一偏差损害了光学系统，使光线无法形成锐利的焦点。

The trouble began with calibration. A lens in the assembly that checked the mirror's shape had been offset by about 1.3 millimetres, so the mirror was ground to the wrong curve. A second, independent test by the contractor, Perkin-Elmer, gave a divergent result, but engineers and NASA managers judged it less reliable and left the contradictory reading unexplored.

问题始于校准。检测主镜形状的那套组件中，一块透镜错位了约1.3毫米，主镜因此被磨成了错误的曲面。承包商珀金-埃尔默公司做的第二次独立测试给出了不同的结果，但工程师和美国航天局的管理人员认为它不太可靠，这一相互矛盾的读数也就无人深究。

The mirror could not be repaired in orbit, so engineers prescribed tailored glasses. COSTAR, a corrective cabinet the size of a refrigerator, carried small mirrors on adjustable arms that intercepted the light and compensated for the imperfect mirror's distortion before the light reached the instruments. The mirror itself would stay untouched, and a new main camera was prepared.

主镜无法在轨道上修复，于是工程师开出了一副量身定制的眼镜。COSTAR 是一个冰箱大小的矫正箱体，可调节的机械臂上装着小镜子，在光线到达仪器之前将光截住，抵消有缺陷的主镜造成的畸变。主镜本身将保持原封不动，一台新的主相机也已准备就绪。

On 2 December 1993 Endeavour ascended with seven astronauts. After a rendezvous, they worked in tandem across five spacewalks, about 35 and a half hours of exertion, installing COSTAR and the new camera. The imagery turned crisp, Hubble's vision renewed. The mirror still carries that 2.2-micrometre flaw, and the light it gathers is corrected only after leaving it.

1993年12月2日，"奋进号"载着七名宇航员升空。完成交会后，他们协同进行了五次太空行走，辛苦作业共约35个半小时，安装了 COSTAR 和新相机。影像变得清楚分明，哈勃的视力得以恢复。主镜至今仍带着那2.2微米的缺陷，它收集的光要离开镜面之后才得到矫正。

考研英语精读 · No.43

From a Garage to the Top of the World

从车库到世界之巅

In May 1985, after a bruising confrontation, Apple's board ultimately backed chief executive John Sculley. Steve Jobs, 30, was stripped of his managerial duties at the company he had co-founded. That September, sidelined and alienated, he resigned.

1985年5月，经过一场激烈的交锋，苹果公司董事会最终支持首席执行官约翰·斯卡利。时年30岁的史蒂夫·乔布斯在他参与创办的公司里被剥夺了管理职务。同年9月，已被架空、与公司心生隔阂的乔布斯辞职。

Rewind three decades. Born in San Francisco on 24 February 1955, Jobs was adopted as a newborn. On 1 April 1976 he founded Apple with Steve Wozniak, the electronics engineer behind its first prototypes. Apple's garage origins are largely symbolic; Wozniak calls their significance inflated. Apple floated on 12 December 1980; the flagship Macintosh debuted on 24 January 1984.

回溯三十年。1955年2月24日，乔布斯生于旧金山，还是新生儿时就被人收养。1976年4月1日，他与史蒂夫·沃兹尼亚克创办了苹果公司；沃兹尼亚克是一名电子工程师，苹果最早的样机出自他手。苹果的车库起家史在很大程度上只具象征意义；沃兹尼亚克认为它的分量被夸大了。1980年12月12日，苹果公司上市；1984年1月24日，旗舰产品 Macintosh 亮相。

Exile proved productive. Later in 1985 Jobs founded NeXT, a computer start-up. In February 1986 he acquired Lucasfilm's computer-graphics outfit, which became Pixar. It began as a manufacturer of specialised hardware, not chiefly an animation studio. Toy Story, released on 22 November 1995, was the first feature-length computer-animated film.

这段放逐岁月成果丰硕。1985年晚些时候，乔布斯创办了电脑初创公司 NeXT。1986年2月，他收购了卢卡斯影业的电脑图形团队，这个团队后来成为皮克斯。皮克斯起初是一家制造商，生产专用硬件，主要并不是动画工作室。《玩具总动员》于1995年11月22日上映，是第一部长篇电脑动画电影。

The reversal came on 20 December 1996: Apple agreed to buy NeXT, gaining the core software of the eventual Mac OS X and reuniting with its estranged co-founder. In August 1997 Microsoft invested $150 million in its distressed competitor; that September Jobs took the reins as interim chief. Apple, he later said, had been weeks from ruin. The iMac, iPod and iPhone followed, the iPhone showcased on 9 January 2007.

1996年12月20日，局面出现转机。当天苹果公司同意买下 NeXT，得到了日后 Mac OS X 的核心软件，并与那位关系疏远的联合创始人重聚。1997年8月，微软向这个陷入困境的竞争对手投资1.5亿美元；同年9月，乔布斯接掌大权，出任临时负责人。他后来说，苹果当时离破产只差几周。iMac、iPod 和 iPhone 相继问世，iPhone 于2007年1月9日公开展示。

In October 2003 Jobs received a diagnosis: an uncommon, cancerous pancreatic tumour. In August 2011 Apple briefly overtook Exxon Mobil as the world's most valuable company. His tenure ended on 24 August 2011; he died on 5 October 2011, aged 56. He had spent nearly fourteen years at the helm of the company that ousted him.

2003年10月，乔布斯被诊断出患有一种罕见的恶性胰腺肿瘤。2011年8月，苹果公司一度超越埃克森美孚，成为全球市值最高的公司。2011年8月24日，他的任期结束；2011年10月5日，他去世，终年56岁。他已在那家曾把他逐出的公司掌舵近十四年。

考研英语精读 · No.44

The Night Sweden Switched Sides

一夜之间换边开车

In a 1955 referendum, about 83 per cent of voters chose to keep driving on the left, an overwhelmingly clear rejection of change. On 10 May 1963, however, legislators in parliament overruled the electorate, approving right-hand traffic from 1967 in defiance of a populace among whom the plan was unpopular.

在1955年的一次全民公投中，约83%的投票者选择继续靠左行驶，压倒性地明确否决了改变。然而1963年5月10日，议会中的议员推翻了全体选民的意见，批准从1967年起改为靠右行驶。他们全然不顾这一计划在民众中不得人心。

The case rested on conformity. Norway and Finland drove on the right, with about five million border crossings a year, so a left-hand orientation meant isolation and inconvenient journeys. Safety favoured alignment too. About 90 per cent of cars were left-hand drive, so motorists overtaking on narrow two-lane highways risked head-on collisions.

理由在于与邻国保持一致。挪威和芬兰都靠右行驶，每年约有500万车次过境，因此靠左的行车方向意味着孤立和不便的出行。安全方面也支持统一。约90%的汽车是左舵车，于是驾车者在狭窄的双车道公路上超车时，有发生迎头相撞的危险。

A state body ran the mammoth preparation under a logo, a large H with an arrow. Its propaganda included a song contest on television, and the winning jingle, whose lyrics urged people to keep right, reached fifth in the chart. Some 360,000 items of signage were changed, and a massive fleet of over 1,000 new buses with right-hand doors was procured.

一个国家机构以一个大写H加箭头作为标志，主持这项浩大的准备工作。它的宣传包括一场电视歌曲比赛，获胜的广告歌用歌词劝人们靠右行驶，登上了排行榜第五名。约36万件标识被更换，一支由1,000多辆右侧开门的新公交车组成的庞大车队也已购置到位。

The transition came on Sunday 3 September 1967. A curfew kept non-essential vehicles off the asphalt from one to six, with essential traffic exempt. At ten to five, all traffic stopped and crawled across, restarting at five, before dawn, with its direction flipped. Stockholm and Malmö had a longer closure, from Saturday morning to Sunday afternoon, while crews reconfigured junctions.

转换于1967年9月3日星期日进行。宵禁令非必要车辆在凌晨1点至6点不得驶上柏油路，必要车辆可以豁免。4点50分，所有车辆停下，缓缓挪到另一侧，5点重新出发，此时还在黎明之前，行驶方向已经翻转。斯德哥尔摩和马尔默的封闭时间更长，从星期六上午持续到星期日下午，工人们在此期间重新调整了路口。

On the following Monday, 125 accidents were reported, against 130 to 198 on earlier Mondays, a statistical improvement put down to the heightened care of hesitant drivers. The nervousness apparently subsided and prudent habits faded, for by 1969 accident and fatality rates had reverted to their baseline.

随后的星期一，报告的事故有125起，而此前几个星期一为130至198起；这一统计上的改善被归因于犹豫的驾车人格外小心。紧张情绪似乎逐渐平息，谨慎的习惯也慢慢消退——到1969年，事故率和死亡率已回到原来的基准水平，便是明证。

考研英语精读 · No.45

The Record Humanity Threw into the Stars

写给外星人的一张唱片

In late summer 1977, NASA fastened a disc wrought from gilded copper, 30 centimetres wide, to each Voyager spaceship before takeoff. Nobody could foresee who, if anyone, would play one.

1977年夏末，NASA在每艘旅行者号宇宙飞船的起飞之前，给它固定上一张圆盘。圆盘用镀金的铜打造而成，宽30厘米。谁也无法预见，究竟会不会有人、又会是谁来播放其中一张。

Advisers under Carl Sagan of Cornell distilled our earthly abode into this compendium. Voices greet the listener in 55 languages, followed by the rumble of thunder, the whistle of wind, whales, footsteps, laughter and a rhythmic heartbeat. About 90 minutes of eclectic music cover a spectrum of cultures and eras, notably Bach and Chuck Berry's Johnny B. Goode.

康奈尔大学卡尔·萨根手下的顾问们，把我们在尘世的家园，提炼成这部汇编。人声用55种语言向听者致以问候，接着是隆隆的雷声、风的呼啸声、鲸歌、脚步声、笑声和富有节奏的心跳。约90分钟兼收并蓄的音乐，覆盖了多种文化与年代的广阔谱系，尤其值得一提的是巴赫和查克·贝里的《Johnny B. Goode》。

An array of 115 images, encoded in analogue form, sits beside printed words from two statesmen, Jimmy Carter and Kurt Waldheim. The sleeve gives guidance on playing the disc, plus a diagram fixing the Sun with pulsars as beacons.

一系列共115幅图像，以模拟方式编码，旁边印着两位政治家吉米·卡特与库尔特·瓦尔德海姆的文字。封套上给出播放的指引，还有一幅示意图，以脉冲星作信标标定太阳的位置。

On 14 February 1990, at Sagan's request, Voyager 1 pivoted its camera for a glance backwards across about six billion kilometres. Sixty frames formed a panorama of the solar system, a mosaic in which Earth was a glimmer about an eighth of a pixel across. They reached Earth's receivers between March and 1 May, each signal taking five and a half hours.

1990年2月14日，应萨根之请，旅行者1号转动相机，向后投去一瞥，跨越约60亿公里。60帧画面拼成太阳系的全景，在这幅拼图中，地球只是一点微光，宽约八分之一个像素。这些画面在3月至5月1日间抵达地球的接收设备，信号单程要走五个半小时。

In a 1994 book, Sagan offered that pale dot as a perspective on our small and ephemeral place amid cosmic vastness.

在1994年的一本书中，萨根把那个暗淡的小点作为一种视角，借以审视我们在宇宙的浩瀚之中渺小而短暂的位置。

Voyager 1 crossed into interstellar space in August 2012, the first human-made object there, and Voyager 2 followed in 2018. Both work on with a longevity unmatched at NASA, but depletion of their power should silence them within a few years.

2012年8月，旅行者1号进入星际空间，成为第一个到达那里的人造物体；旅行者2号于2018年跟进。两者仍在工作，其长寿在NASA无可匹敌，但电力耗竭预计会让它们在几年之内沉寂。

The Voyagers' discs will glide on outwards through the void as messengers with a map leading back to the Sun.

两艘旅行者号的唱片将作为信使，向外穿过虚空继续滑行，携着一幅指回太阳的地图。

考研英语精读 · No.46

Lost in the Glare

光之殇

A moth skims over an ultraviolet lamp and flips upside down, belly to the sky. In Costa Rica's misty Monteverde cloud-forest, stereo cameras caught such acrobatics for a 2024 Imperial College study that unravelled an age-old puzzle, the helpless swirl of insects round porch lights.

一只飞蛾掠过一盏紫外灯，随即上下颠倒地翻过身来，肚皮朝天。在哥斯达黎加蒙特韦尔德雾气缭绕的云雾林里，立体摄像机为伦敦帝国理工学院2024年的一项研究拍下了这类杂技动作。这项研究解开了一个由来已久的谜团，那就是昆虫为什么会身不由己地绕着门廊灯打转。

Folk wisdom blames the circling on attraction. In fact, insects instinctively keep their backs to whatever shines brightest, normally the sky, to stay level. A lamp, a counterfeit sky, hijacks this built-in habit, leaving insects eddying in its halo or inverted above it. In 477 field clips, precious few darted straight at the light.

民间的老说法把这种打转归咎于吸引。事实上，昆虫会本能地让背部朝向最亮的东西，那通常是天空，以此保持平稳。灯成了一片冒牌的天空，劫持了这种与生俱来的习性，让昆虫在它的光晕里打着旋，或在它上方翻转过来。在477段野外视频片段中，径直冲向灯光的昆虫寥寥无几。

Many fireflies flirt with their lanterns. A male blinks an overture, a female winks back, and bright nights smother the banter. At Tufts University, suitors under artificial light mustered about five flash patterns a minute instead of ten. Females reciprocated about half as often under red or blue light, scarcely at all under white and never under amber.

许多萤火虫用自己的灯笼来调情。雄虫闪烁着奏出一段序曲，雌虫眨眼般回应，而太亮的夜晚会淹没这番调笑。在塔夫茨大学，处在人造光下的求偶者每分钟只勉强打出约五组闪光，而不是十组。雌虫在红光或蓝光下回应的次数约减半，在白光下几乎不回应，在琥珀色光下则从不回应。

The harm spills into the ecosystem. Along 55 Thames Valley roadsides, lamp-lit hedges held 47% fewer moth caterpillars than dark ones, with harsh white LEDs worse than mellower sodium lamps. Street lamps rigged up in seven of 14 Swiss meadows cut nocturnal pollinator visits by 62%. The plants set fewer fruit, and daytime pollinators proved poor understudies.

危害还会蔓延到整个生态系统。在泰晤士河谷的55处路边，有灯照明的树篱里，蛾类毛虫比没灯的地方少47%，而刺眼的白色LED比更柔和的钠灯更糟。在瑞士14片草甸中的7片，临时架起的路灯让夜间传粉者的造访减少了62%。植物结的果实变少了，而白天的传粉者也只是不称职的替补。

Over 80% of humanity lives beneath a luminous veil, which hides the pale ribbon of the Milky Way from more than a third. Citizen scientists' star counts from 2011 to 2022 show the sky brightening about 9.6% a year. At that pace, a child born where 250 stars twinkle would see about 100 by their eighteenth birthday.

全球80%以上的人类生活在一层发光的帷幕之下，它让三分之一以上的人看不见银河那条淡淡的光带。公民科学家2011年至2022年的数星记录显示，夜空每年约变亮9.6%。按这个速度，若一个孩子出生在能看到250颗星星闪耀的地方，到18岁生日时只能看到约100颗。

考研英语精读 · No.47

Shadows of Wolves Over Yellowstone

黄石公园狼影再现

Yellowstone's iconic grey wolves had been exterminated by 1926. On 12 January 1995 eight wolves captured in Alberta, Canada, were hauled to the park in crates; six more followed. Released from enclosures in late March, they roamed the wilderness.

黄石公园那些标志性的灰狼，到1926年已遭赶尽杀绝。1995年1月12日，八只在加拿大艾伯塔省捕获的狼装在板条箱里，一路拖运进了公园；随后又来了六只。3月下旬，它们被放出围栏，得以在荒野中四处游荡。

In February 2014 a viral video narrated by writer George Monbiot spun a compelling narrative: elk, thinned by wolves and wary of them, retreated from valleys, letting willows flourish and riverbanks stabilise. Ecologists call this a trophic cascade: predators eat or deter plant-eaters, and vegetation rebounds.

2014年2月，一段由作家乔治·蒙比奥特解说的视频在网上疯传，编织出一个引人入胜的故事。片中说，麋鹿被狼捕杀得数量变少，又处处提防着狼，便撤离了河谷，于是柳树繁茂起来，河岸也稳固下来。生态学家把这称为营养级联：捕食者吃掉或吓阻食草动物，植被便恢复生长。

Yet the census undermined the thinning claim: elk on the park's northern range peaked at about 19,000 in January 1994 and were dwindling before wolves arrived, depleted by hunters, drought and bears. Fear looked weak too: in June 2013 Arthur Middleton's University of Wyoming team found that collared elk east of the park kept grazing unless wolves ventured within half a mile.

然而，普查结果首先就削弱了“鹿被狼吃少了”这一说法。黄石北部草场的麋鹿在1994年1月达到约19,000头的峰值，狼还没来就已日渐减少；猎人、干旱和熊不断削减着鹿群。“恐惧”一说看来也站不住。2013年6月，阿瑟·米德尔顿领导的怀俄明大学团队发现，园区东侧戴项圈的麋鹿照样吃草，除非狼闯进半英里以内。

Trees fared no better: in November 2021 Utah State University's Elaine Brice showed that sampling only the tallest aspens overstated regrowth four- to sevenfold. The deeper flaw was water. Colorado State University's Tom Hobbs and David Cooper had excluded elk with fences and built artificial beaver dams. In January 2024 they reported: eroding streams had cut so deep that stunted willows no longer reached water.

树木方面的证据也好不到哪里去：2021年11月，犹他州立大学的伊莱恩·布赖斯指出，只抽样测量最高的山杨，会把它们的再生程度夸大四到七倍。更根本的漏洞出在水上。科罗拉多州立大学的汤姆·霍布斯和戴维·库珀用围栏把麋鹿挡在外面，并修建人工海狸坝。2024年1月，他们报告说，不断冲蚀河床的溪流下切太深，那些矮小的柳树已经够不着水了。

In January 2025 Oregon State University's William Ripple, an advocate of the hypothesis, reported one of the world's strongest cascades; critics disputed his methods. The question is unresolved, but the wolves' shadow looks fainter than the fable suggested, and the science messier. A discipline willing to revise its cherished example may deserve more trust than the fable ever did.

2025年1月，这一假说的倡导者、俄勒冈州立大学的威廉·里普尔发文称，黄石的级联效应位居世界最强之列；批评者则对他的方法提出质疑。问题仍悬而未决，但狼投下的影子比寓言所说的要淡，相关研究也要复杂纠结得多。一门肯修正自己珍视的案例的学科，或许比那则寓言更值得信赖。

考研英语精读 · No.48

Rice for the World

稻济天下

In July 1964, inspecting rice ear by ear in a paddy at Anjiang Agricultural School in Hunan province, the instructor Yuan Longping spotted a peculiar plant. It was male-sterile, shedding no viable pollen. Rice pollinates itself, so breeding hybrid seed in bulk depends on such plants. In February 1966 he detailed the anomaly in the journal Chinese Science Bulletin.

1964年7月，湖南省安江农校的教员袁隆平在一块稻田里逐穗察看，发现了一株奇特的稻子。它是雄性不育株，散不出有活力的花粉。水稻是自花授粉的，要大批培育杂交种子，就得靠这样的植株。1966年2月，他在期刊《科学通报》上详细描述了这一反常现象。

The specimen launched a six-year hunt: Yuan and his colleagues could not breed a sterile line whose offspring reliably inherited the trait. The breakthrough came on 23 November 1970, in a marsh near Nanhong Farm in Sanya, Hainan. Yuan's assistant, Li Bihu, and Feng Keshan, a farm technician, found wild rice whose pollen was abortive.

这株标本开启了一场长达六年的搜寻：袁隆平和同事们始终培育不出一个不育系，能让后代稳定地遗传这一性状。突破出现在1970年11月23日，地点是海南三亚南红农场附近的一片沼泽。袁隆平的助手李必湖和农场技术员冯克珊在那里找到了一种野生稻，它的花粉败育。

Then came Yuan's decisive move: rather than withhold the strain, he distributed it to collaborators nationwide. Hybrids need three distinct lines: a sterile line, one to maintain its sterility and one to restore fertility in the hybrids. Yan Longan's team in Jiangxi bred sterile lines and Zhang Xiancheng in Guangxi first identified a restorer line. In October 1973, at a national conference in Suzhou, Yuan declared the three-line system complete.

随后，袁隆平迈出了决定性的一步：他没有把这个品系扣在自己手里，而是分给了全国各地的协作者。杂交稻需要三个各不相同的系：不育系，保持其不育性的保持系，以及使杂交种恢复育性的恢复系。江西颜龙安的团队育成了不育系，广西的张先程率先找出了恢复系。1973年10月，在苏州召开的全国性会议上，袁隆平宣布三系已经配套。

By 1976 hybrids were under cultivation on about 140,000 hectares. The World Food Prize Foundation estimates that hybrid rice can yield up to a fifth more grain than conventional varieties. By the early 2000s it covered almost half of China's rice fields.

到1976年，杂交稻的种植面积已达约14万公顷。据世界粮食奖基金会估计，杂交稻比常规品种最多可多产五分之一的稻谷。到21世纪初，杂交稻已覆盖中国近一半的稻田。

Yuan, who shared the 2004 World Food Prize with Sierra Leone's Monty Jones, died in Changsha on 22 May 2021, aged 91. Yet popular myth portrays him as a solitary genius. His eye began the search; his generosity let many hands bring in the harvest.

袁隆平曾与塞拉利昂的蒙蒂·琼斯共获2004年世界粮食奖，2021年5月22日在长沙逝世，享年91岁。然而，一种流传甚广的误解把他描绘成一个单枪匹马的天才。他的慧眼开启了这场搜寻；他的慷慨，让许多双手一起迎来了丰收。

考研英语精读 · No.49

The Woman Who Won Two Nobels

两度摘得诺奖的女人

Marie Curie was born Maria Skłodowska on 7 November 1867 in Warsaw, then under Russian dominion, where women were denied university admission. Eager to pursue formal study, she emigrated to Paris in 1891, enrolling at the Sorbonne. On 26 July 1895 she wed the physicist Pierre Curie, forging a research partnership.

玛丽·居里原名玛丽亚·斯克沃多夫斯卡，1867年11月7日生于华沙。当时华沙处于俄国的统治之下，那里的女性得不到大学的入学资格。她渴望接受正规教育，于1891年移居巴黎，注册入读索邦大学。1895年7月26日，她与物理学家皮埃尔·居里成婚，两人建立起研究上的合作关系。

Henri Becquerel found that uranium emitted rays; she named this property of certain elements radioactivity. In July 1898 the Curies announced polonium, a tribute to her motherland, and on 26 December, radium. In a leaky shed they painstakingly processed tonnes of pitchblende, a uranium ore; by 1902 she had recovered about a tenth of a gram of radium chloride.

亨利·贝克勒尔发现，铀会发出射线；她把某些元素的这种性质命名为"放射性"。1898年7月，居里夫妇宣布发现了钋，以此致敬她的祖国；12月26日，他们又宣布发现了镭。在一间漏雨的棚屋里，他们费尽心力地处理了成吨的沥青铀矿，这是一种铀矿石。到1902年，她已提取出约十分之一克的氯化镭。

On 10 December 1903 she jointly won the physics Nobel with Pierre and Becquerel, the first woman so recognised. The nomination had initially left her out; at Pierre's insistence, the omission was rectified. On 19 April 1906 a horse-drawn wagon fatally struck Pierre. On 5 November she gave her inaugural lecture as his successor, the Sorbonne's first woman professor.

1903年12月10日，她与皮埃尔和贝克勒尔共同获得诺贝尔物理学奖，成为第一位获得诺贝尔奖表彰的女性。最初的提名中没有她；在皮埃尔的坚持下，这一疏漏得到了纠正。1906年4月19日，一辆马拉的货车撞上皮埃尔，致其身亡。11月5日，她作为他的继任者发表了就职演讲，成为索邦大学第一位女教授。

In November 1911 a press scandal about her private life erupted; she was urged to stay away. Undeterred, she collected the chemistry Nobel, won outright, on 10 December. Her distinction in two sciences remains unmatched. In the First World War she mobilised X-ray vans and stationary posts that examined over a million casualties.

1911年11月，一场关于她私生活的媒体丑闻爆发，有人劝她不要前往。她不为所动，于12月10日领取了她独得的诺贝尔化学奖。她在两门科学中双双获得诺贝尔奖，这份荣誉至今仍无人企及。第一次世界大战期间，她调集了X光车和固定X光站，为一百多万名伤员做了检查。

She died on 4 July 1934 of a blood disorder, probably caused by prolonged exposure to radiation. Her notebooks, still radioactive, are preserved in lead-lined boxes at the National Library of France; readers sign a liability waiver and don protective garments. The pages retain her handwriting and her radium.

1934年7月4日，她死于一种血液疾病，这种病很可能是长期受辐射所致。她的笔记本至今仍有放射性，保存在法国国家图书馆的铅衬盒子里；读者须签署责任豁免书，并穿上防护服装。这些书页留存着她的笔迹，也留存着她的镭。

garmentsn. (garment) 衣服，服装（woollen garments；a garment factory）5
retainv. 保留，保持（retain heat/control；retain one's title）5

考研英语精读 · No.50

Microplastics: Everywhere, but How Harmful?

微塑料：无处不在，危害几何？

In May 2004 Richard Thompson, a marine biologist at the University of Plymouth, and colleagues described an overlooked pollutant in the journal Science. Sifting through plankton samples kept since the 1960s, they found that microscopic plastic fragments and synthetic fibres had grown more abundant. They labelled the particles microplastics.

2004年5月，普利茅斯大学的海洋生物学家理查德·汤普森和同事在《科学》杂志上撰文，介绍了一种此前少有人注意的污染物。他们细细筛查自20世纪60年代起保存下来的浮游生物样本，发现极其微小的塑料碎片和合成纤维变得越来越多。他们把这些颗粒称为“微塑料”。

Since then the durable debris has proved ubiquitous, turning up on shorelines, in deep-sea sediment, in rainfall and in Arctic ice. Regulation came before proof of harm to people: in December 2015 America passed a law banning plastic microbeads from scrubs and other cosmetics rinsed down drains.

此后，这些经久不烂的碎屑被证明无所不在，海岸线上、深海沉积物里、雨水中和北极的冰层中都有它们的踪迹。人体受害尚无证据，监管就已先行：2015年12月，美国立法禁止在磨砂膏等用后被冲进下水道的化妆品中使用塑料微珠。

The search turned inward. In March 2022 a small Dutch study found plastic in the blood of about three-quarters of 22 healthy donors. In March 2024 a study in the New England Journal of Medicine linked plastic in the fatty plaque of neck arteries to a higher risk of heart attack, stroke or death. Its authors cautioned that correlation is not causation.

人们的搜寻随后转向了体内。2022年3月，荷兰一项小规模研究检测了22名健康献血者的血液，约四分之三的人血中有塑料。2024年3月，《新英格兰医学杂志》上的一项研究发现，颈部动脉的脂肪性斑块中含有塑料，与心脏病发作、中风或死亡的风险升高有关。研究者提醒，相关性并不等于因果关系。

The most sensational figures are the shakiest. In June 2019 a report for WWF, a conservation group, reckoned people might swallow a credit card's worth of plastic a week; critics call that an overestimate. Two months later the World Health Organization was reassuring but tentative: on limited evidence, microplastics in drinking water posed no apparent risk at current levels. In February 2025 a study in Nature Medicine reported plastic in brain tissue in an amount popularised as a spoonful; others queried its methodology.

最耸人听闻的数字，也最站不住脚。2019年6月，一份为自然保护组织世界自然基金会（WWF）撰写的报告估算，人们每周可能吞下相当于一张信用卡的塑料；批评者则说这是高估。两个月后，世界卫生组织的结论令人安心，却只是初步的：依据有限的证据，按目前的含量，饮用水中的微塑料并无明显风险。2025年2月，医学期刊《自然·医学》上的一项研究报告在脑组织中发现了塑料，其含量后来被传成脑中有一勺塑料；另有学者对它的研究方法提出了质疑。

Detection has outrun understanding; human studies remain small or observational. Harm is plausible but unproven, and until the verdict is in, better measurement may guide policy more safely than panic.

检测走在了认识前面；针对人体的研究规模仍小，或只是观察性的。说它有害合乎情理，却尚未证实；在定论出来之前，依据更好的测量来制定政策，或许比被恐慌左右更稳妥。

考研英语精读 · No.51

The Joke That Became OK

OK 原本是个玩笑

OK, often said to be the planet's most frequently spoken word, began as a newspaper gag. From 1838 abbreviations were all the vogue in Boston's papers, and the craze turned comic through deliberate misspellings: O.W. for 'oll wright', K.Y. for 'know yuse'. The humour lay in the persona of an uneducated bumpkin.

常有人说，OK 是全世界说得最多的词，而它起初只是报纸上的一个噱头。从1838年起，缩写在波士顿各报风靡一时，这股热潮又借故意的错拼变得滑稽，比如用 O.W. 代表 'oll wright'，用 K.Y. 代表 'know yuse'。其中的幽默来自一个没受过教育的乡巴佬人设。

On 23 March 1839 a Boston Morning Post satire took a swipe at a contemporary, the Providence Journal, over a mock society parodying an ordinance against ringing dinner bells. It carried the first known 'o.k.', glossed as 'all correct'. The letters stood for a knowingly illiterate 'oll korrect'. The presumed writer was the editor, Charles Gordon Greene.

1839年3月23日，《波士顿晨邮报》的一篇讽刺文章，挖苦了它的同行《普罗维登斯日报》，话题是一个恶搞社团，这个社团戏仿的是一项禁止敲开饭铃的法令。文中出现了已知最早的 'o.k.'，并注明意为 'all correct'。这两个字母代表的是有意写成文盲拼法的 'oll korrect'。一般推定的作者是该报编辑查尔斯·戈登·格林。

Partisan politics propagated the letters nationwide. In 1840 Martin Van Buren, the incumbent president, sought re-election. His New York backers, punning on his nickname 'Old Kinderhook', after his birthplace, formed the Democratic O.K. Club, making the letters a double-meaning slogan. That November William Henry Harrison unseated him, 234 electoral votes to 60.

党派政治把这两个字母传遍全国。1840年，在任总统马丁·范布伦寻求连任。他在纽约的拥护者拿他的绰号 'Old Kinderhook' 玩双关，这个绰号得名于他的出生地。他们成立了"民主党 O.K. 俱乐部"，让这两个字母成了一句一语双关的口号。那年11月，威廉·亨利·哈里森以234张对60张选举人票把他赶下台。

On 30 March 1840 the New York Morning Herald printed a fabricated smear: the former president Andrew Jackson, a supposedly inept speller, had marked paperwork O.K., for 'Ole Kurrek'. Jackson spelled reasonably well and never marked documents O.K. Within about twenty years, people did so in earnest. The bogus notation was merely premature.

1840年3月30日，《纽约先驱晨报》登出一则捏造的抹黑消息，说据说拼写笨拙的前总统安德鲁·杰克逊，曾在公文上批 O.K.，代表 'Ole Kurrek'。其实杰克逊拼写得还算不错，也从没在文件上批过 O.K.。大约二十年内，人们就当真这样批了。这个子虚乌有的批注不过是出现得过早。

Rival stories offered exotic pedigrees: Choctaw 'okeh' and Greek 'ola kala'. Authoritative dictionaries favoured Choctaw as late as 1961. In 1963–64 the linguist Allen Walker Read excavated the neglected Boston provenance from archival newspapers in six influential American Speech articles. Most dictionaries now embrace his derivation, a pedigree of bad spelling and dinner-bell satire.

与之竞争的说法给出了种种异域的来历，比如乔克托语的 'okeh' 和希腊语的 'ola kala'。权威词典直到1961年还倾向于乔克托语一说。1963至1964年，语言学家艾伦·沃克·里德在《美国语言》杂志上发表了六篇影响深远的文章，从档案报纸中发掘出长期被忽视的波士顿起源。如今大多数词典都采纳了他的词源解释，那是一段由错误拼写和开饭铃讽刺文构成的来历。

考研英语精读 · No.52

The Killer Smog of 1952

夺命毒雾：伦敦1952

On Friday 5 December 1952, cold, stagnant air descended on London. Overhead, warmer air pressed down like a lid, holding the chill captive beneath. Londoners were accustomed to murky, dreary winters. This time, though, the capital's smoke was trapped too, thickening into a choking smog, a mix of smoke and fog.

1952年12月5日，星期五，寒冷而凝滞的空气降临伦敦。头顶上方，较暖的空气像盖子一样压下来，把寒气困在下面。伦敦人早已习惯冬天昏暗、阴沉。不过这一次，首都的煤烟也被困住了，越积越浓，成了令人窒息的烟雾，也就是烟与雾的混合物。

That smoke, from coal burned in domestic fireplaces and power stations, was laden with soot and noxious sulphur dioxide. In places, visibility fell to a few metres. Ambulance services were suspended, an opera at the Sadler's Wells theatre was abandoned at the interval, and prize cattle at the Smithfield agricultural show died or had to be slaughtered.

这些煤烟来自家庭壁炉和发电厂烧的煤，满含烟尘和有毒的二氧化硫。有些地方，能见度降到只有几米。救护车服务暂停；萨德勒之井剧院（Sadler's Wells）的一场歌剧演到幕间休息被迫中止；史密斯菲尔德农业展会上的获奖的牛有的死去，有的只得宰杀。

The smog dissipated on Tuesday 9 December. Officials at the time put the excess deaths at about 4,000, but a 1954 Ministry of Health report blamed later deaths on influenza. A study published in Environmental Health Perspectives in 2001 reassessed the evidence and cast doubt on that explanation, estimating about 12,000 excess deaths between December 1952 and February 1953.

12月9日，星期二，烟雾消散。当时的官员估计超额死亡约4,000人，但卫生部1954年的一份报告把后来的死亡归因于流感。2001年发表于《环境健康展望》（Environmental Health Perspectives）的一项研究重新评估了证据，对这一解释提出质疑，估计1952年12月至1953年2月间超额死亡约12,000人。

For decades Londoners had tolerated the smoke as a nuisance. One villain flickered in their own grates, a source of warmth and comfort so habitual that few thought to accuse it. Such complacency may help to explain the government's grudging response. It withstood pressure for an inquiry until May 1953.

几十年来，伦敦人一直把煤烟看成一种麻烦，容忍了下来。有一个元凶就在他们自家的炉栅里摇曳，它带来温暖与舒适，人们早已习以为常，很少有人想到要指责它。这种麻痹大意或许有助于说明，政府为何勉强作出回应。政府一直顶住要求调查的压力，直到1953年5月才松口。

Reform proved protracted, slowed by official inertia. The Clean Air Act became law on 5 July 1956, three and a half years after the smog. It empowered municipal councils to create smokeless zones, combating smoke at its source. The cosy hearth, beloved of British families, had, all along, been among the chief offenders.

改革旷日持久，又因官方的惰性而进展迟缓。1956年7月5日，《清洁空气法》成为法律，距那场烟雾已有三年半。该法授权市镇议会设立无烟区，从源头上遏制煤烟。英国家庭深受喜爱的、温馨的炉膛，原来一直都是主要的肇事者之一。

考研英语精读 · No.53

The Wind That Kept No Time: The Fall of Galloping Gertie

不合拍的风：塔科马大桥的陨落

On the morning of 7 November 1940, a sustained wind of about 40 miles an hour set the Tacoma Narrows Bridge twisting. Its deck tilted from side to side in ever more violent oscillations until most of the main span tore apart and tumbled into the water. No one died. Footage of the spectacle would later fascinate students worldwide.

1940年11月7日上午，一股时速约40英里的持续大风使塔科马海峡大桥扭动起来。它的桥面左右倾侧，振荡愈发剧烈，直到主跨大部分断裂、跌落水中。无人丧生。这一奇观的影像后来吸引了世界各地的学生。

The bridge had opened in July 1940 as the third-longest suspension span in the world at the time, its roadway hung from steel cables. Girders only eight feet deep stiffened the deck, making it slender, elegant and economical, but far from rigid. Even faint breezes made it heave, earning the nickname Galloping Gertie.

这座桥于1940年7月通车，其主跨是当时世界上第三长的悬索桥跨径，路面悬挂在钢缆上。加劲桥面的钢梁只有8英尺高，使桥身纤细、优雅而又省钱，却远谈不上刚硬。哪怕轻微的微风也会让它起伏，由此得了个绰号——"舞动的格蒂"。

Textbooks have long invoked resonance: a periodic push that matches an object's natural frequency can amplify its motion, as when well-timed shoves propel a child on a swing. The analogy is intuitive but deceptive. That morning's wind did not pulse in time with the bridge; it blew fairly steadily.

教科书长期以来一直援引共振来解释：与物体固有频率相合的周期性的推力能放大其运动，就像合拍的一次次推送推动秋千上的孩子越荡越高。这个类比直观却具有迷惑性。那天上午的风并没有合着大桥的节拍一阵阵涌动；它吹得还算平稳。

The mechanism was subtler. Each twist altered the passing airflow, which reinforced the next twist: a feedback loop that kept tapping the wind for energy. In March 1941 a federal panel blamed the deck's flexibility and wing-like lift. Later analysis called the mechanism flutter; in February 1991 two engineers asserted in a physics journal that textbooks had erred.

其机理要更为微妙。每一次扭动都改变了掠过的气流，而气流又加强了下一次扭动：这是一个不断从风中汲取能量的反馈回路。1941年3月，一个联邦调查委员会把原因归于桥面的柔性和类似机翼的升力。后来的分析把这一机理称为"颤振"；1991年2月，两位工程师在一份物理学期刊上断言，教科书说错了。

The panel urged wind-tunnel tests, and trials on scaled-down replicas gradually became customary for suspension bridges. A sturdier replacement opened in October 1950. Gertie's fall may still instruct engineers, if read accurately: the wind never kept time with the bridge — the bridge's own motion turned a sustained wind destructive.

该委员会力主进行风洞试验，而用按比例缩小的模型做试验也逐渐成为悬索桥的惯例。一座更坚固的替代桥于1950年10月通车。格蒂的倒塌或许至今仍能给工程师以教益——只要解读得准确：风从未合着大桥的节拍，是大桥自身的运动让持续不断的风变得具有破坏力。

考研英语精读 · No.54

The Sound That Shook the World

震动世界的一声巨响

Shortly after ten in the morning on 27 August 1883, Krakatoa, a volcanic island in the Sunda Strait, unleashed its most thunderous explosion. About four hours later, islanders on Rodrigues, nearly 4,800 kilometres across the Indian Ocean, took the roar for distant gunfire. Barometers worldwide recorded the atmospheric pressure wave, some of them seven times in about five days.

1883年8月27日上午10时刚过，巽他海峡中的火山岛喀拉喀托迸发出其最为震耳欲聋的一声爆炸。约四小时后，在印度洋彼岸、相距近4,800公里的罗德里格斯岛上，岛民把这声轰鸣当成了远处的炮火声。世界各地的气压计都记录到了这次大气压力波，其中一些在约五天内先后记录到七次。

Restless since 20 May, the volcano reached a catastrophic climax on 26–27 August. About two-thirds of the island collapsed into its emptied magma chamber, vanishing beneath the sea. Tsunamis, reportedly over 30 metres high in places, swamped coastal settlements on Java and Sumatra. Dutch colonial administrators counted 36,417 dead, mostly drowned, perhaps underestimating the toll.

这座火山自5月20日起便躁动不安，8月26日至27日达到灾难性的高潮。约三分之二的岛体塌入已被排空的岩浆房，消失在海面之下。海啸据报道局部高逾30米，淹没了爪哇岛和苏门答腊岛的沿海居民点。荷兰殖民地行政官员统计出36,417名死者，其中大多数是溺亡的，而他们或许低估了死亡人数。

Telegrams from Batavia (now Jakarta), relayed by submarine cable, reached London within hours. Western newspapers soon began chronicling what is often called the first global news event. Yet early dispatches were terse; the magnitude of the devastation emerged piecemeal over days.

从巴达维亚（今雅加达）发出的电报经海底电缆转发，几小时内就传到了伦敦。西方报纸很快便开始记述这起常被称为首个全球新闻事件的灾难。然而，早期的电讯都十分简短；浩劫的规模是在之后几天里才一点一点地显露出来的。

Ash and sulphur dioxide, hurled tens of kilometres high, formed a haze that circled the equator in about two weeks. In November 1883, crimson sunsets glowed over Europe and North America, summoning firefighters in New York and Connecticut to phantom blazes. Later climate reconstructions suggest global temperatures dipped a few tenths of a degree the following year.

火山灰和二氧化硫被抛射到数十公里的高空，形成一层薄雾，约两个星期便绕赤道一圈。1883年11月，深红色的晚霞在欧洲和北美上空灼灼发亮，招来纽约州和康涅狄格州的消防员赶去扑救子虚乌有的大火。据后来的气候重建研究推测，次年全球气温下降了零点几度。

Some experts discounted the volcanic explanation, blaming the sunsets on moisture at high altitude or cosmic dust. In February 1884, Britain's Royal Society appealed in The Times for dated observations, eliciting worldwide replies; the Society's 1888 report tied the glow to Krakatoa. Through barometers, cables and crimson skies, far-flung observers began to glimpse how interconnected the planet already was.

一些专家对火山之说不予采信，而把晚霞归因于高空的水汽或宇宙尘埃。1884年2月，英国皇家学会在《泰晤士报》上征集注明日期的观测记录，引来了世界各地的回信；学会1888年发表的报告指出，这片红霞源自喀拉喀托火山。借助气压计、电缆和深红色的天空，相隔遥远的观察者开始隐约看出，这颗星球早已何等紧密相连。

考研英语精读 · No.55

The Secret of Rome's Undying Concrete

罗马混凝土的千年之谜

Nearly 1,900 years after its completion, in about AD 125–128, the Pantheon's dome still crowns the vast chamber below. About 43 metres in diameter, it remains the world's largest unreinforced concrete dome. It is an astonishing feat of architecture, and a stubborn puzzle.

万神殿约于公元125至128年竣工，近1,900年后的今天，它的穹顶依然高踞于下方宏大的殿堂之上。穹顶直径约43米，至今仍是世界上最大的无钢筋混凝土穹顶。它是建筑史上令人惊叹的壮举，也是一道久解不开的谜题。

Modern concrete is usually reinforced with embedded steel bars, which are prone to corrosion. As the steel rusts, it swells and fractures the concrete around it, and many structures are designed for a lifespan of around 50 years. Roman concrete is not stronger than its modern counterpart; its merit lies in durability.

现代混凝土通常靠嵌入其中的钢筋加固，而钢筋容易发生锈蚀。钢筋生锈时会膨胀，把周围的混凝土撑裂；许多建筑的设计使用寿命约为50年。罗马混凝土并不比现代的同类材料更坚固，它的长处在于耐久性。

Yet the ancient material seemed to have a shortcoming. It is dotted with white chunks of lime, known as lime clasts. Experts long ascribed these lumps to sloppy mixing or inferior raw ingredients, a defect that, they assumed, a more conscientious builder would have avoided.

然而，这种古代材料似乎有个缺点。它的内部散布着白色的石灰小块，称为"石灰团块"。长期以来，专家把这些块状物归因于搅拌马虎或原料成分低劣。他们认为，换一个更认真负责的工匠，就不会出这种瑕疵。

A study published in Science Advances in January 2023 offered a different interpretation. The clasts, it suggested, were a by-product of "hot mixing" with quicklime, a reactive lime that heats up in water. When water seeps into a crack, the clasts dissolve and their calcium recrystallises as calcium carbonate, plugging the gap. In tests, water ceased to trickle through cracked hot-mixed samples within weeks, but kept flowing through samples without quicklime.

2023年1月发表于《科学进展》（Science Advances）的一项研究提出了不同的解释。该研究认为，这些团块是用生石灰"热混合"留下的副产品；生石灰是一种遇水会发热、活性强的石灰。水渗入裂缝时，团块会溶解，其中的钙重新结晶为碳酸钙，堵住缝隙。试验中，几周之内，水就不再从开裂的热混合样本中细细流过；而在不含生石灰的样本中，水却一直流个不停。

The implications extend beyond archaeology. Cement manufacture accounts for about 8 per cent of global carbon dioxide emissions, the think-tank Chatham House reported in June 2018. Concrete that mends its cracks could last longer, making demolition and rebuilding rarer and trimming emissions. The white lumps once scorned as signs of clumsy workmanship have prompted a rethink of construction.

这项发现的意义超出了考古学的范围。据智库查塔姆研究所（Chatham House）2018年6月的报告，水泥生产约占全球二氧化碳排放的8%。能自行修补裂缝的混凝土可能更经久耐用，拆除与重建因而减少，排放也随之削减。这些白色块状物曾被轻视为手艺拙劣的痕迹，如今却促使人们重新思考建造之道。

考研英语精读 · No.56

The Letter That Startled Darwin

一封信惊醒了达尔文

Charles Darwin disembarked from HMS Beagle in October 1836. In September 1838 he began formulating the principle of natural selection: individuals better adapted to their surroundings survive and pass on their hereditary traits. By 1844 he had composed a 230-page manuscript, yet kept it unpublished, cautiously amassing evidence.

1836年10月，查尔斯·达尔文从小猎犬号上登岸。1838年9月，他开始提出自然选择的原理：对环境适应得更好的个体得以存活，并把自身的遗传性状传递下去。到1844年，他已写成一份230页的手稿，却一直未予发表，谨慎地积累证据。

In February 1858, two decades after Darwin's first insight, Alfred Russel Wallace, a naturalist afflicted by fever in the Malay Archipelago, independently conceived the same idea. His concise essay reached Darwin on 18 June 1858. Its argument paralleled Darwin's own almost precisely; he told Charles Lyell he had never seen a more remarkable coincidence. His claim to priority seemed threatened.

1858年2月，距达尔文最初的设想已有二十年，博物学家阿尔弗雷德·拉塞尔·华莱士在马来群岛身患热病，独立地构想出了同一个想法。华莱士那篇简明的文章于1858年6月18日寄到达尔文手中。文中的论证与达尔文本人的论证几乎完全相合；达尔文告诉查尔斯·莱尔，自己从未见过比这更惊人的巧合。他对优先权的主张似乎受到了威胁。

Lyell and the botanist Joseph Hooker arranged for both papers to be read simultaneously at the Linnean Society, a scientific institution in London, on 1 July 1858. Neither author attended the proceedings. Wallace was abroad, and Darwin was grieving: scarlet fever had killed his infant son on 28 June. The compromise, struck without Wallace, may have kept rivalry over precedence at bay.

莱尔和植物学家约瑟夫·胡克促成两篇论文于1858年7月1日在伦敦的科学机构林奈学会同时宣读。两位作者都没有出席当天的会议。华莱士身在海外，达尔文则处于哀痛之中：6月28日，猩红热夺去了他幼子的生命。这一折中办法在华莱士未参与的情况下达成，或许避免了围绕先后次序的竞争。

The papers' publication on 20 August 1858 met a muted reception. In May 1859 the Society's president remarked in his yearly address that the year had been barren of revolutionary discoveries. The sensation came on 24 November 1859 with Darwin's treatise, On the Origin of Species: booksellers had subscribed for every copy of its first impression, 1,250 in all.

1858年8月20日，两篇论文刊出，得到的反响却很平淡。1859年5月，学会会长在年度致辞中谈到，这一年毫无革命性的发现。1859年11月24日，达尔文的专著《物种起源》问世，引起轰动：书商们已认购了该书第一次印刷的每一册，共1,250册。

Wallace had readily signified his acceptance in October 1858, assuring Hooker in a cordial letter of his satisfaction with the arrangement. In 1889 he published a lengthy exposition of the idea and entitled it Darwinism. Wallace never named the theory after himself.

1858年10月，华莱士就已欣然表明了接受的态度，在一封亲切的信中向胡克保证，自己对这一安排感到满意。1889年，他出版了一部长篇论述阐释这一思想，并将其定名为《达尔文主义》。华莱士从未用自己的名字为这一理论命名。

考研英语精读 · No.57

A Change of Anthem in the Deep

鲸歌易帜

Each winter, male humpback whales, and only males, fill their breeding grounds with a haunting melody. Sound units constitute phrases, and phrases build themes in an elaborate hierarchy. Each rendition lasts minutes, and the song can recur for hours, probably for courtship. All the males in a population sing an identical version, which evolves subtly each year.

每年冬天，雄性座头鲸——而且只有雄性——会让它们的繁殖地回荡着一段萦绕不散的旋律。若干声音单元构成乐句，乐句又组成主题，形成一个精巧的层级结构。每一遍演唱持续数分钟，整首歌则反复出现数小时，很可能是为了求偶。同一种群的所有雄鲸都唱完全相同的版本，而这个版本每年都在演变，只是变化细微。

That is the rule. Off eastern Australia, it was broken. From 1995 to 1998, researchers used hydrophones, or underwater microphones, to record humpbacks on their seasonal migration along the coastline. In the mid-1990s, merely two of 82 singers emitted a distinctive new song, utterly unlike the reigning chorus.

这是常规。可在澳大利亚东部海域，常规被打破。1995年至1998年间，研究人员用水听器，即水下麦克风，录下了沿海岸线进行季节性迁徙的座头鲸。20世纪90年代中期，82只歌者中仅仅有两只唱出了一首与众不同的新歌，与当时盛行的合唱截然不同。

Within about two years, virtually every singer had converted, and by 1998 the old song had been displaced wholesale. A study published in Nature in November 2000 found that the new song resembled that of the Indian Ocean population off western Australia, apparently ferried east by a handful of visiting performers.

大约两年之内，几乎每只歌者都已改唱新歌；到1998年，旧歌已被全盘取代。2000年11月发表于《自然》（Nature）的一项研究发现，这首新歌与澳大利亚西部海域印度洋种群的歌相似，似乎是由一小批来访的歌手向东带来的。

Nor was it a freak. In April 2011, a study in Current Biology analysed over a decade of recordings from six South Pacific populations. Song types primarily arose off eastern Australia, then rippled eastward through successive populations to French Polynesia, generally crossing the region within about two years.

这也并非偶然的怪事。2011年4月，《当代生物学》（Current Biology）上的一项研究分析了南太平洋六个种群十余年的录音。歌型主要发端于澳大利亚东部海域，随后经由一个个相继的种群如涟漪般扩散，向东传至法属波利尼西亚，通常在大约两年内横贯整个海区。

Such rapid change, too fast for genetic evolution, points to imitation rather than inheritance, a persuasive case of cultural transmission. Why males sing, and why they overhaul their songs, remain controversial. One idea is that novelty itself spurs change. Some scholars are reluctant to define this as culture in the human sense. Whatever the label, a few outsiders off eastern Australia reshaped an entire population's music.

如此迅速的变化，快得远非基因演化所能及，指向的是模仿而非遗传，是文化传递的一个有说服力的例证。雄鲸为何歌唱、又为何彻底改换歌曲，至今仍有争议。一种观点认为，是新奇本身推动了变化。一些学者不愿把这种现象界定为人类意义上的文化。无论如何称呼，一小批外来者曾在澳大利亚东部海域重塑了整个种群的音乐。

考研英语精读 · No.58

From a Scratch to a World Without Smallpox

从一道划痕到天花绝迹

Smallpox, a contagious affliction, killed about three in ten of its sufferers and an estimated 300 million in the 20th century. An early safeguard, deliberately implanting matter from its sores in the healthy, was practised in China by the 16th century and reached England in 1721. The procedure itself could cause grave sickness or death, if less frequently than natural smallpox.

天花是一种传染性病痛，约三成患者因此丧命；仅在20世纪，估计就有3亿人死于天花。一种早期的防护手段，是把天花脓疮中的物质有意植入健康人体内。到16世纪，中国已在施行这种方法，1721年这种方法又传入英国。这种做法本身也可能引起严重疾病乃至死亡，虽然不像自然感染的天花那样频繁。

Rustic lore held that milkmaids with cowpox, a mild ailment of livestock, were immune to smallpox. In 1774 Benjamin Jesty, a Dorset farmer, employed cowpox to protect his wife and two sons. On 14 May 1796 Edward Jenner, a provincial practitioner, inserted fluid from a milkmaid's cowpox sore into a scratch on eight-year-old James Phipps.

乡间传说认为，挤奶女工若得过牛痘这种牲畜的轻微病症，就对天花免疫。1774年，多塞特郡农夫本杰明·杰斯蒂使用牛痘保护妻子和两个儿子。1796年5月14日，地方医生爱德华·詹纳从一名挤奶女工的牛痘疮中取出液体，注入8岁的詹姆斯·菲普斯身上的一道划痕。

On 1 July 1796 Jenner exposed the boy to smallpox; no symptoms ensued. In 1797 the Royal Society declined to publish his paper, judging his evidence unconvincing. In June 1798 he privately funded his book, the Inquiry, with supplementary cases. The word vaccine derives from the Latin vacca, cow.

1796年7月1日，詹纳让这个男孩接触天花，任何症状都没有随之出现。1797年，皇家学会不予发表他的论文，认为他的证据缺乏说服力。1798年6月，詹纳私人出资出版了他的著作《探究》，书中附上了补充病例。表示疫苗的英文词源自拉丁语的 vacca，意思是“牛”。

Jenner was not the idea's originator. His contribution was a test, with results accessible in print, so other doctors could replicate and disseminate the technique. By today's standards, experimentation on a child without informed consent would be unethical. Jenner died on 26 January 1823, with smallpox far from eliminated.

詹纳并不是这一想法的首创者。他的贡献在于做了一项试验，并把结果付印，供人查阅，其他医生因而得以重复并推广这项技术。按今天的标准，拿孩子做实验而不取得知情同意，属于不道德行为。詹纳于1823年1月26日去世，那时天花还远未被消除。

In 1967 the WHO launched an intensified, concerted campaign to eradicate smallpox. The last naturally occurring case was diagnosed in Somalia on 26 October 1977. On 8 May 1980 the World Health Assembly formally declared the world free of smallpox, the only human disease ever wiped out. That pronouncement came almost 184 years after Phipps was vaccinated.

1967年，世界卫生组织发起了一场强化、协同的运动，要根除天花。最后一例自然发生的病例于1977年10月26日在索马里确诊。1980年5月8日，世界卫生大会正式宣布全世界已摆脱天花，天花也成为迄今唯一被消灭的人类疾病。这一宣告距菲普斯接种已近184年。

考研英语精读 · No.59

Saving the Sky

拯救天空

A layer of ozone shields Earth from much of the sun's ultraviolet radiation. In June 1974 Mario Molina and Sherwood Rowland, chemists in California, warned in Nature of a menace in fridges and spray cans. Chlorofluorocarbons (CFCs) are so inert that they drift intact into the stratosphere, high in the atmosphere, where they can liberate chlorine, a catalyst that destroys ozone molecule after molecule. The pair foresaw gradual erosion.

一层臭氧为地球遮挡了太阳的大部分紫外辐射。1974年6月，加州的化学家马里奥·莫利纳和舍伍德·罗兰在《自然》杂志上撰文，警告冰箱和喷雾罐里藏着一种威胁。氯氟烃（CFCs）惰性极强，能完好无损地飘升到高层大气中的平流层，在那里还可能释放出氯，而氯是一种催化剂，会把臭氧分子一个接一个地破坏掉。两人预料到的，是一场缓慢的侵蚀。

The alarm, when it sounded, was abrupt. In May 1985 Joseph Farman and colleagues at the British Antarctic Survey reported that ozone over Halley Bay, their Antarctic station, had plummeted each southern spring since the mid-1970s. Their humble Dobson spectrophotometer gauged ozone from the ground. The staff were persistent, reading it in a painstaking routine of manual observation.

警报真的拉响时，却来得十分突然。1985年5月，英国南极调查局的约瑟夫·法曼和同事报告称，他们的南极考察站哈雷湾上空的臭氧，自20世纪70年代中期起，每到南半球春季就骤降。他们那台不起眼的多布森分光光度计，从地面测量臭氧。工作人员持之以恒，按例行规程读数，靠人工观测，做得一丝不苟。

Yet NASA's Nimbus-7 satellite, a sophisticated eye in orbit, had recorded the same lows. Its software flagged them as dubious, since ozone that low had never been reliably measured. NASA says it was still verifying them when Farman published. The sensor had worked; expectation had not.

然而，美国国家航空航天局（NASA）的雨云7号卫星——一只在轨道上运行的精密“眼睛”——也记录到了同样的低值。它的软件把这么低的读数标记为可疑，因为此前从没有人可靠地测到过这么低的数值。据NASA的说法，法曼发表论文时，它还在核实这些读数。传感器并没有出问题；出问题的是预期。

Remarkably, the response was swift. In September 1987 diplomats signed the Montreal Protocol, a binding accord to curb CFCs. Amendments tightened it: in June 1990 affluent countries pledged to phase out CFCs by 2000. In September 2009 the Protocol and its parent Vienna Convention became the UN's first treaties to win universal ratification.

令人称奇的是，各国的反应十分迅速。1987年9月，外交官们签署了《蒙特利尔议定书》，这是一项限制CFCs、具有约束力的协定。此后的修正案又收紧了管控：1990年6月，富裕国家承诺在2000年前逐步淘汰CFCs。2009年9月，该议定书与其所依据的《维也纳公约》一道，成为联合国首批获得普遍批准的条约。

In January 2023 a UN-backed assessment forecast recovery to 1980 levels around 2066 over Antarctica, 2045 over the Arctic and 2040 elsewhere. Healing will take decades. The sky's rescue began with diligent observers who trusted that a baffling number was no glitch, and published it.

2023年1月，一份由联合国支持的评估报告预测，臭氧层将恢复到1980年的水平，南极上空约在2066年，北极上空约在2045年，其他地区约在2040年。臭氧层的愈合还需要数十年。天空得以拯救，始于一群勤勉的观测者——他们相信，一个令人费解的数字并不是小故障，于是把它发表了出来。

考研英语精读 · No.60

The Scientific Giant Who Changed the World

改变世界的科学巨匠

On 29 May 1919, as a total eclipse obscured the sun, two British expeditions photographed stars in its apparent vicinity. Arthur Eddington observed from Príncipe, an island off West Africa; Crommelin and Davidson from Sobral, in north-eastern Brazil. They were testing general relativity's bold prediction that the sun's gravity would deflect starlight.

1919年5月29日，一次全日食遮蔽了太阳，两支英国考察队拍摄了天空中看似位于太阳附近的恒星。阿瑟·爱丁顿在西非外海的普林西比岛观测；克罗姆林和戴维森则在巴西东北部的索布拉尔。他们要检验广义相对论的一个大胆预言，即太阳的引力会使经过它附近的星光偏折。

On 6 November, at a joint meeting of the Royal Society and the Royal Astronomical Society, the findings were hailed as confirming it. The Times proclaimed a "Revolution in Science"; a New York Times headline read "Lights All Askew in the Heavens". Almost overnight, Einstein, eminent among physicists, became a global celebrity.

11月6日，英国皇家学会与皇家天文学会召开联席会议，会上盛赞观测结果证实了这一预言。《泰晤士报》宣告了一场“科学革命”；《纽约时报》的标题写着“天上的光全歪了”。几乎一夜之间，在物理学家中声望卓著的爱因斯坦成了举世皆知的名人。

Stockholm proved harder to dazzle. The Nobel physics committee valued rigorous experimental confirmation, and committee member Allvar Gullstrand, a Nobel-winning eye specialist, deemed the empirical support for relativity insufficient. Since no nomination fulfilled Nobel's criteria, the 1921 prize was reserved for a year, as the statutes permit.

斯德哥尔摩却没那么容易被眩惑。诺贝尔物理学委员会看重的是严格的实验证实。委员阿尔瓦·古尔斯特兰德是眼科专家，本人也是诺贝尔奖得主，他认定相对论的实证支持不足。由于没有一项提名符合诺贝尔定下的标准，1921年的奖便依照章程允许的办法保留一年。

On 9 November 1922 the Royal Swedish Academy of Sciences conferred it on Einstein, citing his services to theoretical physics, especially his discovery of the law of the photoelectric effect. That law, which governs how light ejects electrons from metal, helped underpin quantum theory. A letter the next day clarified that the prize took no account of relativity. Einstein, then aboard a steamer bound for Japan, would miss the December ceremony.

1922年11月9日，瑞典皇家科学院把这个奖授予爱因斯坦，表彰他对理论物理学的贡献，尤其是他发现了光电效应定律。这条定律决定了光如何把电子从金属中打出，曾帮助奠定量子理论的基础。第二天的一封信说明，此奖并未考虑相对论。爱因斯坦当时正乘一艘开往日本的轮船，将错过12月的颁奖典礼。

On 11 July 1923, in Gothenburg, Sweden, he delivered his Nobel lecture before a congress of Nordic scientists, devoting it to the fundamental ideas and problems of relativity. The theory the committee looked past reached a Nobel podium after all, carried there by the man the prize had honoured.

1923年7月11日，他在瑞典哥德堡向北欧自然科学家大会发表诺贝尔演讲，专门讲相对论的基本思想与问题。委员会绕开不看的那个理论，终究登上了诺贝尔的讲台，把它带上去的，正是这个奖所嘉奖的人。

考研英语精读 · No.61

The Steel Box That Remade the World

集装箱：重塑世界的铁盒子

On 26 April 1956 a converted Second World War tanker, the Ideal X, slipped out of Port Newark, New Jersey, for Houston. Clamped to her deck were 58 containers, trailer bodies detached from their wheels and chassis. Few spectators would have taken so unremarkable a cargo for a landmark.

1956年4月26日，一艘由二战油轮改装而成的“理想X号”（Ideal X）悄然驶离新泽西州纽瓦克港，开往休斯敦。甲板上牢牢固定着58个集装箱，其实就是拆掉车轮和底盘、卸下来的挂车车厢。当时的旁观者恐怕很少会把这样一批平淡无奇的货物当作里程碑。

At the time, freight travelled loose. Gangs of twenty or more dockworkers carried sacks, barrels and crates piece by piece. It was strenuous drudgery. Ships could spend up to half their time berthed, and theft was rampant. In 1956 loading loose cargo cost about $5.83 a ton; loading the Ideal X cost, by the company's reckoning, about 16 cents.

那时，货品都以散装方式运输。二十多人一帮的码头工人把麻袋、木桶和板条箱一件件扛上扛下。这是繁重的苦差事。船舶有时多达一半时间停泊在港，偷窃猖獗。1956年，装运散货每吨约需5.83美元；而据公司估算，给理想X号装货每吨只要约16美分。

Containers were not new. The hurdle was consensus: rival firms' boxes had incompatible dimensions and latches. An international standards committee formed in 1961. In January 1963 Sea-Land, the Ideal X's renamed operator, let the committee use its patents free of royalties, including those on corner fittings for hoisting. Lifted by crane, a box could be transferred smoothly between ship, lorry and train.

集装箱并不新鲜。真正的障碍在于共识：相互竞争的各家公司，箱子尺寸和锁扣互不兼容。1961年，一个国际标准委员会成立。1963年1月，理想X号的运营方、已更名的海陆公司（Sea-Land）允许该委员会使用其专利，不收专利费，其中包括用于吊装的角配件。由吊机吊起，一只箱子便能在船舶、卡车和火车之间顺畅地转运。

This efficiency came at the expense of livelihoods. New York's waterfront employment shrank by about three quarters between 1963 and 1976. Much of the cargo crossed the harbour to a new terminal at Elizabeth, inaugurated in August 1962. Most of London's historic docks closed one after another from 1967 to 1980, while Felixstowe, on the Suffolk coast, expanded.

这种效率是以生计为代价换来的。1963年至1976年间，纽约滨水区的就业缩减了约四分之三。大量货物越过港湾，转往伊丽莎白港1962年8月启用的新货运站。1967年到1980年，伦敦大部分历史悠久的码头接连关闭，而萨福克海岸的费利克斯托港则不断扩张。

The rewards were substantial. A study published in the Journal of International Economics in 2016 estimated that containerisation had lifted trade between industrialised countries by a cumulative 700 per cent over 20 years. Tireless cranes now lift identical containers into orderly, immense stacks.

回报相当可观。2016年发表于《国际经济学杂志》（Journal of International Economics）的一项研究估计，20年间，集装箱化使工业化国家之间的贸易累计增长了700%。如今，不知疲倦的吊机把一模一样的集装箱吊起，码成整齐的、巨大的箱垛。

考研英语精读 · No.62

Dolly, the Copied Sheep

多莉：一只羊的复制

Dolly was born at Roslin, Scotland, on 5 July 1996. Her genome came from a Finn Dorset ewe's mammary cell, fused with a Blackface egg stripped of its nucleus. A Blackface surrogate carried the embryo through pregnancy, yet her white face was proof of a genetically different lineage. She alone survived from 277 attempts, an extraordinary failure rate.

1996年7月5日，多莉在苏格兰罗斯林出生。她的基因组来自一只芬兰多塞特母羊的乳腺细胞，这个细胞与一枚去除了细胞核的黑脸羊卵细胞融合。一只黑脸羊代孕母羊怀着这个胚胎度过了孕期，然而多莉的白脸证明她来自遗传上不同的血统。在277次尝试中只有她存活下来，失败率惊人。

Dolly is popularly called the first clone, a misconception. Her predecessors included frogs cloned from transplanted nuclei in 1962 and Roslin's embryo-derived duo, Megan and Morag, in 1995. She was genuinely the first mammal cloned from an adult cell. Roslin's announcement on 22 February 1997 coincided with the paper, and her name, a mammary pun, honoured Dolly Parton.

多莉被普遍称为第一个克隆体，这是一种误解。她的前辈包括1962年用移植细胞核克隆的青蛙，以及罗斯林1995年用胚胎细胞培育的一对绵羊梅根和莫拉格。她确实是第一个用成年细胞克隆的哺乳动物。1997年2月22日，罗斯林研究所的公布与论文发表同时进行；她的名字是一个关于乳腺的双关语，向多莉·帕顿致敬。

The news provoked uproar and apprehension about the ethics of human cloning. In March 1997 Clinton prohibited federal funding for such experiments. In 1999 her telomeres, or chromosome tips, were reported shortened by about 20% for her age. That fed anxiety; some speculated she was ageing prematurely.

这一消息引发了轩然大波，也让人们对人类克隆的伦理问题深感忧虑。1997年3月，克林顿禁止联邦资金用于这类实验。1999年，有报道称她的端粒，即染色体末端，比同龄羊缩短了约20%。这加剧了人们的焦虑，有人推测她正在过早衰老。

Dolly was put down, aged six, on 14 February 2003, with arthritis and a progressive lung disease caused by a sheep virus. Indoor confinement probably contributed. In July 2016, 13 clones, four from her cell line, were ageing normally, without hypertension or diabetes. In November 2017, her skeletal X-rays showed ordinary arthritis, and fears of early onset were judged unfounded.

2003年2月14日，多莉被实施安乐死，时年六岁；她患有关节炎和一种由绵羊病毒引起的进行性肺部疾病。圈养在室内很可能是原因之一。2016年7月，13只克隆羊正在正常衰老，其中4只来自与她相同的细胞系；它们没有高血压，也没有糖尿病。2017年11月，她的骨骼X光片显示出普通的关节炎，关节炎提早发病的担忧被判定为毫无根据。

She bore six lambs by natural mating; Bonnie, first of these descendants, arrived in April 1998. Since 2003 her stuffed body, now famously an icon and memorial, has been encased in the National Museum of Scotland. Behind glass, her white face plainly testifies to her Finn Dorset ancestry.

她通过自然交配产下六只小羊；这些后代中的第一只邦妮生于1998年4月。自2003年起，她的填充标本一直封存在苏格兰国家博物馆，如今已成为出了名的标志和纪念物。隔着玻璃，她的白脸清楚地见证着她的芬兰多塞特祖源。

考研英语精读 · No.63

The Crowded Heavens

拥挤的苍穹

On 10 February 2009, nearly 790 kilometres above Siberia, two satellites slammed together at about 42,000 kilometres an hour. Iridium 33 belonged to an American satellite-phone constellation; Kosmos 2251, a Russian military satellite, had been derelict since about 1995. Both shattered in the first accidental collision between two intact satellites.

2009年2月10日，在西伯利亚上空近790公里处，两颗卫星以约每小时42,000公里的速度猛撞在一起。铱星33号隶属于美国的一个卫星电话星座；宇宙2251号是一颗俄罗斯军用卫星，约自1995年起便已废弃。在这起两颗完整卫星之间的首次意外相撞中，两星双双粉碎。

Neither craft swerved. The Russian hulk, lacking propulsion, could not. A public collision-warning service had forecast a narrow miss of about 584 metres, yet the predicted encounter was not among the week's closest; such warnings were frequent and imprecise. One owner's discarded relic demolished another's working asset.

两颗卫星都没有转向避让。那具俄罗斯空壳没有推进装置，想躲也躲不了。一项公开的碰撞预警服务曾预报，两星将相距约584米惊险擦过，但在当周的预测中，这次交会并未排进距离最近的几次之列；这类预警既频繁又不精确。一方弃置的遗物，撞毁了另一方仍在工作的资产。

The wreckage, however, endangered everyone else. The impact flung out more than 2,000 catalogued fragments and a multitude of others too small to pinpoint. At that height air drag is feeble, so many would linger for decades. In January 2012, the International Space Station fired its thrusters to dodge an Iridium 33 remnant.

然而，这些残骸却危及了其他所有人。撞击抛出了2,000多块已编目的碎片，另有大量碎片小到无法精确定位。在那个高度，空气阻力微弱，因此许多碎片会在轨道上滞留数十年。2012年1月，国际空间站启动推进器，躲开了一块铱星33号的残片。

In June 1978, two NASA scientists envisaged a vicious spiral, later named the Kessler syndrome: each collision would scatter fragments, raising the odds of subsequent crashes. The 2009 crash illustrated that premise without triggering a runaway chain reaction. By the end of 2024, surveillance networks tracked about 40,000 objects, only some 11,000 of them functional.

1978年6月，两位美国国家航空航天局（NASA）的科学家设想了一种恶性螺旋，后来被称为凯斯勒综合征，即每次相撞都会散布碎片，使后续撞击更有可能发生。2009年的这次相撞为这一前提提供了例证，却并未引发失控的连锁反应。截至2024年底，太空监视网络跟踪的物体约有40,000个，其中能正常运行的只有约11,000个。

Such a cascade would unfold over decades, though, and is not inevitable; the risk can be mitigated. Operators now routinely manoeuvre satellites out of harm's way. In September 2022, America's Federal Communications Commission, which licenses satellites, mandated disposal of low-orbit craft within five years of retirement instead of 25. Years after the crash, spacecraft whose owners had no part in it kept swerving around its splinters.

不过，这样的连锁效应会在几十年的时间里逐步展开，而且并非不可避免，风险也可以加以缓解。如今，运营方会例行操纵卫星避开危险。2022年9月，负责为卫星发放许可的美国联邦通信委员会强制要求低轨航天器在退役后五年内完成处置，而非原先的25年。相撞多年之后，那些主人与此事毫无瓜葛的航天器，仍不得不一再转向，绕开那次相撞留下的碎屑。

考研英语精读 · No.64

The Day Zero That Never Came

没有到来的零日

In January 2018, Cape Town issued a stark alert: Day Zero could arrive in April. The term denoted a grim threshold, reached when water in the city's reservoirs, as a percentage of capacity, fell to 13.5 per cent. Most taps would then be shut, and residents would queue at about 200 designated collection points.

2018年1月，开普敦发出了一则严峻的警报，称零日可能在4月到来。这个词指的是一道严酷的门槛：该市水库存水占库容的百分比一旦降到13.5%，便跨过了这道门槛。届时大部分水龙头将被关闭，居民要到约200个指定的取水点排队取水。

The prospect was genuine. Three consecutive years of meagre rainfall, from 2015 to 2017, had diminished the reservoirs serving a metropolitan area of about four million people. By early 2018 the dams were roughly a fifth full. The countdown turned a chronic shortage into an imminent deadline.

这一前景是真实存在的。2015至2017年连续三年降雨稀少，使为约400万人口的大都市区供水的各座水库蓄水日渐减少。到2018年初，水库只剩约五分之一的蓄水。倒计时把一场长期的短缺变成了一个迫在眉睫的最后期限。

The city paired mandatory restrictions with unprecedented transparency. From 1 February 2018, each person was allotted 50 litres a day. Officials disclosed dam levels and household consumption; the published tallies made thrift visible and let neighbours benchmark their use. Daily use was more than halved, from about 1.2 billion litres in 2015 to about 500 million by early 2018.

该市把强制性的限制措施与前所未有的透明度结合起来。自2018年2月1日起，每人每天分配50升水。官员们公布水库水位和家庭用水量，公开的统计数字让节俭成效一目了然，也让邻里之间得以对照用水量。日用水量减半有余，从2015年的约12亿升降至2018年初的约5亿升。

Other forces intervened. In February 2018, upstream farmers released water from their irrigation dams; with farms drawing less, Day Zero was postponed to July. In March 2018, officials judged Day Zero unlikely that year. Winter rains subsequently replenished the reservoirs, which were about half full by July. The 50-litre limit was eased in October 2018.

其他力量也介入了。2018年2月，上游的农民从他们的灌溉水坝中放出了水；加上农业用水减少，零日被推迟到7月。2018年3月，官员们判断零日当年不太可能到来。随后，冬雨补充了各水库，到7月时水库已约半满。50升的限额于2018年10月放宽。

The burden, critics argued, fell unevenly. Townships, poor areas segregated by race under apartheid, had long made do with scant water, yet steeper tariffs squeezed them hardest. Others blamed alarmist warnings for tourist cancellations. Households, farmers and rain kept the collection points idle, but the sacrifice weighed heaviest on those with the least to give.

批评者认为，负担分配得并不均衡。城镇区——在种族隔离制度下按种族被隔离出来的贫困地区——长期以来就靠很少的水勉强度日，然而更高的水价让它们最不堪重负。另一些人则把游客取消行程归咎于危言耸听的警告。家庭、农民和雨水共同让取水点一直闲置，但这份牺牲最沉重地压在了最拿不出余力的人身上。

考研英语精读 · No.65

The Day the Doomsday Vault Opened

末日种子库的第一次取种

On Spitsbergen, in Norway's Svalbard archipelago, a corridor has been drilled deep into a frosted hillside. At its end, cartons of precious seed sit silently in a sub-zero repository, insulated by permanently frozen ground.

在挪威斯瓦尔巴群岛的斯匹次卑尔根岛上，一条通道已经深深地钻进一面覆着白霜的山坡。通道尽头，一个个纸箱里装着珍贵的种子，它们静静地躺在一座零度以下的储藏库里，四周永久冻结的土层为它们隔热保冷。

The Svalbard Global Seed Vault opened near Longyearbyen in February 2008 to insure crops such as beans, rice and wheat against the horrors people dread, from atomic war to disease. It has ample room for about four and a half million samples. Essentially the planet's ultimate backup, it is run like a bank's safe-deposit box. Depositors keep ownership, and only they can take their seeds out.

斯瓦尔巴全球种子库于2008年2月在朗伊尔城附近启用，为豆类、水稻、小麦等作物投保，防范人们所畏惧的种种灾难，从原子战争到疾病。它有充足的空间，可容纳约450万份样本。它本质上是这颗星球终极的备份，运作起来就像银行的保险箱。存放者仍保有所有权，也只有他们能取出自己的种子。

The first retrieval came not after an asteroid strike but amid strife on the ground. ICARDA, a research centre whose gene bank stood near Aleppo, had moved its headquarters to Beirut during Syria's civil war. In September 2015 it asked for about 116,000 samples, 130 of the 325 boxes it had sent to the Arctic before the hostilities. They included robust strains of wheat and barley tolerant of drought and heat.

第一次取种不是发生在小行星撞击之后，而是发生在地面上的纷争之中。国际干旱地区农业研究中心的基因库原在阿勒颇附近，在叙利亚内战期间，它已把总部迁到了贝鲁特。2015年9月，它申请取回约11.6万份样本，也就是战事爆发前它送往北极的325箱中的130箱。其中有能耐受干旱和高温的强健小麦和大麦品种。

In Lebanon and Morocco, ICARDA sowed the seeds, and vigorous plants sprouted to regenerate its collection. Once multiplication had gone far enough, a homecoming began in 2017, consignment by consignment, restocking the Arctic stronghold.

研究中心在黎巴嫩和摩洛哥播下这些种子，种子萌发，长成茁壮的植株，让收藏得以再生。繁育达到足够规模后，2017年开始了回归之旅，种子一批一批地运回，重新充实这座北极堡垒。

That same year brought a scare. After record-breaking warmth, meltwater ran into the roughly 100-metre entrance tunnel and solidified. The intrusion stopped short of the seeds, which stayed safely at minus 18 degrees Celsius. Norway waterproofed the tunnel and dug drainage ditches.

同一年还有过一场虚惊。在一段破纪录的温暖天气之后，融水流进大约100米长的入口隧道，随后凝固成冰。这次入侵没有到达种子，它们安然保持在零下18摄氏度。挪威给隧道做了防水，并挖了排水用的沟渠。

A haven built against the apocalypse had a weakness at its own entrance, and warmer weather found it.

一座为抵御末日而建的避风港，在自家入口处却有一个弱点，而更暖的天气找到了它。

考研英语精读 · No.66

The Burrs That Became Velcro

一颗刺果钩出的发明

In 1941 George de Mestral, a Swiss engineer, returned from hunting in the Alps with his hound, an Irish Pointer. Prickly burdock seed-heads had hitched themselves to his clothes and lay tangled in the dog's fur. Under a microscope he saw thousands of tiny barbs, each a hook grasping the small loops in cloth.

1941年，瑞士工程师乔治·德·梅斯特拉尔带着他的猎犬，一只爱尔兰指示犬，在阿尔卑斯山打猎归来。多刺的牛蒡种子头紧紧挂住了他的衣服，也缠结在狗的毛皮里。在显微镜下，他看到成千上万个细小的倒刺，每一根都是一个钩子，钩住布料上的小毛圈。

Few took his idea seriously at first. In Lyon, then a weaving centre, a cotton version proved too frail to survive abrasion. Working with a textiles firm, he turned to nylon, whose hooks were stiff enough to hold yet easy to pull apart. The trial-and-error at the looms and mills lasted about ten years.

起初，很少有人认真看待他的想法。在当时的纺织中心里昂，一个棉制版本太脆弱，经不起磨损。他与一家纺织品公司合作，转而采用尼龙。尼龙做成的钩子足够硬挺，既能扣住，又容易拉开。在织机旁和工厂里的反复试验持续了约十年。

The name Velcro merged two French words, velours, meaning velvet, and crochet, meaning hook. A US patent duly followed in 1955. In 1959 a New York fashion show sent apparel stitched with the fasteners down the runway. The public was less charmed than expected, mostly because of the aesthetic.

Velcro 这个名字合并了两个法语词，velours 意为天鹅绒，crochet 意为钩子。1955年，美国专利顺理成章地到手。1959年，纽约一场时装秀让缝有这种搭扣的服装走上T台。公众却不如预期那样着迷，主要是因为它的外观。

The underdog proved its utility in outer space. NASA used it in the Apollo programme to keep gear anchored in weightlessness, so that nothing would hover in mid-air. Apollo 11 was equipped with a sizeable amount, about 3,300 square inches. NASA did not invent the nylon clasp.

这个不被看好的弱者在外层空间证明了自己的用处。美国航天局在阿波罗计划中用它把装备牢牢固定住，免得东西在失重状态下飘浮在半空中。阿波罗11号配备了相当多的用量，约3,300平方英寸。美国航天局并没有发明这种尼龙扣件。

When the patent ran out in 1978, cut-price knock-offs from Taiwan, China and South Korea overran the market, and a niche product went mainstream. In 2017, to stop its brand-name melting into a generic noun, the company asked people to call such mimics hook and loop fasteners. In effect, the firm was asking the public to stop using its own name for rivals' goods.

1978年专利到期后，来自台湾、中国大陆和韩国的廉价的仿冒品大量涌入市场，一种小众产品成了主流。2017年，为了不让自己的品牌名逐渐消融为一个通用的名词，公司请大家把这类仿品称作"钩毛搭扣"。实际上，这家公司是在请公众别再用它自己的名字去称呼对手的商品。
